# Living Atlas changelog

One line per publish: `date · id · new|enriched|corrected · confidence · least-certain field`

2026-10-21 · -66my-chicxulub-impact · new · established · Least sure of the extinction percentage (only the '>60%' lower bound verified) and of the impactor size, which depends on assumed speed and angle.
2026-10-21 · -4540my-formation-of-earth · new · established · Least sure of the start and end years: 4.54 Ga may date Earth's source material rather than accretion (Dalrymple 2001), and the Moon-impact end date spans 40-150 Myr across models; the 4.403/4.374 Ga zircon re-dating literature was not checked.
2026-10-21 · -252my-permian-triassic-extinction · new · established · Least sure of the mechanism attribution (sill-driven CO2/CH4 as trigger is the leading but not proven hypothesis) and of the terrestrial-timing debate; the Siberian Traps region pin is the town of Norilsk as a proxy point for a province of up to ~5 million km².
2026-10-21 · -3700my-origin-of-life · new · contested · Least sure of the window end (3.48 vs 3.43 Ga as 'widely accepted') and of the proxy coordinates for the Dresser and Jack Hills sites, which are gazetteer centroids rather than published outcrop positions.
2026-10-21 · -200my-breakup-of-pangaea · new · established · Least sure of the onset age of Central Atlantic seafloor spreading (Middle Jurassic vs c. 195–190 Ma), of CAMP's total area (only the 1999 figure was verified), and of the pin's paleo-latitude, which spans about 17 degrees between plate models.
2026-10-21 · -538my-cambrian-explosion · new · established · Least sure of the paleo-positions (PALEOMAP and MERDITH2021 differ by ~56° latitude for South China, and MERDITH2021 gave no result for the Burgess Shale) and of the end date, which depends on how authors bound the radiation; several peer-reviewed sources were read at abstract level only, and the Burgess Shale point is a ridge midpoint, not a surveyed quarry coordinate.
2026-10-21 · -300ka-rise-of-homo-sapiens · new · probable · Least sure of the end date and framing: 233 ka for Omo I is a minimum age chosen to bound the process, and the Florisbad date and association, and the very definition of where H. sapiens begins, are actively disputed; Omo KHS coordinates are low precision.
2026-10-21 · -40ka-extinction-of-neanderthals · new · established · Least sure of the final end date (Higham's 41,030–39,260 cal BP versus disputed southern Iberian persistence to c. 37 ka) and of the start bound, which is approximate; the main pin at Gorham's Cave is a reference site for a range-wide process, and Higham 2014 was read only as its abstract and press release.
2026-10-21 · place-uruk · new · established · Least sure of the size figures (2.5 km² Late Uruk vs 5–6 km² walled city, 9.8 vs 11 km of wall) and of the end date, which is the ICOMOS 'late Sasanian' period, not an attested abandonment; the footprint is an OSM trace, not a DAI survey plan.
2026-10-21 · -70ka-out-of-africa · new · established · Least sure of the start date (genetic clocks depend on mutation-rate and generation-time assumptions, giving ~72 to ~50 ka) and of the contested Madjedbebe (~65 ka) and Lida Ajer (73–63 ka) dates; several site pins are approximate (Payakumbuh for Lida Ajer, Jabiluka Mine for Madjedbebe, Mount Carmel centroid for Misliya/Skhul).
2026-10-21 · 0476-fall-of-the-western-roman-empire · new · established · Least sure of exact day-dates taken from modern reconstructions (24 August 410 rests only on a World History Encyclopedia caption and is not used; the 455 sack's start is 24 May in Mathisen versus early June elsewhere), and no sourced dated border polygons are attached, so the region geometry is a set of sourced sites rather than extents.
2026-10-21 · place-giza-pyramids · new · established · Least sure of absolute dates (the Ministry's Shaw-based dates, Hornung's lower chronology and the radiocarbon model differ by up to ~80 years) and of the casing-stripping chronology, which rests on Petrie's hedged reading of medieval reports; present-day height omitted for lack of an opened source.
2026-10-21 · -0508-rise-of-rome · new · established · Least sure of the dated extents: only the c. 60 BCE polygon (AWMC) is sourced, so 509 and 264 BCE are textual and the 27 BCE extent is unmapped; ancient captive and casualty figures are claims, and 509 BCE is a conventional date.
2026-10-21 · polity-han-dynasty · new · established · Least sure of the dated border polygons: CHGIS has gaps before 1350 CE and I could not open the Cambridge maps themselves, so extents beyond capitals and Hexi Corridor anchors are unverified; the 57.7 million census variant is unexplained.
2026-10-21 · -23ka-peopling-of-the-americas · new · contested · Least sure of the start date (White Sands LGM ages versus critics and the post-16 ka consensus) and of Monte Verde after the 2026 challenge; Pigati 2023 and Surovell 2026 were read only as abstracts/records, not full text; the Anzick pin is a Park County proxy.
2026-10-21 · 0250-classic-maya-period · new · established · Least sure of the period boundaries (250 vs 300 start; 900 vs 1000 end are conventions), the conquest reading of the 378 entrada, and the 562 victor (Martin 2005 favours the Kaan ruler Sky Witness but calls it unproven); Kennett 2012 was read only as its abstract record and press release.
2026-10-21 · 1501-transatlantic-slave-trade · new · established · Least sure: the disembarked total (10.5 vs 10.7 million, hence the derived death range) and that I opened no source for Brazil's own disembarkation share or for national abolition-act dates.
2026-10-21 · place-cusco · new · established · Least sure of the Inca-period chronology and of the city's Inca population. No scholarly population range was found, so none is given. Coricancha coordinates and measurements were not verified, so it has no pin. The 1536 siege details rest on encyclopedias, not chronicles.
2026-10-21 · place-troy · new · probable · Least sure of the absolute dates of individual layers (Troy VI's start c. 1700 BCE and the post-Bronze Age phases rest partly on Livius) and of the Troy VI–VIIa lower-city size, which is the core of an unresolved scholarly dispute; the end of habitation is unsourced beyond 'late Byzantine'. Coordinator set confidence to probable per spec 4 (the site is certain; its identification with Homer's Troy and Hittite Wilusa is probable).
2026-10-21 · -10000-neolithic-revolution · new · established · Least sure of the end date (a convention, not an event) and the regional pins for Mesoamerica, eastern North America and the Sahel, which are town, state-centroid and valley proxies; the Younger Dryas causal link is contested.
2026-10-21 · -3300-invention-of-writing · new · established · Least sure of the start date and priority: the Tomb U-j radiocarbon date (c. 3320 BCE) is disputed and Uruk IV is only conventionally c. 3200 BCE; the Oaxaca and Yinxu pins are single-gazetteer points.
2026-10-21 · person-siddhartha-gautama · new · probable · Least sure of the dates (a spread of roughly eighty years or more, with the pin set to the long chronology only as a convention), the exact Kapilavastu coordinates, and the Harvey authorship (the CUP page opened shows only an overview, not the byline). Biographical details come from a museum essay and tradition, not early texts I opened; UNESCO site pages were read in the browser pane. Coordinator: Sarnath and Kushinagar waypoint coordinates are correct to within about 1 km but are attributed to the Met essay, which gives no coordinates; re-source them in enrichment.
2026-10-21 · 1789-french-revolution · new · established · Least sure of the Terror and Vendee death figures: Greer's 1935 tables could not be opened, so 16,594 and the 30,000-50,000 range rest on secondary summaries; the Vendee figure is a single sourced estimate.
2026-10-21 · 1543-scientific-revolution · new · contested · Least sure of the bounds (1543 to 1687 are a convention, and the SEP entry was read only to its first 100,000 characters), of historical polity names for the pin (not separately sourced beyond the cited pages), and of Principia publication details, since Library of Congress and Royal Society catalogue pages returned errors.
2026-10-21 · 1947-08-15-indian-independence · new · established · Coordinator review: removed figures attributed to sources that do not contain them (200,000-2 million deaths, 75,000-100,000 abducted women, 565 princely states); restored to Chester (2002) and TNA. Enrichment should add the Menon & Bhasin / Butalia abduction figures and the princely-state count from opened sources. Partition mortality estimates remain wide (200,000 to 2,000,000) due to colonial administrative breakdown and unrecorded deaths during mass migration; exact boundary coordinates along Punjab and Bengal canals reflect the 17 August awards rather than the 15 August transfer day.
2026-10-21 · place-great-wall-of-china · new · established · Least sure of the engine path: the 2012 survey line is not openly published, so geometry rests on eight sourced placed elements; also the start date (c. 500 BCE vs ICOMOS's Chunqiu-period origin) and the 'other' 1,573.5 km not broken down in the SACH report.
2026-10-21 · -0220-qin-great-wall · new · established · Least sure of the start date (221 vs 215 BCE in the two Shiji chapters), of the route between Lintao and Liaodong (not separable from Han walls in the survey, and Lintao's location is not pinned), and the death toll, which no opened source quantifies.
2026-10-21 · 1368-ming-great-wall · new · established · Least sure of the phase lengths, which Yang et al. reconstruct from documents and GIS with acknowledged error, and of the year of the Longqing peace, which the Ming Shi passage consulted does not state explicitly (1571 not asserted here).
2026-10-21 · -0507-classical-athens · new · established · Least sure of the population figures (all modern estimates based on military manpower figures, citizens counted as men only, no ancient count of women) and of the 322 BCE end bound, which is a convention chosen to bracket the democracy.
2026-10-21 · place-tenochtitlan · new · established · Coordinator review: Toxcatl death-toll claim (2,000-3,000) removed: not found on the cited Olivier paper. Least sure of the 1519 population (sourced estimates span 75,000 to 212,500) and the precise island area (9.44 to 13.5 km²); the island footprint is a digitized reconstruction map rather than a modern field survey.
2026-10-21 · person-jesus-of-nazareth · new · probable · Least sure of the exact birth year (6 to 1 BCE, dependent on Herod's death) and the crucifixion year (30 versus 33 CE), as first-century sources provide relative synchronisms rather than absolute calendar years.
2026-10-21 · 1450-gutenberg-printing-press · new · established · Least sure of the start date (the date of the first working type is unknown; 1450 rests on a 1499 recollection and Dittmar's 1448–1450) and of Rubin's headline figure, which comes from the 2011 working-paper abstract, not the published article; the Mainz point is the city centre, not the workshop.
2026-10-21 · 1775-american-revolution · new · established · Coordinator review: Yorktown surrender corrected to the cited almost 8,000; Loyalist exile figure (60,000) removed as not on its cited page. Least sure of eighteenth-century troop tallies and casualty figures due to conflicting rosters and retrospective estimates across sources.
2026-10-21 · person-muhammad · new · established · Least certain of the exact historical circumstances and casualties surrounding the Banu Qurayza arbitration, where classical Sira reports conflict with modern revisionist assessments.
2026-10-21 · 1492-columbus-first-voyage · new · established · Least sure of the exact island of Guanahaní (San Salvador vs Samana Cay) and pre-contact demographic baselines (colonial estimates of one million vs genomic Ne of 1,530–8,150).
2026-10-22 · 1760-industrial-revolution · new · established · Coordinator review: Removed Watt patent number (913) not shown on the cited Science Museum page. Least sure of the conventional chronological bounds (1760–1840 vs. narrower waves) and of the precise quantitative weighting between domestic high wages and colonial slavery profits in triggering the initial takeoff.
2026-10-22 · 1787-abolition-of-slavery · new · established · Least sure of the exact pace and extent of de facto emancipation in nomadic Mauritanian society following the 1981 statutory decree prior to the 2007 criminalizing statute.
2026-10-22 · 1914-world-war-i · new · established · Coordinator review: WWI civilian and total death ranges trimmed to what the cited pages state (as many as 13 million civilians; an estimated 16 million total). Least sure of the exact global tally of civilian deaths (estimates run as high as 13 million owing to unrecorded war-related famine and disease, compounded by the 1918 influenza outbreak) and whether the official termination of the war should be anchored to the 11 November 1918 Armistice or the 28 June 1919 Treaty of Versailles.
2026-10-22 · 1917-russian-revolution · new · established · Coordinator review: Pogrom deaths restated as tens of thousands, per USHMM. Least sure of the upper bounds for White Terror and Civil War pogrom deaths due to chaotic record-keeping, and whether the Civil War's formal end should be dated to Wrangel's evacuation in 1920 or the creation of the USSR in December 1922.
2026-10-22 · person-benito-mussolini · new · established · Least sure of the exact casualties specifically attributable to mustard gas in Ethiopia given the lack of contemporary census records, and of the debated details regarding who fired the fatal shots at Giulino di Mezzegra.
2026-10-22 · person-joseph-stalin · new · established · Coordinator review: display corrected; Russia used the Gregorian calendar from 1918, so the 1953 death date has no Julian form. Least sure of the exact boundary between systemic policy failure and specific genocidal intent during the 1932–1933 famines outside Ukraine, and the degree to which medical delays following his 1 March 1953 stroke were orchestrated by Beria and Malenkov versus driven by fear.
2026-10-22 · person-adolf-hitler · new · established · Least sure of the exact degree of administrative micromanagement versus subordinate improvisation in early Holocaust directives, as reflected in the intentionalist-functionalist debate.
2026-10-22 · 1939-world-war-ii · new · established · Least sure of the exact global death count (demographic estimates vary between 70 and 85 million due to unrecorded civilian famine and disease in China, the USSR, and colonies) and whether historiographical consensus will formally adopt 1937 rather than 1939 as the global starting date.
2026-10-22 · 1941-the-holocaust · new · established · The exact proportion of victims killed by mobile shooting operations versus camp gas chambers remains subject to ongoing archival research in post-Soviet local mass graves.
2026-10-22 · 1942-manhattan-project · new · established · Least certain of the precise portion of early Congolese pitchblende processed specifically into Little Boy versus Hanford reactor targets, as wartime inventory accounting aggregated Shinkolobwe and Canadian feedstocks after refinery blending.
2026-10-22 · 1945-08-06-hiroshima-bombing · new · established · Least sure of the exact number of colonized Korean forced laborers and military personnel present at detonation, which creates the wide variance between early counts and later demographic models.
2026-10-22 · 1945-08-09-nagasaki-bombing · new · established · Least sure of the exact total death toll (RERF range of 60,000–80,000 acute deaths vs Nagasaki City committee count of 73,884), as wartime municipal rosters were destroyed and thousands of unrecorded Korean forced labourers were present.
2026-10-22 · 0030-crucifixion-of-jesus · outline · probable · Least sure of the year: the date page is a secondary catalog summarizing Humphreys and does not name its calendar. Crucifixion under Pilate rests on Tacitus; Gospel details not checked here.
2026-10-22 · org-nihon-hidankyo · outline · established · Founding date and place come from two secondary pages; Nobel and Britannica pages were blocked. Nagasaki pin is the founding venue city, not a current office.
2026-10-22 · 1521-siege-of-tenochtitlan · outline · established · Least sure of the start date (April vs late May by source) and of the calendar: 13 August 1521 is assumed Julian per convention, converted to 23 August. Pin is Mexico City centre, not the exact Templo Mayor.
2026-10-22 · 1916-central-asian-revolt · outline · probable · Least sure of exact start and end dates and of the pin: the revolt spanned Turkestan and Semirech'e, so the pin is a regional anchor with wide uncertainty. Death toll not stated because sources disagree (tens of thousands to over 100,000).
2026-10-22 · 1945-korean-atomic-bomb-victims · outline · probable · Numbers differ across sources (about 70,000 exposed and 40,000 killed vs up to 30,000 killed in Hiroshima and 10,000 in Nagasaki) and are not stated in the summary. Pin is Hiroshima only.
2026-10-22 · 1792-black-loyalist-migration-sierra-leone · outline · established · Least sure of the 1,196 vs about 1,200 count (DCB says some 1,200 gathered, Dalhousie 1,196 sailed) and of the exact arrival day.
2026-10-22 · person-mary-magdalene · outline · probable · Historical details beyond the Gospels are uncertain; her hometown comes from a popular history page. The Gospel of Mary and other non-canonical traditions were not opened. Pin is her reported hometown, not a verified residence.
2026-10-22 · person-bilal-ibn-rabah · outline · probable · Least sure of dates and of who freed him (Abu Bakr per one page; WikiShia calls this uncertain). Sources are modern encyclopedic or popular pages, not early Arabic sources.
2026-10-22 · org-korean-atomic-bomb-victims-association · outline · probable · Founding place is not stated by opened pages, so the pin is the Hapcheon area with wide uncertainty. The article calls it the Korean Atomic Bomb Victims Relief Association; exact legal name and later history are unchecked.
2026-10-22 · 0615-first-migration-to-abyssinia · outline · probable · Least sure of the year (613 vs 615 in tradition) and of the pin: the event spans Mecca, the Red Sea crossing and Aksum; the pin is the destination city, not a precise site. Identity of the Najashi (Ashama; modern proposals Armah or Ella Tsaham) is not settled. Source base is late Muslim tradition.
2026-10-22 · -6750-maize-domestication-central-balsas · outline · established · Least sure of the pin: coordinates are from GeoNames for the barranca beside the shelter (the paper gives no latitude/longitude). The companion Piperno et al. 2009 starch/phytolith paper was not opened (its host returned 403); the claim is read from Ranere et al. 2009, which summarizes it.
2026-10-22 · 0800-terminal-classic-maya-collapse · outline · probable · Least sure of the single pin: the collapse is regional; Tikal is only a reference point in the southern lowlands (uncertainty set to 300 km). Cause is debated (drought, war, trade shifts); the summary does not assert one cause.
2026-10-22 · place-templo-mayor · outline · established · Least sure of the start date: opened pages date the Mexica settlement (c. 1325), not the first temple stage. The 1978 Coyolxauhqui discovery was read on the INAH museum page but is not entered in as_recorded.
2026-10-22 · 1520-smallpox-epidemic-in-mexico · outline · established · Least sure of the pin and extent: the epidemic was regional; Tenochtitlan is the anchor. No death toll is stated. The month of arrival in Tenochtitlan (popular sources say autumn 1520) was not confirmed on a scholarly opened page.
2026-10-22 · polity-xiongnu · outline · established · Least sure of the pin (a representative point for a very large steppe state, 700 km uncertainty) and of the end date, which depends on whether the Northern Xiongnu collapse (91 CE) or the later disappearance of the Southern shanyu is taken as the end.
2026-10-22 · 0105-cai-lun-papermaking · outline · probable · Least sure of the Gregorian equivalence of Yuanxing 1 (taken as 105 CE from the reference summary, not from a conversion table) and of the place: the Hou Hanshu implies the court but names no building. The Wikisource page is a modern transcription in simplified characters.
2026-10-22 · place-monte-verde · outline · contested · DATING CONTESTED since March 2026 (Surovell et al., Science); other archaeologists dispute it, so both ranges are kept. Coordinates are a GeoNames populated-place fix for Monte Verde near Puerto Montt, not a surveyed excavation point; neither opened page gives site coordinates (10 km uncertainty).
2026-10-22 · place-hattusa · outline · established · Least sure of the exact start of the capital (a "1600s" statement) and the end year (1190 BC per one ministry source; other reconstructions put the abandonment near 1200 BC). Both opened sources are state/UN bodies; no independent scholarly chronology was opened.
2026-10-22 · 1539-first-printing-press-americas-mexico-city · outline · established · Least sure of the pin: sources only say Mexico City (Centro); the coordinates are the Templo Mayor, a few hundred metres away, hence the 1 km uncertainty. The press-house location was not confirmed on an opened page. Earlier press claims (Escala espiritual, c. 1537) are unsurvived and unconfirmed.
2026-10-22 · 1511-hatuey-resistance · outline · probable · Place and day (Yara, 2 Feb 1512) rest on one popular site; the World History page gives only the year 1512. Another account places the burning at Baracoa. The Las Casas account was not opened directly. Hatuey's arrival date in Cuba is unverified, so the start is the conquest year.
2026-10-22 · 0062-execution-of-james-the-just · outline · probable · Year 62 is inferred from the date given for Albinus; the passage itself is undated. Some scholars dispute the phrase identifying James as brother of Jesus; later Christian traditions of his death differ and were not opened. A stub exists at 0062-execution-of-james-brother-of-jesus that may be the same event.
2026-10-22 · 1920-09-01-congress-of-the-peoples-of-the-east · outline · established · Delegate counts vary by source (1,891 in the stenographic protocol; about 1,900 to over 2,000 elsewhere), so the summary says nearly 1,900. The Comintern summons is attested by a Marxists.org foreword, not opened in full here.
2026-10-22 · 1944-chechen-deportation-operation-chechevitsa · outline · established · Counts differ: about 496,000 (Soviet figures) to 650,000 deported; deaths 123,000–200,000 per one source. Summary uses approximate wording only. No academic or archival source opened; the upgrade should add one.
2026-10-22 · 1942-02-19-bombing-of-darwin · outline · established · Only one event source (Australian War Memorial) opened; National Archives of Australia page timed out. Casualty figures vary in the literature ("about 243" in the 1950s histories, later revised upward); the summary says about 250.
2026-10-22 · 1556-first-printing-press-india-goa · outline · established · Both sources are journalism; no scholarly or museum page opened. Sources disagree on the first book (a 1557 Doctrina Christam is cited elsewhere; The Goan names the 1563 Orta Colóquios as the first documented work), so the summary avoids naming one. Upgrade should add a library or scholarly source.
2026-10-22 · 0052-thomas-christians-malabar · outline · legendary · Tradition (Thomas arrives 52) and earliest evidence (4th–6th century) are kept apart. Pin at Kodungallur is the Cranganore of the later community tradition; no opened source locates the supposed landing, so uncertainty is wide. GEDSH reads the date as "52" without stating CE; the CE reading is inferred. The Kalyan/Quilon identification of Cosmas's Caliana is disputed.
2026-10-22 · species-denisovans · outline · established · Outline is for the species via its type locality. Pages disagree on age of the original finger bone (UCSC 2010 says about 30,000 years, later work much older), so no bone age is given. Coordinates match the existing Neanderthal record; GeoNames summary was returned by the fetch tool secondhand.
2026-10-22 · place-kuk-early-agricultural-site · outline · probable · UNESCO pages (WHC 887) returned 403, so the inscription and exact site coordinates are unconfirmed; the pin is the Mount Hagen town point from GeoNames, with the ANU "12–13 km northeast" offset noted only in uncertainty_km (20). A refinement is needed from UNESCO. Earlier reports of ditches about 9,000 BP are not used.
2026-10-22 · -7450-rice-domestication-lower-yangtze · outline · contested · Domestication timing is contested (wild, transitional or domesticated); the span here is a compromise of opened sources. The record id suggests c. 7450 BCE but opened sources support about 9000–6450 BCE, so the id and dates differ. Pujiang County centroid is used for the Shangshan site; exact site coordinates not opened.
2026-10-22 · -3319-abydos-tomb-u-j-inscriptions · outline · probable · Dates conflict by about 200 years across sources (3320 vs 3300 vs 3100 BCE); no source opened here gives the radiocarbon calibration directly. Coordinates are the Umm el-Qa'ab necropolis, not the tomb itself.
2026-10-22 · -1219-shang-oracle-bone-inscriptions · outline · established · Pin is the Yinxu site, not a single findspot; the id year -1219 falls in the Wu Ding era but the range here is the whole capital period. The 150,000 and 4,500 figures come from a provincial government news item.
2026-10-22 · 1428-aztec-triple-alliance · outline · established · Year is firm, but the order of events (war council, Tepanec War, formal alliance) and the roles of Texcoco and Tlacopan are only lightly sourced here.
2026-10-22 · 0184-yellow-turban-rebellion · outline · established · Least sure of location: the uprising was spread over several provinces; EBSCO names Henan and Shandong, while Zhang Jue's base is usually placed in Hebei, which the opened sources did not state. End year varies by source.
2026-10-22 · 1780-tupac-amaru-ii-rebellion · outline · established · Pin is Tinta; the march on Cusco and later battles lie outside it. The rebellion label 1780-1781 follows the target title while sources extend the wider revolt to 1783.
2026-10-22 · 1802-napoleonic-restoration-of-slavery · outline · established · Two theatres combined in one pin; the formal Guadeloupe restoration decrees (dated later in 1802 or 1803 in some accounts) were not confirmed on an opened page. Slavery was never formally restored in Saint-Domingue.
2026-10-22 · 1644-battle-of-shanhai-pass · outline · established · Exact day uncertain (27 vs 28 May in common accounts; 18 May is when Li left Beijing); check a source using the Chinese calendar before expansion.
2026-10-22 · 1948-09-13-operation-polo · outline · contested · Death toll is contested: the 27,000–40,000 figure is reported through a book review by a critic of the operation; official figures are lower and I did not open them. Pin is the capital city; the fighting covered the state border.
2026-10-22 · 1816-bussas-rebellion · outline · established · Pin is the parish where the first fires were set, not a single plantation; CARICOM gives a planned date of 17 April and leader roles varied by estate.
2026-10-22 · 0378-arrival-of-sihyaj-kahk-at-tikal · outline · probable · Day-level date and the same-day-as-Jaguar-Paw-death reading depend on Stuart's interpretation, which his page calls debated. The Calendar Round month name (15 Mac) is as printed by Stuart and was not checked against a calendar table.
2026-10-22 · 0330-christianity-in-aksum · outline · probable · Least sure of the date: sources place the conversion anywhere from the mid-330s to the 340s, and Butts treats the Frumentius story as a late outside narrative; the span given is Ezana's reign, not a pinned event date.
2026-10-22 · 1947-12-06-recovery-of-abducted-women · outline · established · Least sure of the place: only the Tribune names Lahore as the venue; the Graduate Institute says only that the treaty was signed 6 December 1947. The Nehru Archive page was blocked and not opened.
2026-10-22 · -3500my-barberton-greenstone-belt · outline · probable · Least sure of the "early life" reading: biogenicity of the Moodies laminae is an interpretation (Gamper et al. say "suggest"); the 3.5 Ga in the id is a midpoint, not a measured age.
2026-10-22 · -3480my-pilbara-stromatolites · outline · probable · Coordinates are a Geoscience Australia field-site point labelled "Dressers Barite centre" (no coordinates in the papers). A stub with id -3480my-dresser-formation-stromatolites also exists and may be a duplicate to merge.
2026-10-22 · -539my-nama-group-ediacaran-cambrian-boundary · outline · contested · Least sure of the boundary: Nelson et al. suggest the Cambrian base may be younger than 537.6 Ma and Bowyer et al. question some ash-bed ages. The pin is the Swartkloofberg area, one of several Nama localities.
2026-10-22 · place-catalhoyuk · outline · established · Least sure of the end date: UNESCO runs occupation to 5200 BCE via the west mound, while the east mound is abandoned about 6200 BCE; other scholarship gives somewhat different spans.
2026-10-22 · 1991-draining-of-the-mesopotamian-marshes · outline · established · Least sure of the end year and area figures: NASA says about 85 percent area lost and HRW about 90 percent, and UNEP attributes part of the loss to upstream dams. The pin is a single point for a region about 100 km across.
2026-10-22 · 1520-toxcatl-massacre · outline · established · Least sure of the day: Kruell says 23 May while other accounts give 20–22 May; the Spanish justification (an alleged plot) is contested and not assessed here.
2026-10-22 · person-zhang-qian · outline · established · Least sure of life dates: sources disagree on birth (164 vs 195 BCE) and return (125 vs 126 BCE). The pin is Chang'an, the departure point, not a single place of activity.
2026-10-22 · 1945-06-26-un-charter · new · established · Least sure of the signatory count (153 in FRUS vs 160 in a UN history) and of the small-state perspective, sourced mainly from one scholarly article; the Wikisource copy of GA res 66(I) is a transcription.
2026-10-22 · -0110-han-conquest-of-nanyue-and-northern-vietnam · outline · established · Least sure of the pin and the commandery detail. The BAS page was read for dating, the Josephus translation for the Jericho setting only; the pin is modern Jericho, not the exact palace site. Burial at Herodium was not confirmed on an opened page and is not claimed.
2026-10-22 · person-rajkumari-amrit-kaur · outline · established · Least sure of the end of her ministerial term: opened sources show her as Minister of Health in 1956 and as AIIMS president from 1957, but no page states the exact end date. 2026-10-22 · 1947-06-03-mountbatten-plan · outline · established · Least sure of the pin: the plan was broadcast from New Delhi and read in both Houses in London; New Delhi is used. 2026-10-22 · -2499-pearl-millet-domestication-sahel · outline · probable · Least sure of the dating basis: the Manning et al. 2011 paper itself could not be opened (ScienceDirect blocked), so its dates are read from later papers that cite it (Scientific Reports 2025, African Archaeological Review 2021) and the paper is confirmed only through its Crossref record. The pin is the Tilemsi commune, a proxy for the Lower Tilemsi Valley sites.
2026-10-22 · -2499-indus-script · outline · established · Least sure of the pin and span: the script is spread over a region of about a million square kilometres, so the Mohenjo-daro pin carries a 600 km uncertainty, and the dates come from one museum object plus a general chapter. Sign-count estimates range from about 400 to hundreds more depending on counting method.
2026-10-22 · place-calakmul · outline · established · Least sure of the dynasty dates: UNESCO and EBSCO disagree on when the Kaan arrived at Calakmul (Late Classic move from Dzibanche versus early sixth century), so the range is wide. Coordinates are from Wikidata for the ruins; the UNESCO centre point for the large World Heritage property (N18 3 10.9, W89 44 14.22) lies about 8 km away and is not used.
2026-10-22 · person-ban-zhao · outline · established · Least sure of the pin and death date. The sources say she worked in the imperial library at "the capital" (Dongguan Imperial Library) but do not name Luoyang; identifying the Later Han capital as Luoyang is reference knowledge. Death is given as 116 or 120.
2026-10-22 · place-valongo-wharf · outline · established · Least sure of the end date: 1843 is when a later wharf was built over the site, not when landings stopped, and no opened page gives the end of landings there. The 900,000 figure is a UNESCO estimate ("up to" in the synthesis text).
2026-10-22 · polity-vilcabamba · outline · probable · Least sure of the founding year (1537 vs 1539/1540) and of the exact coordinates, which are Wikidata's for Espíritu Pampa; the state's territory was much wider than the point.
2026-10-22 · polity-arzawa · outline · contested · Coordinator review: removed a Wikipedia-mirror source (HandWiki) and narrowed the span to the 14th century BCE that Garstang supports. Least sure of location: Garstang placed Apasa near Antiphellus (Kaş), a modern view puts it at Ephesus, and the Seha River Land is also debated; the 1700–1300 BCE span is a loose reference figure.
2026-10-22 · place-piprahwa · outline · contested · Least sure of the date: the museum says 4th-5th century BCE for the relics while the inscription is reported as 240–200 BCE; the Kapilavastu identification is also contested.

2026-10-22 · 1449-tumu-crisis · outline · established · Least sure of the day: sources disagree (31 August, 1 September, lunar 15th day of the 8th month); troop numbers in the travel source were not used.

2026-10-22 · -0599-early-zapotec-writing-san-jose-mogote · outline · contested · Least sure of dating (600 vs 200 BCE) and of the glyph reading (1 Earthquake vs 1 Motion); the id year follows Marcus.

2026-10-22 · 1950-korean-war · new · established · Least sure of the casualty ranges: the totals rest on one scholarly essay (Yuh) plus an amateur compilation of older estimates, because the Armstrong, Cumings and PRIO sources were not retrievable; the UN resolutions were read only as database abstracts, not the original scans.

2026-10-22 · 1990-end-of-apartheid · new · established · Least sure of the exact CODESA opening date and the Record of Understanding date (sources here give only month or year), of the SAHO-hosted election and referendum figures (secondary compilations of IEC data), and of Khoisan and other Indigenous voices, which I did not find sourced.
2026-10-22 · person-che-guevara · new · established · Least sure of: the Las Coloradas landing site name (taken from gazetteers, not from an opened narrative page), the Congo dates (sources differ: April-November 1965 vs EBSCO's July 1965-March 1966), and that sources contest the La Cabaña toll and his personal role.
2026-10-22 · 1953-cuban-revolution · new · contested · Least sure of the Moncada and Granma counts (Castro's own speech; survivors 12 to 17 by source), the execution totals (an advocacy NGO and a student thesis quoting Bandeira), and women's roles, which are thin: the Mariana Grajales platoon and Celia Sanchez could not be sourced to a page I opened.

2026-10-22 · 1950-05-01-chinese-marriage-law · outline · established · Pin is Beijing as national capital; the law applied countrywide and the sources do not name the signing place. Law text and Council meeting dates were not opened.
2026-10-22 · 1945-06-07-brazilian-declaration-women-un · outline · probable · Credit is divided: SOAS credits Lutz and Bernardino with Article 8, while the Wilson Center credits Pinto de Vidal with the equal-access amendment. No exact day was confirmed, so the id's 06-07 is unverified.

2026-10-22 · 1959-first-agrarian-reform-law-cuba · outline · established · Pin is the Sierra Maestra range as a whole; other searched sources name the Comandancia de La Plata but I did not open one that confirms it. The 30-caballeria cap and bond terms come from one source.
2026-10-22 · 0562-defeat-of-tikal-star-war · outline · probable · The victor is unresolved (Martin: unknown; the Snake kingdom is a candidate, Caracol's king is discounted). Long Count requested but unconfirmed, so absent. Tikal ruler's fate is not stated in the sources I opened.
2026-10-22 · place-tlatelolco · outline · established · Dates are from chronicles; INAH and UNAM agree on 1473. The 1968 massacre is a separate stub (1968-10-02-tlatelolco-massacre) and was not researched here. The 1521 end of Mexica rule was not on the opened pages.
2026-10-22 · process-intra-american-slave-trade · outline · established · No aggregate captive total was found on opened pages; the Emory 2019 article cites "some 92,000 Africans" in a sentence whose scope is unclear, so I omit it. 2026-10-22 · 1935-10-03-second-italo-ethiopian-war · outline · established · Invasion commenced on 3 October 1935 with Italian troops crossing the border and air attacks on Adwa and Adigrat; coordinates anchor at the capital Addis Ababa via GeoNames 344979.
2026-10-22 · 1941-12-07-attack-on-pearl-harbor · outline · established · Attack commenced at 7:55 a.m. local Hawaiian time on Sunday, December 7, 1941; coordinates point to Pearl Harbor, Oahu via GeoNames 10057006.
2026-10-22 · 1948-12-09-genocide-convention · outline · established · Palais de Chaillot hosted the 1948 UN General Assembly session where Resolution 260 A (III) was adopted; coordinates are anchored to Chaillot district in Paris via GeoNames.
2026-10-22 · 1954-03-01-castle-bravo-and-daigo-fukuryu-maru · outline · established · Detonation took place on 1 March 1954 on an artificial island off Namu Island, Bikini Atoll; coordinates anchor to Bikini Lagoon via GeoNames 2080854.
2026-10-22 · place-chichen-itza · outline · established · Shape: stepped_pyramid (nine terraces, four stairs, summit temple). Height 24 m excludes the 6 m summit temple. Coordinates are the UNESCO property point, within about 0.1 km of the pyramid. Least sure of the facing stone; the opened papers say the infill is mostly limestone and give no facing material. Restoration dates (1930s) are from the same papers, not an INAH page.
2026-10-22 · place-hagia-sophia · outline · established · Shape: domed_building. Heights disagree by datum: ASCE gives a dome height of 48.5 m (160 ft) without naming the datum, World History Encyclopedia gives 55 m above the floor; both are listed. The museum year is the 1934 cabinet decision (Harvard, Al Jazeera); World History Encyclopedia says 1935. Length and width (81 x 73 m) come from a non-institutional reference page.
2026-10-22 · place-forbidden-city · outline · established · Shape: block (a group of rectangular halls on terraces). Hall figures (64 x 37.2 m, 26.92 m high, 35.05 m with platform) come from a Guangming Daily article quoting Palace Museum material, not from the Palace Museum site, which would not load; the palace extent (760 x 960 m) is from the Japanese-language UNESCO text. The point is the Hall, not the whole palace; start 1406 is from EBSCO only.
2026-10-22 · place-taj-mahal · outline · established · Shape: domed_building (square chamfered tomb, central dome; minarets and chhatris omitted from the base shape). Total height 74.22 m, 57 m square tomb and 100.05 m plinth are from a university photograph-collection record. Other sources give about 73 m and plinth heights of 6-7 m. UNESCO gives 1631-1648.
2026-10-22 · place-rapa-nui-moai · outline · established · Shape: statue_group. The pin is the Rano Raraku quarry, where 397 statues remain; the 887 are spread over the island on scattered platforms. Typical height 13 ft (about 4 m) is NOVA's average; UNESCO gives a 2-20 m range. The plan dimension is the island length (14 miles, Smithsonian Magazine). The date range is UNESCO's; other sources date carving to 1250-1500.
2026-10-22 · place-eiffel-tower · outline · established · Shape: tower. Height 300 m is the as-built height (ASCE, EBSCO); today's figure with antennas is about 330 m. The base is 125 m a side (EBSCO). ASCE calls the material cast iron, EBSCO elsewhere mentions wrought iron, so the material is given as iron. The official Eiffel Tower site could not be fetched.
2026-10-22 · place-statue-of-liberty · outline · established · Shape: statue. Heights are NPS figures (ground to torch tip 305 ft 1 in; waist width 35 ft). The exact day of dedication was not entered since the NPS caption (28 October 1886) was not on a dated text page; year only. The construction start is not sourced, so the phase uses the 1886 inauguration. Coordinates: UNESCO point, which matches the Wikidata point to about 25 m.
2026-10-22 · place-world-trade-center-1973-2001 · outline · established · Shape: tower (two). Heights are the roof heights, 1,368 ft and 1,362 ft; the North Tower also carried a 360 ft antenna mast (NIST). Footprint about 210 ft square (NIST); floors about 206 ft. The pin is the midpoint of the two Wikidata tower points (1 WTC 40.7121, -74.0132; 2 WTC 40.7111, -74.0131). The start of construction is not stated on any page opened, so the construction phase begins at the 1972 completion of the North Tower.
2026-10-22 · place-teotihuacan · outline · established · Shape: stepped_pyramid. Height 75 m and base 225 x 222 m are UNESCO figures; EBSCO gives 212 feet (about 65 m), so the height is the least certain number. The stub at this id was overwritten. Coordinates: Wikidata point for the pyramid, about 0.2 km from the UNESCO property point (N19 41 30, W98 50 30).
2026-10-22 · place-templo-mayor · outline · established · Least sure of the start date: opened pages date the Mexica settlement (c. 1325), not the first temple stage. The 1978 Coyolxauhqui discovery was read on the INAH museum page but is not entered in as_recorded.

2026-10-22 · place-stonehenge · outline · established · Shape ring: the outer sarsen circle (30 m across) and its concentric settings. 2026-10-22 · place-gobekli-tepe · outline · established · Shape ring (enclosures of T-pillars, per the brief). The date the enclosures were backfilled is unsourced, so the 'ruined' phase begins at the end of the PPNA range. The DAI's own blog (dainst.blog) was unreachable, so the Tepe Telegrams mirror on wordpress.com is cited.
2026-10-22 · place-acropolis-of-athens · outline · established · Shape temple_colonnade for the Parthenon; the place record is the whole Acropolis (UNESCO: about 170 by 350 m, hill 156 m). The 'intact' end year is the midpoint of 'the fifth century AD'. The MIT length and width sit in the page's embedded metadata JSON, which spotcheck strips; confirmed by reading the raw page. Coordinates are the Parthenon way in OpenStreetMap, about 70 m from UNESCO's point for the property.
2026-10-22 · place-colosseum · outline · established · Shape arena. Least sure of the start year (70 AD per Roma Capitale, 75 per Texas Tech) and the arena size (76 x 46 m vs 86 x 54 m; the wooden floor is lost, so both are reconstructions). Height 48 m is the lower figure; Texas Tech gives 48.5 m. Material span for the cavea and wooden floor is limited to the period of use (80-438) because later survival is not sourced. Coordinates are the POINT printed on the Turismo Roma page, matching UNESCO's Italian component point.
2026-10-22 · place-petra · outline · probable · Shape block, not a freestanding temple: Al-Khazneh is a rock-cut facade, so a flat block at the sourced width and height with a cut-in portico is the closest of the available shapes, and the depth is unsourced. Least sure of the date: it rests on the Aretas IV attribution, which the MIT record itself calls probable. The pin dates the monument, not the city. The sandstone material is UNESCO's description of the landscape, not of the facade specifically; MIT lists only 'stone'. Google Arts & Culture gives a smaller 23 x 30 m, treated as an outlier against the MIT/Archnet survey figures.
2026-10-22 · place-great-zimbabwe · outline · probable · Shape ring: the Great Enclosure, an ellipse not a circle (the plan dimension is the long axis of 88.5 m from EBSCO). Least sure of the wall height: the three opened sources disagree (ICOMOS 7 m, National Geographic Education over 9.7 m, EBSCO 11 m); ICOMOS is listed first as the heritage body, and the true maximum should be treated as uncertain. The Met Museum and Britannica pages (11 m, 252 m) were blocked, so not opened. The 1400 boundary between construction and intact is a round number inside the '14th century'. ICOMOS PDF text is OCR.
2026-10-22 · place-angkor-wat · outline · established · Shape stepped_pyramid (three levels with a quincunx of towers). Least sure of the base size: 187 x 215 m is the outer gallery per Notre Dame and is printed in parentheses in the MIT record next to the 1025 x 802 m outer wall, so the MIT figure's meaning is ambiguous; I used Notre Dame for the pyramid-level plan and MIT for the enclosure. The 65 m height is in the MIT record and is repeated by the German Angkor Imagery Project, a non-institutional source (class other), which is the one spotcheck can read. The MIT figures (1025 x 802, 60 x 60, 65 m) sit in the page's embedded metadata JSON, which spotcheck strips; I confirmed them by reading the raw page. The 'intact' phase glosses over 16th-century changes and modern restoration, noted in the description only. UNESCO's points for property 668 are not at the temple, so coordinates come from OpenStreetMap.
2026-10-22 · place-borobudur · outline · probable · Shape stupa: UNESCO calls the main temple 'a stupa built in three tiers', so stupa fits better than stepped_pyramid. Base 120 m a side is JAXA's; an e-museum copy of a UNESCO publication gives 113 m (123 m overall with projections) and 'nearly 35 m' high, which agrees on height. The 1814 reference appears only in the Japanese text of UNESCO's page.

Herat, Tabriz and Istanbul waypoints use city-centre Wikidata coordinates.

The Tiberieum reading is partly conjectural and has been disputed.
2026-10-22 · 1571-longqing-peace-with-altan-khan · outline · established · The year 1571 is firm on both sources. The day-level dates in search results (title on 21 April, investiture 13 June) were not on an opened page and are not used. The pin is Datong as a stand-in for the frontier market towns, which spread across several garrisons (hence 250 km). Tribute figure of 500 horses a year is from EBSCO only.
2026-10-22 · 1951-korean-war-comfort-stations-and-women-volunteers · outline · probable · Two distinct subjects joined in one record at the requester's instruction. Founding date 6 September 1950 comes from a 1960 Stars and Stripes piece. The comfort-unit facts (about 1951 start, closed March 1954, 89 women in 1952 per the army's 1956 Rear-Area Military History, an estimated 300 women in total by sociologist Kim Gwi-ok) come from a 2020 news report, not the army volume itself; the 1951 start is an estimate. The Women's Voluntary Labor Service Corps was not confirmed on an opened page and is not claimed.
2026-10-22 · 1994-bantustan-reincorporation · outline · established · 27 April 1994 is from SAHO only; EBSCO says only 'in 1994'. The 'no foreign recognition' claim and the legal instrument (the 1993 interim constitution) were seen in search snippets, not on an opened page, so the instrument is not named. The Bophuthatswana crisis of March 1994 is not covered here. The pin is Pretoria as a stand-in for a nationwide change.
2026-10-22 · 1958-12-battle-of-santa-clara · outline · established · Start of the fighting (often given as 28 December) was not on an opened page, so the date is 'late December 1958'. Both sources are secondary; the Cuba Solidarity Campaign article is an advocacy publication. Beat dated 1958-12-31 is placed by the 'within 12 hours' wording relative to the 30 December surrender and the capture; the exact capture day was not confirmed on an opened page. Garrison size and rebel numbers are given as the source states them.
2026-10-22 · 1945-04-25-san-francisco-conference-ethiopia-liberia-delegations · outline · established · The Asian side is thin: opened pages confirm China's role as a sponsoring power (T. V. Soong chaired plenaries) but do not itemise the other Asian delegations, so only China is named. Egypt counts among the four African states per Africa Renewal. 2026-10-22 · 1950-12-hungnam-evacuation · outline · established · Refugee figures vary by source (98,100 on the DVIDS page; navy-only counts are lower) and the counter uses the DVIDS figure. 2026-10-22 · -514my-emu-bay-shale-lagerstatte · outline · established · The record id uses -514 Ma; the Heritage Council document gives the span 514-509 Ma, the only numerical age on an opened page. The Journal of the Geological Society review (514 +/- 1 Ma) could not be opened. Coordinates are the Wikidata point for the formation (on the north coast near Emu Bay); Big Gully is the fossil locality, within a few km. Model spread for the paleo-position is large; Merdith 2021 is the stored value.
2026-10-22 · person-olaudah-equiano · outline · contested · Birthplace is contested: the pin is the centre of Anambra State, Nigeria, as a coarse stand-in for his own account of an Igbo birth; Carretta's South Carolina evidence (1759 baptismal record, 1773 muster) is recorded in the summary, not pinned. Birth year c. 1745 rests on one opened page; the exact death day was not confirmed on an opened page.
2026-10-22 · 1919-03-01-march-first-movement · outline · established · Casualty totals are left out of the summary because sources disagree; the Korean Culture Project page gives over 7,000 killed, about 16,000 wounded and about 47,000 arrested, and lower Japanese-era counts were not verified on an opened page. The Korea Culture Project page is a nonprofit newsletter, not scholarship.
2026-10-22 · 1937-02-19-yekatit-12-massacre · outline · established · Death toll reported as a range: 1,400-6,000 (newspapers and others), about 3,000 (Mockler, Del Boca), about 19,000-19,200 (Campbell 2017, roughly 19-20% of the city), 30,000 (Ethiopian authorities), as listed on the opened pages; not adjudicated. End date 21 Feb is inferred from "three days". The second source is a publisher page, not the book itself. The memorial in Sidist Kilo and Debre Libanos reprisal are not covered.
2026-10-22 · person-haile-selassie · outline · established · Sources disagree on when his regency began (1916 vs 1917), so the summary only says regent. Biography.com lists Harar as birth city but its text names Ejersa Gora; the pin is the village. Both sources are non-scholarly; a scholarly biography should replace them at expansion.
2026-10-22 · 1932-kazakh-famine-asharshylyk · outline · established · Death toll shown as a range from RFE/RL (at least 1 million; Omarbekov 2.3 million Kazakhs) and the Wilson Center page (more than 1.5 million, citing Cameron). 2026-10-22 · place-shinkolobwe-mine · outline · established · Opening date of the mine was not confirmed on an opened page, so the when range starts at the 1940 shipment. The two-thirds share is the NPS statement about Union Minière supply to the Manhattan Project. Tonnage 1,200 tons (NPS) differs from other published figures (about 1,050 tons seen only in a search lead). Mining labour conditions not covered.
2026-10-22 · person-sadako-sasaki · outline · established · Exact birth and death days not confirmed on opened pages. Sources disagree on distance from the hypocentre (about a mile vs two kilometres) and on how many cranes she folded; both omitted. Pin is the Hiroshima city centroid. No animation: no sourced life path.
2026-10-22 · place-ziggurat-of-ur · outline · established · Shape ziggurat. Least sure of the height: the only figure from a museum page is Penn Museum's 92 feet (28 m) from a 1924 restoration plan, while Khan Academy gives 21 to 30 m; the 1924 lower stage (130 x 195 ft) also disagrees with the 64 x 46 m base used here, which is the modern summary figure. Khan Academy renders its text by script, so the spotcheck tool cannot fetch it; its figures were read through a text proxy. The 'ruined' start (fifth century BC) follows the 1924 report. Coordinates: OSM way and UNESCO Ur component agree to about 30 m.
2026-10-22 · place-great-mosque-of-djenne · outline · established · Shape block (rectangular mass with towers; no dome). Least sure of the dimensions: Zamani Project (University of Cape Town) lists 85 x 65 m and an 18 m central minaret, while other popular accounts (not opened) give a 75 x 75 m platform and about 16 m; the Zamani figures are the only university or museum measurements found. The standing mosque is the 1906-07 rebuilding; the 1280 first mosque is not modelled.
2026-10-22 · place-lalibela-rock-hewn-churches · outline · established · Shape block, modelling only Bete Giyorgis (the place record covers the whole site). The construction window 1150-1250 follows 'a 100-year period around 1200 AD' and is approximate. Trench depth was not found in a university or museum source, so none is stored. The UNESCO XML point for property 18 (12.0333 N, 38.8167 E) puts the longitude about 24 km west of the town, so the OpenStreetMap node for Bete Giorgis is used instead.
2026-10-22 · place-sanchi-great-stupa · outline · established · Shape stupa. The diameter and height come from UNESCO's Japanese-language description (36.6 m base diameter, 16.5 m current height); the spotcheck string is in Japanese. Least sure of the phase boundaries: the Ashokan start is the century only, the gateway period is 'first century BC', and 1901 for the restoration is the start of 'the early 20th century'.
2026-10-22 · place-persepolis · outline · established · Shape temple_colonnade for the Apadana (the place is the whole Persepolis terrace). Least sure of the plan and height: the 109 m and 58 m figures are the apadana type as reconstructed from the Persepolis and Susa plans in Encyclopaedia Iranica, not a Persepolis-only survey; column heights range 19 m (Iranica) to 25 m (Livius, class other), so UNESCO's 20 m is used. The destroyed phase is dated to 330 BCE; Livius itself headlines the burning as a question ('Destruction?').
2026-10-22 · place-cahokia-monks-mound · outline · established · Shape stepped_pyramid (four terraces) rather than mound. Least sure of the date: the marker text (class other) says 950-1200 and an Archaeological Conservancy report on a 2015 study favours a short chronology starting about 1050 or later, so 950 is the older, longer model. Height and base are the Cahokia Mounds Museum Society's figures; base depends on the contour used (291 x 236 m at 130 m, 320 x 294 m at 128 m). UNESCO's own text says 30 m, fourteen stages, four terraces.
2026-10-22 · place-leshan-giant-buddha · outline · established · Shape statue (single seated figure). Least sure of the shoulder width: china.org.cn gives 24 m, while other accounts (not opened) give 28 m; the source is a government information site, not a museum or university. The 803 completion is arithmetic from 'a 90-year period beginning in 713', not a quoted date. UNESCO XML and OSM points are about 0.46 km apart; the OSM point is used.
2026-10-22 · place-bamiyan-buddhas · outline · established · No model written: UNESCO gives heights (55 m and 38 m; the UNESCO Publishing volume says 56 m for the Western Buddha) but no source opened gives a niche or statue width, so the plan-dimension rule is not met. Least sure of the dates: the statues are dated only by radiocarbon to the second half of the 6th century (East) and start of the 7th (West), and other sources date them differently. Point is the midpoint of the two OSM nodes, about 0.8 km apart.
2026-10-22 · place-tikal-temple-i · outline · established · No model written: Penn Museum's Expedition article gives a height (Temple I, 145 feet, nine terraces) but no museum, university or heritage-body page opened gives a base or plan dimension. 2026-10-22 · place-shwedagon-pagoda · outline · established · No model written: UNESCO's tentative list gives the height (99 m after 1774, 112 m after the 1871 umbrella) and a 5.66 ha platform, but no university, museum or heritage page opened gives the stupa's base diameter, so the plan-dimension rule is not met. Least sure of the start date: 'between the 6th and 10th centuries AD' is a wide window and the 750 midpoint is a convenience. Tentative-list coordinates (N16.798356 E96.149705) and OSM agree to about 100 m.
