No model written: INAH gives the Temple of the Inscriptions as eight levels and nearly 23 m high, but no base dimensions from an acceptable page (a tourism page gives 60 by 42.5 m and 27.2 m, which disagree with INAH on height); skipped.
2026-10-22 · place-copan · outline · established · Least sure of the end year: UNESCO says abandonment in the early 10th century, so 900 is a round figure. No model written: UNESCO says the Acropolis mass rises some 30 m and the Great Plaza covers three hectares, but I found no sourced single-structure height with a plan dimension from an acceptable page.
2026-10-22 · place-caracol · outline · established · Least sure of the end: the Classic period is AD 250-950 on the project page and AD 250-900 in the Springer article (which spotcheck cannot fetch, so it is background only). No model written: Caana's height (43.5 m above the plaza) is sourced, but the opened pages give no base dimensions, and it is a four-palace, three-temple complex rather than a single pyramid.
2026-10-22 · place-elmina-castle · outline · established · Least sure of the end-date convention (1872 cession used as the end of the European-power era; the castle still stands). No model written: I found no sourced height from an acceptable page; Museu EXEA gives only an area of about 9,000 square metres.
2026-10-22 · -1800ka-homo-erectus-dispersal-from-africa · outline · contested · Least sure: Ain Hanech (the source says hominins, not Homo erectus, and the evidence is lithic); Java dates (1.3-1.5 Ma versus older argon dates); no East African waypoint because the earliest African erectus (ca. 1.78 Ma, per Ferring) is not later than Dmanisi and would reverse the route order. Coordinates are Wikidata, except Dmanisi (Ferring et al.).
2026-10-22 · -65ka-peopling-of-sahul · outline · contested · Least sure: the route does not reach Australia, because dated Australian sites (Madjedbebe 65 ka claim; Warratyi 49 ka and others, not opened) would break the date order of a single path; Ivane Valley coordinates are a proxy (Kokoda); Niah is a park centroid. 65 versus 50 ka debate is stated in when and summary.
2026-10-22 · -45ka-modern-humans-across-eurasia · outline · probable · Least sure: Ust'-Ishim at 44 ka (Sumer) versus 45 ka midpoint (2014 paper); Ranis point is the town, not the cave; the four sites are not one migrating band, and Levant stepping stones are omitted for lack of an opened source.
2026-10-22 · -3000-bantu-expansion · outline · contested · Least sure: Great Lakes date (600 BC vs 1500 BCE vs 2nd-5th century CE); rainforest point is a basin centroid; KwaZulu-Natal and Kwale are regional proxies, not sites; Shum Laka stands for the homeland; the newsletter source is class other and should be replaced by an academic review.
2026-10-22 · -3000-austronesian-expansion · outline · probable · Least sure: the Philippines waypoint is a country centroid with a range-only date; the Barito and Madagascar waypoints share one year (the date is a linguistic estimate, and archaeology disagrees); the route skips Sulawesi and the Pacific arm; the Taiwan midpoint blends two date estimates. Needs a source naming Batanes or Luzon sites (e.g. Nagsabaran).
2026-10-22 · -1300-lapita-expansion · outline · probable · Least sure: Fiji and Samoa are not waypoints (Fiji dates overlap Teouma/Tonga; no opened source dates Samoa); the Bismarck point is a centroid and its date is an upper bound that Petchey et al. consider too old; Vatcha is "shortly after 3000" approximate; Tonga site not named. The Oxford Handbook and ANU Press sources are reused from the Austronesian record.
2026-10-22 · 1000-polynesian-settlement-of-east-polynesia · outline · probable · Least sure: no dated West Polynesian starting waypoint is included (Wilmshurst says only that Polynesians dispersed from the Samoa-Tonga area); the waypoints are island-group arrival dates in order, not one voyage; Hawaii and New Zealand points are centroids; the short chronology is the current mainstream view but older longer chronologies still appear in the literature.
2026-10-22 · 1000-thule-inuit-migration · outline · probable · Least sure: the Smith Sound date is the generic migration date, not a site date; the three points are regions, not dated sites; timing is genuinely debated (AD 1000, late 11th century, 1200-1250, plus a 2022 paper argued in search results that some eastern dates pre-date 1200, not opened). Add Birnirk or Cape Parry site dates and Greenland site dates in the full record.
2026-10-22 · -335my-assembly-of-pangaea · outline · established · Pangaea assembly was diachronous, so the dates are a representative window, not a single event. The pin is the Appalachian Mountains centre point on Wikidata (the southern Appalachians hosted the ~290 Ma terminal collision per Murphy et al.). The Nance et al. abstract gives only period names, not numeric ages. Paleo-position computed at 310 Ma.
2026-10-22 · -190my-opening-of-the-central-atlantic · outline · established · Opening age is contested: 190 Ma (Labails) vs about 168.5 Ma for the Blake Spur anomaly (Greene, who frames it as a possibility); other literature quotes 195-175 Ma, not opened. The pin is a loose centre of the Blake Plateau, not the oldest oceanic crust.
2026-10-22 · -130my-opening-of-the-south-atlantic · outline · established · Dates are for the southern Brazilian margin only; breakup continued northward to about 113 Ma and other sources date first breakup earlier. Pin is Wikidata Pelotas Basin centre (offshore); no paleo-position is possible for it.
2026-10-22 · -50my-india-asia-collision · outline · established · Collision age is genuinely disputed (59 vs 55 vs 50 vs 40 Ma). The pin is Lhasa as a proxy for the long east-west suture zone, so the uncertainty is large.
2026-10-22 · -201my-central-atlantic-magmatic-province · outline · established · Pin is a single dated intrusion, not the province centre. The 40,000-year duration comes from MIT News/EarthSky and may refer to the main eruptive bursts; the Science paper (not opened) is said elsewhere to describe pulses over a longer interval.
2026-10-22 · -201my-end-triassic-extinction · outline · established · The extinction was global; the pin is the Triassic-Jurassic boundary GSSP, a reference section, not an extinction epicentre. The ICS page gives the boundary age but does not describe the extinction; the 76 percent figure is from MIT News.
2026-10-22 · -252my-siberian-traps-magmatism · outline · established · Regions follow UN M49 (Russia is Eastern Europe) with Central Asia as a secondary tag, a judgement call. The pin is Noril'sk, a northern lava-section locality; the province centre lies further south. The source gave no coordinates for the traps. Volume: Burgess and Bowring give a minimum of about 3 x 10^6 km3 and a broader range of 1 to over 10 x 10^6 km3.
2026-10-22 · -66my-chicxulub-tsunami · outline · established · The pin is the crater centre; the megaripple field in Louisiana is about 1,000 km away. Wave height is as stated by Curtin. The ULL page does not state ripple dimensions, so none are given.
2026-10-22 · place-meishan-gssp · outline · established · Coordinates converted from IUGS degrees-minutes-seconds (31 04 51 N, 119 42 22 E); the ICS page gives a slightly different, garbled form. Ages vary widely by method; 251.94 Ma is the extinction age, not the formal boundary age.
2026-10-22 · -3430my-strelley-pool-stromatolite-reef · outline · established · The pin is the Duda et al. sampling section on the other side of the Shaw River from the Trendall locality, not the exact type locality; the reef extends over more than 10 km of outcrop (Allwood). Biological origin is debated, and the summary reflects that. No GPlates model reaches 3.43 Ga.
2026-10-22 · -70ka-out-of-africa · enriched · established · Least sure of the start date (genetic clocks depend on mutation-rate and generation-time assumptions, giving ~72 to ~50 ka) and of the contested Madjedbebe (~65 ka) and Lida Ajer (73–63 ka) dates; several site pins are approximate (Payakumbuh for Lida Ajer, Jabiluka Mine for Madjedbebe, Mount Carmel centroid for Misliya/Skhul).
2026-10-22 · -23ka-peopling-of-the-americas · enriched · contested · Least sure of the start date (White Sands LGM ages versus critics and the post-16 ka consensus) and of Monte Verde after the 2026 challenge; Pigati 2023 and Surovell 2026 were read only as abstracts/records, not full text; the Anzick pin is a Park County proxy.
2026-10-22 · place-machu-picchu · outline · established · Least sure of the end year: the Antiquity abstract says 1532 while Yale says about 1530. UNESCO point 13.1849 S, 72.5334 W is the sanctuary property, not a single building, so uncertainty is set at 1.5 km. No model: no height and plan of one structure were opened.
2026-10-22 · place-coricancha · outline · established · Least sure of the date: both sources only say 15th century and Pachacuti is an attribution. The church consecration year 1654 is Getty CONA only. No model: no wall height or plan dimension was found on an opened page.
2026-10-22 · place-saqsaywaman · outline · established · Least sure of the start year (mid-15th century is one source; the Peruvian register is undated). No model written: the sources conflict on wall height (18 m in one, 5 m stones in the other) and I found no wall height from a heritage body, so the linear wall model is left out.
2026-10-22 · place-eridu · outline · established · Least sure of the dates: World History Encyclopedia gives both c. 5400 and c. 5000 BCE for founding, so I used only the scholarly sixth-millennium statement; its abandonment c. 600 BCE is a single source. The UNESCO XML lists seven unnamed points for property 1481 and I took the one at 30.817 N, 45.996 E as Eridu by position.
2026-10-22 · place-memphis-and-its-necropolis · outline · established · Least sure of the start: the Penn page dates the Old Kingdom capital only, and tradition places Memphis earlier. The pin sits on the open-air museum at Mit Rahina, 1 km from the UNESCO point; the necropolis itself spans some 30 km, hence 2 km uncertainty.
2026-10-22 · place-jerusalem · outline · established · Least sure of the start: the study dates the first settlement, with sparse evidence, to the 20th century BC; earlier activity is discussed in the literature but I did not open a source for it. Neutral wording used; no opened source covers the city's contested status, so it is left out. The pin is the UNESCO Old City point.
2026-10-22 · place-hiroshima · outline · established · Shape domed_building. The record is the city, but the pin and model are the Genbaku Dome (UNESCO point) because the model rules need one structure; the city centre lies about 1.1 km away. Least sure of the dome diameter: 10.6 m is the long axis of a pseudo-ellipse from a 1989 university paper, short axis 8.6 m.
2026-10-22 · place-urakami-cathedral · outline · established · Least sure of the construction dates: the museum says 1895 to 1914 with towers in 1925, the prefecture says consecrated 1914 while incomplete and finished 1925. No model: no height or plan dimension of the cathedral appeared on any opened page (one travel blog gave 26 m for the towers, unconfirmed).
2026-10-22 · place-moncada-barracks · outline · established · Least sure of the start: 1859 is the authorisation, works began January 1860, and one article only places its origins under Governor Tacon. All sources are Cuban state-aligned press. No model: no dimensions found on an opened page.
2026-10-22 · place-ur · outline · established · Least sure of the start: the 1960 Penn text says perhaps the fifth millennium. The city point is the same UNESCO coordinate as the ziggurat record place-ziggurat-of-ur (the ziggurat is modelled there, not here).
2026-10-22 · 1960-03-21-sharpeville-massacre · outline · established · 69 dead in every source; wounded given as 180 seriously wounded (SAHO, citing the official inquest) and more than 180 (CMHR). Banning of ANC/PAC is 7 April 1960 in SAHO but 8 April in CMHR; SAHO used. Crowd size also varies by source.
2026-10-22 · 1976-06-16-soweto-uprising · outline · established · Deaths: 176 after police opened fire and closer to 700 as protests spread (Africanews); about 566 schoolchildren killed during the protests (SAHO). Hector Pieterson is 12 per SAHO and 13 per Africanews. Pin is the township centre, not a single street.
2026-10-22 · person-nelson-mandela · outline · established · Pin is the birthplace; his life ran through Johannesburg, Robben Island and Pretoria. The Foundation says convicted 11 June 1964 and sentenced the next day; Nobel gives 12 June. The 27-year figure is from SAHO.
2026-10-22 · 1962-10-cuban-missile-crisis · outline · established · Pin is Havana as a stand-in; decisions were taken in Washington and Moscow, and the quarantine ran across the Atlantic approaches. Start date uses the 14 October photographs; History.com counts 13 days. Jupiter missile removal from Turkey (April 1963) is a secret-deal element not stated as a public bargain.
2026-10-22 · 1950-09-15-inchon-landing · outline · established · Pin is Incheon city; Wolmido lies a few km west. Seoul recapture date was not on the opened pages, so the summary says only "days later". No path animation: only three dated points were sourced and two lack coordinates in sources opened.
2026-10-22 · 1948-01-30-assassination-of-mahatma-gandhi · outline · established · Pin is Birla House (Gandhi Smriti). Gandhi's reported last words are disputed and left out. The trial opened in May 1948 at the Red Fort and the hangings (Ambala Jail) are dated November 1949 only by month in Live History India.
2026-10-22 · 1971-12-16-liberation-of-bangladesh · outline · established · Casualties: "most estimates" 300,000 to 3 million (The Daily Star, 2007), R. J. Rummel about 1.5 million (same page), "upwards of 3 million" (Amnesty USA). The official Bangladeshi figure is 3 million; Pakistani official figures are far lower but were not on an opened page. Surrender was signed at Ramna Race Course, Dhaka, per search results only. Region tag: southern_asia.
2026-10-22 · 1791-haitian-revolution · outline · established · Queue tagged this "oceans"; region set from sources. Pin is Cap-Haitien as the centre of the northern uprising, with wide uncertainty. Toussaint Louverture's role in 1791 is not asserted. No casualty figures on opened pages. Independence day (1 January 1804) was not on an opened page.
2026-10-22 · polity-kingdom-of-kongo · outline · established · Queue tagged this "oceans"; region set from sources. Dates are broad: no source opened gives a founding year or a single end date. Pin is the capital; the realm spanned a much larger area.
2026-10-22 · 1888-brazil-lei-aurea · outline · established · Pin is central Rio de Janeiro; the Senado page says the princess awaited the law at the Paco da Cidade. Vote counts differ on secondary sites and were not used. The "last country in the Americas" claim was not on an opened page and is omitted.
2026-10-22 · place-lumbini · outline · probable · Birth at Lumbini and the 623 BCE date are Buddhist tradition (UNESCO states them as fact; they are reported here as tradition). Confidence uses the schema value "probable" because the schema has no "traditional" level. Coordinates are UNESCO's property point, 27 28 8.004 N, 83 16 33.996 E. Stub related links from the other Buddhist sites are mutual.
2026-10-22 · place-bodh-gaya · outline · probable · No 3D model written: UNESCO gives the temple as 50 m high and the ASI says 55 m, and no opened page gives a plan dimension. Dates of the enlightenment and of Ashoka's temple are traditional. Coordinates are UNESCO's 24 41 43.008 N, 84 59 38.004 E. Confidence "probable" because the schema has no "traditional" level.
2026-10-22 · place-sarnath · outline · probable · Model shape: stupa, the closest listed form to a solid brick-and-stone cylinder. Dimensions rest on a single tourism-ministry page (43 m, 28 m); the same ministry's Sarnath page gives 42 m, and other sites quote 33 to 43.6 m, so the height is the weakest number. The phase dates (249 BCE Ashoka, 500 CE rebuild) are as stated by that page; the Ashokan attribution is tradition. UNESCO's page lists no Dhamek dimensions. Coordinates are the Wikidata point for the Dhamek Stupa; Wikidata is not a publisher. Confidence "probable" because the schema has no "traditional" level.
2026-10-22 · place-kushinagar · outline · probable · The sutta source is a translation of the canonical text and is undated; the death date is traditional. The Indian tentative-list entry gives 26 45 N, 83 55 E (to the arcminute), while Wikidata gives 26.740 N, 83.889 E, so uncertainty is set to 3 km. I found no model-quality dimensions: the tentative text says the main stupa may once have reached 45.72 m but gives no plan dimension. Confidence "probable" because the schema has no "traditional" level.
2026-10-22 · place-tilaurakot · outline · contested · Identification with Kapilavastu is contested (Piprahwa is the rival). Durham says the citadel is about 500 by 400 m and the site is 3 km north of Taulihawa; Wikidata supplies the point. "Shakya capital" is tradition, as is the Mahapajapati link (the Buddha's stepmother).
2026-10-22 · place-chengjiang-fossil-site · outline · established · Two opened sources disagree on age: UNESCO 530 Ma and IUGS about 518 Ma; the record uses 518 with a 12 Ma range. Coordinates are UNESCO's property point, 24 40 8 N, 102 58 38 E, matching IUGS. Merdith 2021 paleo-position is indicative only.
2026-10-22 · place-burgess-shale · outline · established · Age differs between sources (ROM about 507 Ma; foundation 508 Ma). Coordinates are the Wikidata point for the formation (51.4333 N, 116.467 W), near the Walcott Quarry; UNESCO's Canadian Rockies listing gives only a park-wide point so it was not used. The ROM introduction page was used for location in Yoho National Park. MERDITH2021 failed for this point, hence the PALEOMAP fallback.
2026-10-22 · place-omo-kibish · outline · probable · Age is a minimum from the overlying tuff and is revised from the 2005 value. Pin is the dated tuff locality at Nakaa-kire near the Omo II site, 5 24.6 N, 35 54.5 E, from the McDougall supplementary table listing; the Wikidata point for the formation (4.80 N, 35.97 E) is about 67 km south and was not used. The Omo I find spot is not given on opened pages, so uncertainty is 10 km. Page text for the Vidal paper opened via PubMed Central.
2026-10-22 · place-misliya-cave · outline · probable · Both opened sources are journalism summaries of the 2018 Science paper (Hershkovitz et al.), which I could not open (403). The age is stated as published; a later published comment questioning the uranium-series dates appeared in search results but was not opened, so the dispute is flagged without detail. Coordinates are from Wikidata.
2026-10-22 · place-wadi-al-jarf · outline · established · Archaeology Magazine gives Khufu as r. ca. 2633-2605 B.C.; the existing Giza record uses 2589; the date range here follows the harbour, not a Khufu regnal date. The "60 miles south of Ayn Sukhna" locator is from that page; the pin is the Wikidata point for the area, uncertainty 5 km. Archaeology Magazine says 2008 for the site's re-location; IFAO says excavated since 2011.
2026-10-22 · 1950-10-19-chinese-intervention-in-korea · outline · established · Least sure of the exact crossing date: Chen Jian describes units crossing from 17 October and a 19 October deadline. The army.mil page gives only a timeline entry for 19 October, and the pin is a border stretch, not one crossing point.
2026-10-22 · 1953-07-27-korean-armistice-agreement · outline · established · Least sure of the pin: Panmunjom coordinates come from Wikidata, with a few km of slack.
2026-10-22 · 1964-rivonia-trial · outline · established · Least sure of the start date (inferred from trial length) and the count of accused: UCT says eleven accused with all but two convicted; the Foundation names eight sentenced and Rusty Bernstein acquitted on 11 June.
2026-10-22 · 1994-04-27-south-african-general-election · outline · established · Least sure of the date label: sources give a four-day voting window, while the record ID says 27 April. The pin is symbolic, as the election was nationwide.
2026-10-22 · 1950-01-26-constitution-of-india · outline · established · Least sure of the pin: sources do not name a place for the entry into force, so the pin is the capital.
2026-10-22 · 1947-10-22-first-kashmir-war · outline · established · Least sure of the start date: the record ID says 22 October but no opened page gave that day, so the date is month-level. Accession and ceasefire dates are disputed between sources. The pin is the Kashmir Valley, not a battle site.
2026-10-22 · 1945-08-09-soviet-invasion-of-manchuria · outline · established · Least sure of the pin (a regional centre, not a battle site) and the troop figures. No sourced dated waypoints, so no path animation.
2026-10-22 · 1954-guatemalan-coup · outline · established · Least sure of the day-level dates: the opened sources give only "June 1954". Casualties and motives are contested; the framing here follows US government records.
2026-10-22 · 1781-zong-massacre · outline · established · Least sure of the pin, a stand-in at Jamaica because the position of the killings is not given. Death toll sources agree on 132 but split it differently. Only departure and arrival are dated, so no path animation.
2026-10-22 · 1948-12-10-universal-declaration-of-human-rights · outline · established · Least sure of the "reference point for later law" claim, which is general knowledge rather than from an opened page. Venue is from the UN legal library page captions.
2026-10-22 · place-florisbad · outline · contested · Least sure of the age: the 259 ka ESR date rests on a tooth-skull association that Berger and Hawks dispute. S2A3 describes the site as a salt pan north-east of Bloemfontein; Wikidata gives the pin. Hawks is a personal site (class other).
2026-10-22 · place-lida-ajer · outline · probable · No source I opened gives cave coordinates. The pin is Payakumbuh (Dubois's report names Payakombo in the Padang Highlands), so uncertainty is 25 km. Several caves in the region share the name Lida Ajer.
2026-10-22 · place-ahwar-of-southern-iraq · outline · established · The property has seven separate components; the pin is Wikidata's single point, so 80 km uncertainty. I could not open UNESCO's own page (403). End year 2016 is the inscription, not an end of the place.
2026-10-22 · place-silk-roads-chang-an-tianshan-corridor · outline · established · Waypoints are 8 of the 33 listed components, west to east; component names come from a tour-guide list (class other) because UNESCO returned 403. Sources disagree on length (5,000 km vs 8,700 km), so none is stated. Pin is the Wikidata centre in Shaanxi.
2026-10-22 · place-yumen-pass · outline · probable · The UW page gives no founding date or exact location for the pass; dates follow the commandery (111 BCE) and the Stein documents (90s BCE), so the start is inferred. Other sources date the pass from 121 BCE.
2026-10-22 · place-korean-demilitarized-zone · outline · established · Length figures differ: NASA 250 km, EBSCO 258 km (160 miles); the summary says roughly 250 km. "Most heavily militarised" in the importance text is not from an opened source. The pin is Wikidata's centre; the zone is a line, not a point.
2026-10-22 · place-nazareth · outline · probable · Start year 1 is approximate; evidence points to the Late Hellenistic period. Custodia is a Franciscan source (class other). The bibleinterp page gives no coordinates, so the pin is Wikidata.
2026-10-22 · 1992-06-17-boipatong-massacre · outline · established · Date 17 June is from one opened page (Commonwealth Oral History); the other opened pages give only June 1992. Death toll given as a range: about 40 (Commonwealth), more than 40 (Mail & Guardian), 45 (O'Malley page); the TRC figure of 46 was seen only in a search snippet and is not used. The police-involvement question is contested (TRC 1998 vs Amnesty Committee 2000). Pin is the township centre.
2026-10-22 · 1993-04-10-assassination-of-chris-hani · outline · established · Date and Boksburg confirmed on both opened pages. Hani as SACP leader is from the TRC/Sapa page; his title (general secretary) is not used. Pin is the town centre, not the house. The teenage daughter detail and amnesty refusal come from search snippets; the sentencing and parole detail is from the Mail & Guardian page. Check before publishing.
2026-10-22 · 1988-cuito-cuanavale · outline · contested · Contested outcome stated as such: the Angolan government, Cuba and the ANC say South Africa was decisively defeated; many South African veterans reject that; The Conversation describes a tactical stalemate. Casualty figures are left out of the summary: The Conversation lists 79 South African dead as admitted by South Africa; DefenceWeb notes SADF claims of 500 Angolan and 32 Cuban casualties at Chambinga on 14 February 1988 and says figures vary. Pin is the town; engagements were spread along the Lomba River and nearby.
2026-10-22 · 1952-03-10-batista-coup · outline · established · Pin is Havana city centre with 15 km uncertainty because the opened EBSCO page says only "the main army camp in the capital" (Camp Columbia is not named on an opened page). The election date is not on an opened page and is not stated.
2026-10-22 · 1961-04-17-bay-of-pigs-invasion · outline · established · Pin is the bay, not a beach: the opened pages do not name Playa Girón or Playa Larga. Brigade size is 1,400 on the CIA page (other accounts say 1,400-1,500). Killed and captured totals are not on the Office of the Historian page; the CIA page gives more than 75 percent imprisoned and 1,113 held. Death count deliberately omitted. The 19 April end date is "within two days" and not a stated date.
2026-10-22 · 1956-12-02-granma-landing · outline · established · Both opened sources are Cuban outlets (Radio Rebelde is state media); no museum or university page could be opened (several blocked). The route is two dated points joined by a straight line, not the actual track. Alegría de Pío (5 December) is not used because no opened page names it. Casualty and survivor counts are omitted: accounts differ.
2026-10-22 · 1967-10-09-execution-of-che-guevara · outline · established · Sources agree on 8 October capture and 9 October execution; time of death differs (1:10 vs 1:15 p.m.). Executioner is described as Sgt. Jaime Terán in the National Security Archive page, who "volunteered" or was chosen by straws depending on the account; not named in the summary. CIA presence is Felix Rodriguez per the archive page. Pin from Wikidata, with 3 km allowance.
2026-10-22 · 1946-08-16-direct-action-day · outline · established · Deaths given as attributed figures: 4,000 dead and 100,000 injured as the official estimate (Banglapedia, not independently verified); 5,000 to 10,000 dead and about 15,000 wounded (Sciences Po, noting that no precise figures exist). Blame is contested and the summary does not assign it. Pin is the city centre; the worst-hit area is a district of north Calcutta.
2026-10-22 · 1945-08-15-jewel-voice-broadcast · outline · established · Dates confirmed on both opened pages (recording 14 August, broadcast 15 August). The time of broadcast and the exact recording location are not used because only a tour-company page and search snippets give them. Pin is the palace, where the recording was made; the broadcast itself went out nationwide. Fate of the coup plotters not described.
2026-10-22 · 1950-07-26-nogeun-ri-massacre · outline · contested · Casualty figures are attributed in the record, not in the summary: South Korea certified 163 dead or missing and 55 wounded in 2005 (Military.com); the No Gun Ri Peace Foundation estimates 250-300 killed; survivors estimate about 400; the US Army says the number is unknown. Orders are disputed: the Army found no evidence of orders to shoot; South Korean investigators cited veterans who believed such orders existed. Dates 26-29 July are on two opened pages. Not opened: Wikipedia, AP articles.
2026-10-22 · place-qhapaq-nan · outline · probable · Dates are from ASCE (1000 BC to 1533 AD; Inca expansion 1493-1527), which other scholars may date earlier for the Inca state. UNESCO returned 403, so only 4 waypoints are tied to sources I opened; Huánuco Pampa coordinates are Wikidata Q9005560, and Pachacámac and the Inca Trail are Wikidata items that are part of the Qhapaq Ñan. UNESCO counts 273 components.
2026-10-22 · place-heit-el-ghurab · outline · established · Coordinates are the Giza pyramid complex (Wikidata Q12508), not the site: no source I opened gives coordinates for Heit el-Ghurab, which AERA places about 400 m south of the Sphinx on the Giza Plateau. Uncertainty set to 1.5 km. Dates are rounded (c. 2600 BC; no reign years opened).
2026-10-22 · place-sirius-passet · outline · established · Age is the Stage 3 range (521-514 Ma) from Park et al. 2019; the fauna is not dated more finely in sources opened. Wikidata (82.793, -42.245) and IUGS (82 47 35 N, 42 13 32 W) coordinates differ by about 2 km.
2026-10-22 · -2588-construction-of-the-great-pyramid · outline · established · Exact years depend on which Old Kingdom chronology is used; the atlas uses the ministry's reign dates. The ministry page gives height (146.5 m) but no base length, so no model was written here (see place-giza-pyramids).
2026-10-22 · -2557-carving-of-the-great-sphinx · outline · probable · Attribution to Khafre is the ministry's reading, not proven; neither opened page gave the Sphinx's dimensions, so no model (shape would be statue) was written.
2026-10-22 · -2565-diary-of-merer · outline · established · The point is the pyramid site. The route is Tura to Akhet-Khufu in day-by-day entries without calendar dates, so no path animation was written. Wadi al-Jarf is where the papyri were found, not a stop in the log.
2026-10-22 · 1356-mosque-of-sultan-hasan · outline · established · Shape "block" for the whole complex (facade 38 m high x about 145 m long); the same page also gives a 36.7 m portal. The patron is named differently in the two sources (Hasan v. al-Nasir Muhammad). The Giza casing link is not confirmed on any page I opened, so no link is drawn.
2026-10-22 · person-khufu · outline · probable · Reign length is the weak point: Year 26 (Tallet) conflicts with 23 years (Turin canon). Coordinates are his tomb.
2026-10-22 · -3500-white-temple-of-uruk · outline · established · The point is the Uruk component point (31 19 27 N, 45 38 14 E), not the temple, so no model was written despite a 12 m terrace and 22 x 17.5 m hall. Both detail sources are class "other" (engineering firm, illustration studio).
2026-10-22 · -3200-uruk-expansion · outline · probable · The "colonies" reading is debated (Stein stresses symmetric exchange). The pin is Uruk with 1000 km uncertainty; the regional spread is stated, not mapped. Godin Tepe was not confirmed on a page I opened.
2026-10-22 · polity-tawantinsuyu · outline · probable · Start year is traditional and contested by radiocarbon; the pin is the capital, not the empire. UNESCO states the 30,000 km network reached its maximum in the 15th century during consolidation of Tawantinsuyu. Marsh 2017 was opened for the Mendoza range only.
2026-10-22 · person-pachacuti · outline · probable · The 1471 end rests on one reference site (class other) and is not corroborated by a second source I opened.
2026-10-22 · 1533-spanish-capture-of-cusco · outline · established · 8 versus 15 November is unresolved. Sources describe the city as taken with little fighting; no casualty figures were found.
2026-10-22 · species-non-avian-dinosaurs · outline · established · The outline anchors non-avian dinosaurs to the classic Hell Creek fossil beds of Garfield County, Montana, representing the final Cretaceous communities before the impact; the clade as a whole had a worldwide distribution throughout the Mesozoic.
2026-10-22 · -66my-paleogene-mammal-radiation · outline · established · The outline anchors the global Paleogene mammalian radiation to the Denver Basin's Corral Bluffs section, which uniquely preserves a continuous fossil record of mammal skull, tooth, and body size evolution from the K-Pg boundary through the first million years of the Paleocene.
2026-10-22 · 1980-alvarez-impact-hypothesis · outline · established · Coordinates pin the Bottaccione Gorge section near Gubbio in Umbria where Walter Alvarez collected the original pelagic limestone samples and thin boundary clay layer that Frank Asaro and Helen Michel analyzed for iridium at Berkeley.
2026-10-22 · 1991-identification-of-chicxulub-crater · outline · established · The outline pins Chicxulub Puerto on the north coast of the Yucatán Peninsula, the surface village above the crater center identified by Hildebrand and Penfield from Pemex borehole data and gravity anomalies.
2026-10-22 · person-clair-patterson · outline · established · Pinned to Caltech in Pasadena where Patterson spent his academic career from 1952 to 1995, built the first geochemical cleanroom, and did his seminal dating and lead-pollution work.
2026-10-22 · -4567my-formation-of-the-solar-system · outline · established · Astronomical event in the proto-planetary disk anchored on Earth to Pueblito de Allende in Chihuahua, Mexico, where thousands of fragments of the CV3 Allende carbonaceous chondrite fell on 8 February 1969, providing the pristine CAIs dated to 4567.3 Ma.
2026-10-22 · -4404my-jack-hills-zircon · outline · established · Least sure whether earlier magma ocean crystallization or localized impact melting formed the parent magma of grain W74/2-36.
2026-10-22 · -4031my-acasta-gneiss · outline · established · Least sure of the exact tectonic setting—shallow impact melting versus subduction initiation—that produced the felsic protoliths of the Idiwhaa unit.
2026-10-22 · -251my-early-triassic-biotic-recovery · outline · established · Least sure of the relative roles of episodic heat spikes versus persistent oceanic anoxia in delaying reef and benthic ecosystem recovery until the Middle Triassic.
2026-10-22 · -260my-end-guadalupian-extinction · outline · established · Least sure of the exact temporal overlap and causal kill mechanism connecting the terrestrial tetrapod extinction pulse in the Karoo with the explosive phase of the Emeishan Large Igneous Province in southwestern China.
2026-10-22 · species-lystrosaurus · outline · established · Least sure whether physiological adaptations like torpor or generalist dietary plasticity were the primary factor enabling Lystrosaurus to proliferate across post-extinction Pangaea.
2026-10-22 · -4200my-last-universal-common-ancestor · outline · probable · Least sure of whether LUCA should be pinned to hydrothermal vent environments versus open oceans; coordinates are set to global (0,0) as no single geographic site is preserved or known.
2026-10-22 · -3700my-isua-stromatolite-controversy · outline · contested · Least sure of whether any biogenic consensus will ever emerge for Isua, as metamorphic deformation is severe; the pin represents the general Isua supracrustal belt coordinates from Wikidata.
2026-10-22 · 1965-cuban-expedition-to-the-congo · outline · established · Least sure of the exact location of the main guerrilla mountain base above Lake Tanganyika; coordinates are anchored to Fizi as the key regional base territory.
2026-10-22 · 1967-bolivian-guerrilla-campaign · outline · established · Least sure of whether the campaign coordinate anchor should be the initial training camp at Ñancahuazú or the final battle and execution ground at La Higuera; pinned to La Higuera as the culmination point.
2026-10-22 · person-fidel-castro · outline · established · Least sure of whether to anchor his location to Havana or Birán; chosen Havana as his main lifelong seat of governance and deathbed.
2026-10-22 · 1950-bodo-league-massacres · outline · established · Least sure of the nationwide death toll, which official estimates place broadly between 60,000 and 200,000; the coordinate pin marks Daejeon (Taejon) as the most thoroughly documented and photographed execution site.
2026-10-22 · 1912-01-06-wegener-continental-drift-lecture · outline · established · Venue within Frankfurt (reported elsewhere as the Senckenberg Museum) was not on a page I opened, so the pin is the city centre (Wikidata). The two sources agree on date and city; the dealer page also gives the printed versions (Petermanns Mitteilungen, Geologische Rundschau of 9 July 1912).
2026-10-22 · person-alfred-wegener · outline · established · The exact day of death is not given on the pages I opened (UCMP says a day or two after his fiftieth birthday, i.e. November 1930); only the year is recorded. The pin is his birthplace.
2026-10-22 · -575my-ediacaran-biota · outline · established · Queue id says 575 Ma but the pages I opened give about 571 Ma for the oldest diverse assemblage; I used 571 (Palaeontology Online) and dated the pin to Mistaken Point at 565 Ma (UCMP). Other work dates the Drook Formation near 575 Ma; I did not open a page confirming that. The type area (Ediacara Hills, South Australia) was not on a page I opened.
2026-10-22 · -485my-great-ordovician-biodiversification-event · outline · established · No single place hosts a global event; Huanghuachang (Dapingian GSSP, South China, which GSA Today names as a GOBE study region) is a representative reference section, not an origin. The ICS page does not mention the GOBE. Sources differ on when the diversification began (some say early Floian or all of the Early Ordovician); I recorded the 485 to 460 Ma span of the GSA Today review.
2026-10-22 · -513my-sinsk-extinction-event · outline · established · Pin is the village of Sinsk, opposite which the Sinsk Formation type locality lies on the Lena River (Ivantsov 1999); exact outcrop coordinates were not found. Magnitude differs by paper (about 45 percent of genera vs over 60 percent of species). Queue id says 513 Ma; sources give 513.5.
2026-10-22 · -300ka-onset-of-the-middle-stone-age · outline · probable · Dates are for Olorgesailie only; the Middle Stone Age is not a single event and other African sites (e.g. Jebel Irhoud) are covered in related records. Wikidata gives the site to two decimals only; uncertainty set to 10 km for the basin.
2026-10-22 · species-homo-naledi · outline · established · Dates use the combined 236-335 ka estimate; the sediment unit alone spans 236-414 ka. Pin is the cave system (Wikidata); the Dinaledi and Lesedi chambers lie within it. The Smithsonian page gives 2015 as discovery date and lists over 1,550 Dinaledi specimens.
2026-10-22 · -160ka-herto-homo-sapiens · outline · established · Coordinates are the differentially corrected GPS point of holotype locality BOU-VP 16 (10 deg 15.5484 min N, 40 deg 33.3834 min E) in White et al. 2003. The Berkeley item is a press release (class reference). Whether the subspecies name idaltu is still accepted was not checked.
2026-10-22 · -47ka-neanderthal-modern-human-interbreeding · outline · probable · The sources do not say where the interbreeding happened; the pin is the Ust'-Ishim find area as an illustrative site of early Neanderthal ancestry, not the event place, hence the large uncertainty. The 43,500 end date is my subtraction of 7,000 from 50,500, implied but not stated by the Berkeley page. Queue id says 47 ka (the mean).
2026-10-22 · person-ust-ishim-man · outline · established · The find spot is uncertain (Fu et al.: exact locality unclear; the femur eroded from left-bank alluvium north of the settlement); the pin is the settlement per Wikidata with 20 km uncertainty. The date range is the calibrated radiocarbon interval, not a life span. Admixture timing differs between the MPG summary (50-60 ka) and the paper abstract (7,000-13,000 years before he lived).
2026-10-22 · 0695-tikal-victory-over-calakmul · outline · established · Least sure of the battle location: sources name only the two cities, so the pin is Tikal with a wide radius. Source accounts of the defeated king vary and were not reconciled.
2026-10-22 · 0440-maya-lowland-wet-interval · outline · probable · The pin is the cave region (cave about three miles from Uxbenka per Penn State), not a precise site. A single cave record; regional extent of the wet phase is the authors’ inference.
2026-10-22 · person-kinich-janaab-pakal · outline · established · Reign began 615 (World History Encyclopedia); exact birth/death days differ by source and are left out. Relationship to Lady Yohl Ik’nal was not checked in these sources.
2026-10-22 · 1536-siege-of-cusco · outline · established · The UMN-hosted course page only confirms a 1536 siege by Manco Inca (class other); dates rest on the World History Encyclopedia article. Phases and end date are loosely bounded.
2026-10-22 · 1650-03-31-cusco-earthquake · outline · established · Uncertainty_km is a placeholder for the affected area; no epicentre, magnitude or death count was confirmed in opened sources and none is claimed.

2026-10-22 · polity-hittite-empire · outline · probable · Start year uncertain (sources span c. 1700-1600 BCE for the kingdom, empire from c. 1400). Pin is the capital; the realm covered much of Anatolia.

2026-10-22 · -252my-karoo-basin-terrestrial-turnover · outline · established · Least sure of whether the diachroneity between Karoo vertebrate turnover and Meishan marine extinction reflects regional ecological decoupling or undetected stratigraphic hiatuses in the Karoo Beaufort Group.

2026-10-22 · -26ka-last-glacial-maximum · outline · established · Least sure of the global bounds chosen for the LGM (26.5–19.0 ka versus sea-level lowstand 29–21 ka BP), and representing a global climate event with a Greenland ice-core anchor point.

2026-10-22 · -13ka-clovis-complex · outline · established · Least sure of the exact dates of the earliest regional pre-Clovis industries and whether Clovis ended precisely with the onset of the Younger Dryas cold reversal.
2026-10-22 · -13ka-megafaunal-extinction-in-the-americas · outline · established · Least sure of the relative weights of human hunting versus climate-driven fire regimes in causing the synchrony across North and South America.

2026-10-22 · person-anzick-1 · outline · established · Least sure of the child's exact age at death (estimated between 12 and 24 months) and the degree of relatedness to the older Anzick-2 cranial fragment.
2026-10-22 · polity-roman-empire · outline · established · Least sure of the polity's temporal boundaries: standard convention marks the principate from 27 BCE to 476 CE in the West, but imperial administration began de facto under Julius Caesar and survived in Byzantium until 1453 CE.
2026-10-22 · place-rome · outline · established · Least sure of the earliest urban consolidation date: the 753 BCE founding date is legendary, whereas archaeological traces of settlement go back to at least the 10th–9th centuries BCE.

2026-10-22 · 1942-fall-of-singapore · outline · established · Least sure that one pin represents a campaign: it marks the surrender site only. Prisoner numbers are not on opened pages and are not stated. "Largest British capitulation" in the importance line is an unsourced characterisation to be checked on expansion. No animation: other waypoint coordinates were not sourced.
2026-10-22 · 1975-khmer-rouge-takeover-of-cambodia · outline · established · Least sure of death totals: the 1.5-2 million figure is Kiernan as cited by the Illinois museum; other estimates run higher. 2026-10-22 · 1840-treaty-of-waitangi · outline · established · Least sure of the signatory count on the day (43-46 per the Tribunal, "first 50" per Origins). The link to the Polynesian settlement is thematic, not causal. NZHistory, the usual reference, returned 403 and was not read.
2026-10-22 · -42ka-mungo-man-cremation · outline · established · The id says Mungo Man but the cremation is Mungo Lady; the title corrects it (rename the id on expansion). Least sure of the date width (42,000 stated "at least") and of Mungo Lady's discovery year, which sources give as 1967 or 1968 (not claimed). The pin is the lake centre.
2026-10-22 · place-bagan-temple-plain · outline · established · Place pin only: no model. I found no heritage-body, museum or university page giving a height and plan size for one Bagan temple (Ananda or Shwesandaw), only encyclopedic or travel figures. Coordinates are the arithmetic mean of the seven UNESCO component points for property 1588, hence uncertainty_km 15; UNESCO gives no single site centre. Least sure: start year, a reign date standing for a multi-century process.
2026-10-22 · place-my-son-sanctuary · outline · established · Place pin only. No model: no height-plus-plan figure for a named tower was opened. Start year 301 is just the first year of the 4th century. Coordinate is the single UNESCO point, uncertainty 2 km.
2026-10-22 · place-ayutthaya-historical-park · outline · established · Place pin only. UNESCO gives the destruction only as "the 18th century"; I did not open a page with the year 1767, so none is stated. The B.E. conversion (minus 543) is a standard rule, not stated on the page. The "island at the junction of three rivers" detail in the summary is not from an opened page and could be cut. Coordinate is the single UNESCO point, uncertainty 3 km.
2026-10-22 · place-registan-samarkand · outline · established · Place pin only. No model: no sourced height for a madrasa was opened. Coordinates are from Wikidata (39.6547 N, 66.9756 E), because UNESCO's listed points for property 603 are not at the square. Only the Ulugh Beg date is confirmed; the Sher-Dor and Tilya-Kori dates are left out.
2026-10-22 · place-ellora-caves · outline · established · Place pin only. No Kailasa model: sources I opened give no dimensions for the temple itself (the ASI page has none), and web figures conflict (50 x 33 m vs 91 x 53 m). Least sure: start year, since the ASI and UNESCO ranges differ.
2026-10-22 · place-hampi · outline · established · Place pin only: no model, since no single named structure with sourced height and plan was opened (ASI names the Pampapati and Vitthala temples without sizes). Coordinate is the UNESCO point, inside a 26 sq km site; uncertainty 5 km. Least sure: 1336 is the ASI start of monument building, not a founding date.
2026-10-22 · place-konark-sun-temple · outline · established · Shape block for the jagamohana, the only part with a height and a plan size on the ASI page (30 m square, 30 m high); a pyramidal-roofed hall is approximated as a block. The sanctum height (over 68 m) is "presumably", so it is not used. Phase start 1201 is the first year of the 13th century; no date for the roof losses was found. Single ASI source for the figures.
2026-10-22 · place-karnak-temple-complex · outline · established · Place pin only: no Hypostyle Hall model. The University of Memphis pages I opened give inconsistent column heights (20 and 21 m for the nave columns on two pages) and no hall dimensions; the only plan figures (102 x 53 m) came from Guinness World Records, not an accepted source class. The coordinate is the UNESCO point for property 87 nearest Karnak, treated as Karnak; the list does not label it.
2026-10-22 · place-abu-simbel · outline · established · Place pin only: no facade model. Facade figures in sources I found conflict (30-33 m high, 35-38 m wide) and none came from a page I could open; the Ministry page has no dimensions. Coordinate is the first UNESCO point for property 88, which is the Abu Simbel location; the list does not label it.
2026-10-22 · place-merv · outline · established · Place pin only. Start year relies on a Turkmen government article that says "rose about the VII century BC" without citing evidence, while UNESCO implies older occupation; treat the start as uncertain. Coordinate is one UNESCO point for property 886 in the Sultan Kala and Erk Kala area; the list does not label it; uncertainty 5 km.
2026-10-22 · 0375-hunnic-advance-into-europe · outline · established · Dating follows modern consensus linking Ermanaric's death and the fall of the Greuthungi realm to c. 375 CE, based on Ammianus Marcellinus Book 31. Coordinates use Dnipro on the lower Dnieper as an honest regional proxy with a 250 km uncertainty radius for the Pontic steppe.
2026-10-22 · 0395-division-of-the-roman-empire · outline · established · Dating to 17 January 395 CE is fixed by contemporary sources including Ambrose's funeral oration De obitu Theodosii. The pin marks Mediolanum (Milan), the western imperial residence where Theodosius died and where the division took effect.
2026-10-22 · 0406-rhine-crossing · outline · established · Dating follows Prosper of Aquitaine's chronicle placing the crossing on the day before the Kalends of January in the consulship of Arcadius and Probus (31 December 406). Mainz (Mogontiacum) is used as the focal crossing point recorded in classical accounts including Jerome.
2026-10-22 · person-odoacer · outline · established · Birth c. 433 CE is approximate. Odoacer's thirteen-year reign (476–493) and death at Ravenna on 15 March 493 are documented in Anonymus Valesianus and Mathisen (DIR). Ravenna served as his primary royal residence and administrative capital.
2026-10-22 · person-romulus-augustulus · outline · established · Birth c. 460–465 CE is approximate. Deposition on or soon after 4 September 476 is documented in contemporary chronicles. Cassiodorus' Variae indicates Romulus was still receiving his 6,000-solidi pension in Campania in 507 or 511; his date of death remains unknown.
2026-10-22 · 0493-ostrogothic-kingdom-of-italy · outline · established · Dating to 15 March 493 CE follows contemporary chroniclers recording the banquet of Ravenna ten days after Theodoric's entry into the city. Coordinates pinpoint Ravenna, Theodoric's royal capital and burial site.

2026-10-22 · -0482-laurion-silver-and-the-themistoclean-fleet · outline · established · Least sure of whether the Athenian assembly funded 100 triremes (as in Ath. Pol. 22.7) or 200 (as reported in Herodotus 7.144), and of the manuscript reading of the archon's name (Nicomedes versus Nicodemus).
2026-10-22 · 0751-battle-of-talas · outline · established · Least sure of the exact site (the river is pinned, not the field) and of army sizes: medievalists.net calls 100,000+ per side exaggerated and estimates 30,000-50,000; Qarluq loyalty differs between accounts. 2026-10-22 · 1219-mongol-invasion-of-khwarezmia · outline · established · Attributed casualty claims (Iranica): Herat 1,600,000 (Sayfi) to 2,400,000 (Juzjani); Marv over 1,300,000 (Jovayni); Nishapur 1,747,000 (Sayfi); Iranica says these cannot be taken literally. No path animation: dates for Otrar, Samarkand, Urgench and Merv were not confirmed on opened pages. Iranica dates the Otrar caravan killing to 1218, so 1219 is the invasion, not the pretext. Samarkand fall date not confirmed; not claimed.
2026-10-22 · person-timur · outline · established · Birthplace is "near Samarkand" in World History Encyclopedia and the Shakhrisyabz area in Encyclopedia.com; Iranica calls him a native of the Kesh area. Pin is Shahrisabz with 25 km uncertainty. Encyclopedia.com does not explicitly name Otrar as the place of death; Iranica and WHE do. Campaign casualty figures were not researched. The 1219 invasion link is only contextual (same region).
2026-10-22 · polity-kushan-empire · outline · probable · Dates are century-level; the pin is Peshawar only as a symbolic centre of a realm that also centred on Bactria and Mathura (the UW essay names no single capital). The Kanishka era start is unresolved, so no dated reign is claimed. Founder and capital names were not confirmed on opened pages and are not stated.
2026-10-22 · 1960-aral-sea-desiccation · outline · established · Figures differ by year and source: USGS says the sea once covered about 68,000 sq km and is now about 10% of its 1960 size; NASA/Micklin gives 88% area and 92% volume lost (2010); UN Chronicle says only that it shrank to half its size (1999 text). The UNEP foresight brief (area halved and volume down 75% by the late 1980s, over 90% total) could not be fetched, so is not cited. No animation added: NASA attributes its figures to one expert.
2026-10-22 · person-al-khwarizmi · outline · probable · Least sure of birth place and year: MacTutor says possibly Baghdad and notes the Khwarizm epithet may refer to ancestors; Encyclopedia.com says probably Iraq. The algebra treatise date (roughly 825-830 per Encyclopedia.com) is not pinned. The pin is Baghdad, where he worked, not his birthplace.
2026-10-22 · person-ibn-sina · outline · probable · Least sure of the birth year (964, the 970s, or 980) and the village name (Afshana in the Stanford entry, Kharmaithen in MacTutor). Pin is Bukhara city with 30 km uncertainty.
2026-10-22 · 1526-first-battle-of-panipat · outline · established · Contested figures, attributed: Ibrahim's army 100,000 and 1,000 elephants (what Babur was told) versus as low as 40,000; enemy dead 15,000-16,000 (Babur's men's estimate) versus 40,000-50,000 (Agra inhabitants), per historyofwar.org. The role of artillery is disputed: historyofwar.org says archers mattered most, the Smithsonian says neither guns nor elephants were critical. Babur's 12,000 is the count at the Indus crossing per historyofwar.org.
2026-10-22 · 1757-battle-of-plassey · outline · established · Figures differ and are left out of the summary: Nawab's army about 50,000 (both); Company about 3,000 (NAM) versus roughly 2,100 plus sailors (WHE); Company dead 22 killed and 50 wounded (NAM) versus about 50 fatalities (WHE); Nawab over 500 casualties. 'Secret deal' with Mir Jafar: WHE says he promised not to fight; the bribe detail was not read on an opened page. Pin is the village of Palashi, not the exact field.
2026-10-22 · polity-maurya-empire · outline · established · Founding date varies (322, 321 or 320 BCE in WHE itself). Ashoka reign ca. 268-232 BCE per WHE and UW. The pin is modern Patna, not an excavated Pataliputra site. Capital is stated by the UW essay, not the WHE page.
2026-10-22 · 1788-first-fleet-arrival-at-sydney · outline · established · Botany Bay arrival is 18 Jan (Museums of History NSW) vs 20 Jan (Project Gutenberg Australia); port-of-call dates rest on the Gutenberg page alone; Sydney Cove is a point, not the wider Port Jackson.
2026-10-22 · place-uluru · outline · established · The 30,000-year figure is for Central Australia, not Uluru specifically; Aṉangu significance is paraphrased from Parks Australia and should be reviewed with Aṉangu-authored material.
2026-10-22 · 1280-polynesian-settlement-of-new-zealand · outline · probable · Rat introduction dates human arrival indirectly; the 1280-1300 range is from a press summary of the paper, while the abstract says about 1280.
2026-10-22 · 1893-new-zealand-womens-suffrage · outline · established · Pin is the national centroid since the sources do not name a place of signing; the "first self-governing country" claim is as stated by the sources and not independently compared.
2026-10-22 · -45ka-australian-megafauna-extinction · outline · contested · Date range is the Roberts 2001 95% interval; later work argues for later survival in places. The pin is the continental centroid; cause is contested and the summary does not decide it.
2026-10-22 · 1911-amundsen-reaches-south-pole · outline · established · Departure (19 vs 20 October) and return (25 vs 26 January) vary between accounts; the Fram Museum wording is used and years are implied by context.
2026-10-22 · 1914-endurance-expedition · outline · established · Landing date on Elephant Island is 16 April in NOVA but 15 April in some accounts; the Vahsel Bay point is a bay centroid, not the ship position, and the NOVA page is a timeline summary.
2026-10-22 · 1959-antarctic-treaty · outline · established · Entry-into-force date (23 June 1961) was seen only in a search snippet, so only the year 1961 from the Secretariat page is recorded.
2026-10-22 · 1985-ozone-hole-discovery · outline · established · The Halley station has been rebuilt on the moving Brunt Ice Shelf, so the pin is the modern station within 100 km; the Nature paper itself was not opened.
2026-10-22 · -34my-eocene-oligocene-glaciation · outline · established · Start/end follow the Hutchinson abstract (34 Ma plus 790 kyr); other records give 33.9 or 33.7 Ma for the main step. 2026-10-22 · 1857-indian-rebellion · outline · established · Death tolls are attributed and unreconciled: the museum says more than 100,000 Indians were killed; OER Project says over 50,000 sepoys and about 100,000 civilians died in suppression and revenge, plus a famine with no figure. The museum page gives both 1859 and 8 July 1858 as endpoints. Pin is Meerut but the rebellion spread across northern India; the day of the outbreak was not stated on an opened page.
2026-10-22 · 1930-salt-march · outline · established · Only three waypoints have dates on opened pages (12 March start, 18 March Borsad, 5-6 April Dandi); the route between them is not traced. Distance is 241 miles (mkgandhi.org) vs 390 km (Gujarat Tourism). Marcher count 78 (mkgandhi.org) vs 80 (Gujarat Tourism). Pin for Dandi is the memorial.
2026-10-22 · 1984-bhopal-gas-disaster · outline · established · Death tolls are attributed, not reconciled: the review says at least 3,800 immediately, with early estimates up to 10,000 (Sharma, cited therein) and 15,000-20,000 premature deaths over two decades (same citation); the Indian government reported more than half a million exposed. The Wikidata pin is the disaster location, not a surveyed plant gate.
2026-10-22 · -7000-mehrgarh-neolithic-settlement · outline · contested · Least sure of the start date: three opened sources give 8500, 7000 and 6500 BCE for the earliest phase. The id keeps the 7000 BCE label. A 2025 Scientific Reports study arguing for a later start could not be opened (access blocked) and is not cited.
2026-10-22 · 1912-scott-terra-nova-expedition · outline · established · Only the 1 November 1911 start, 17 January 1912 Pole, 17 February Evans death and 29 March last diary entry are dated on opened pages; Scott's own death date is not stated (29 March is the presumed date per the Gazette). 2026-10-22 · 1942-battle-of-midway · outline · established · Casualty figures differ between the two sources (museum: 307 US personnel; WHE: 362) so none is stated. Least sure of the counts.

Least sure of the no-British-loss claim, which rests on HISTORY alone.
2026-10-22 · person-galla-placidia · outline · probable · Birth year and marriage dates conflict between the two sources (Narbonne 414 vs Narbo 417), so marriage dates are omitted. Neither opened page places her death or burial. Pin at Ravenna is a convention. Least sure of the birth year.
2026-10-22 · place-ravenna · outline · probable · No 3D model: no opened page gives a sourced height for the Mausoleum of Galla Placidia or San Vitale. Importance kept at 2 for that reason. 2026-10-22 · polity-eastern-roman-empire · outline · established · Start date is a convention: the World History Encyclopedia page dates the empire from 330 and notes some scholars start it at 476, 565 or c. 650; this record uses the 395 division. Least sure of the start date.
2026-10-22 · place-terracotta-army · outline · established · No model: UNESCO gives the grave mound as 51.3 m high, but the World History Encyclopedia gives a 60 m mound 350 m a side, and UNESCO gives no plan dimension for the mound, so height and plan could not be sourced together. The point is the Wikidata Terracotta Army coordinate (matches UNESCO's second of two unlabelled geolocations, about 1.9 km east of the first, which sits on the grave mound; the OSM mausoleum way is at 34.3814, 109.2538). Dates: UNESCO says 'Begun in 246 BCE', so -245.
2026-10-22 · place-horyu-ji · outline · established · No model: the five-storey pagoda's height (about 32 m in search snippets) was not stated with a plan dimension on any page I could open (the World History Encyclopedia gives only the central column, over 35 m). UNESCO's XML lists two points (Horyu-ji and Hokki-ji, about 1.8 km apart); I used the one that agrees with the OSM Horyu-ji way (about 0.2 km away). The 607 founding date is from World History Encyclopedia only; UNESCO gives no founding year.
2026-10-22 · place-bulguksa-temple · outline · established · No model: Dabotap and Seokgatap heights (about 10.4 m and 8.2 m) appeared only in search snippets, and no opened page gave a plan dimension. The coordinate is the OSM Bulguksa way; UNESCO's XML point (35.7915, 129.349) is about 1.6 km east, which I take to be the grotto (not stated). The 751 and 790 dates are 'traditional' per World History Encyclopedia; UNESCO gives only the 8th century.

The 'never come under attack' claim is Nippon.com's. Coordinates: OSM way for the castle; UNESCO's XML point is rounded and about 0.9 km away.
2026-10-22 · place-temple-of-heaven · outline · established · Model is the Hall of Prayer for Good Harvests only, shape 'tower' (cylindrical hall with conical triple roof; closest allowed shape, domed_building was the alternative). Other web accounts give diameters of 30, 32 or 36 m (not opened); the two official pages opened agree on about 24 m between the outer eave columns, which is the column ring, not the marble base or eaves. The 'restored' phase starts at the fire year because the sources give no reconstruction date. Place point is the Hall (OSM way, matches UNESCO XML within 0.1 km); the whole property is 273 ha. Spotcheck cannot match the ICOMOS PDF phrases (1889 fire, fifteen years later) verbatim because its two-column layout interleaves lines; I confirmed them by reading the extracted text.
2026-10-22 · place-tiwanaku · outline · probable · Akapana modelled as stepped_pyramid. Sources disagree: EBSCO (opened, not cited) gives 15 m high and sides of about 61 m, while the ICOMOS 194 m by 194.4 m base is far larger; the cited figures are the UNESCO/ICOMOS ones. Height is a lower bound ('over 18 m') and describes the walls as originally built. The 'ruined' start year 950 is the end of monumental construction, not a dated event. Place start 400 and end 950 mix UNESCO and AIA wording.
2026-10-22 · place-chavin-de-huantar · outline · probable · Dates are contested between sources (UNESCO 1500-300 BCE vs WHE 900-200 BCE); I kept UNESCO's span and listed both. No model: no opened page gave a height with a plan dimension for a single structure (WHE gives the New Temple as 100 m long and a 50 m sunken court, no height).
2026-10-22 · place-monte-alban · outline · established · No model: no height-plus-plan dimension for a named structure was sourced. UNESCO's XML lists two unlabelled points; I used the one matching Wikidata's Monte Albán (about 0.3 km away); the other is the Oaxaca city centre. UNESCO's text says the site was inhabited 1,500 years by Olmecs, Zapotecs and Mixtecs, while the 500 BCE-850 CE span is the Zapotec capital's flourishing.
2026-10-22 · place-uxmal · outline · established · Pyramid modelled as stepped_pyramid although the plan is elliptical; the 35 m height and 85 x 50 m ellipse are from the 1996 ICOMOS evaluation (the PDF's text layer garbles the plan, I read '85 m x 50 m' from the page image). Popular accounts (not opened) give a smaller base; the ICOMOS value is the only one cited. The 'ruined' phase from 1200 is a loose reading: ICOMOS says the town was abandoned around 1200 but also that inhabitants returned and it was a pilgrimage place until the Spanish. Materials: WHE says the buildings of Uxmal are limestone.
2026-10-22 · place-kilwa-kisiwani · outline · established · No model: no height-plus-plan dimension for the Great Mosque or Husuni Kubwa was found on a page I could open (Archnet pages rate-limited). The point is UNESCO's first geolocation (the second, -9.049, is Songo Mnara); the island spans several km, so uncertainty is 3 km. Occupation ran 9th-19th century per UNESCO; the record's span is the 13th-16th century trading peak.
2026-10-22 · place-temple-of-heaven · outline · established · Model is the Hall of Prayer for Good Harvests only, shape 'tower' (cylindrical hall with conical triple roof; closest allowed shape, domed_building was the alternative). Other web accounts give diameters of 30, 32 or 36 m (not opened); the two official pages opened agree on about 24 m between the outer eave columns, which is the column ring, not the marble base or eaves. The 'restored' phase starts at the fire year because the sources give no reconstruction date. Place point is the Hall (OSM way, matches UNESCO XML within 0.1 km); the whole property is 273 ha. Spotcheck cannot match the ICOMOS PDF phrases (1889 fire, fifteen years later) verbatim because its two-column layout interleaves lines; I confirmed them by reading the extracted text.

2026-10-22 · 1517-protestant-reformation · outline · probable · Posting on the door is only "the traditional account" (WHE). The Book of Concord page says the theses were soon printed and spread across Europe, but no opened page ties print to the Reformation as a cause, so the press link is plain "related". End set equal to start.
2026-10-22 · 1462-sack-of-mainz · outline · established · Date is on two pages. Sources differ on the end of the feud (Zeilsheim, 5 vs 12 October 1463), so that is left out. The printing-office fire and Gutenberg losses rest on one page (Encyclopedia.com). Pin is the city node.
2026-10-22 · 1457-mainz-psalter · outline · established · The VÖB page places the printers in Mainz; the Rylands page does not name a place. Wording of the "firsts" differs: VÖB says the colophon names title, printer and date for the first time; Rylands says first to name printers. Both agree on ten surviving copies.
2026-10-22 · place-mainz · outline · established · WHE hedges the founder (Drusus "is said to have" founded it) and the 12 BCE date is "official". Gutenberg and the archbishopric are covered in linked records, not asserted here. Pin is the city node.
2026-10-22 · 1833-factory-act · outline · established · The two pages disagree on the daily hours cap for 9-13-year-olds (EBSCO: eight; Encyclopedia.com: nine), so no hours figure is stated. Encyclopedia.com limits schooling to children under 13, EBSCO to ages 9-13; the summary says only "young workers". Pin is London via an arbitrary central point (Palace of Westminster node); the Act applied nationally. Royal assent date was not found on an opened page.
2026-10-22 · 1925-06-17-geneva-gas-protocol · outline · established · The year-range end is the entry into force. The pages opened give no parties or limits, so none are stated. The signing is the main event; pin is the city of Geneva.
2026-10-22 · polity-sparta · outline · established · Founding date differs between pages (c. 900 BCE vs 9th century). End 195 BCE rests on Encyclopedia.com only. Pin is the modern town of Sparta, near the ruins. WHE puts the end of the war in 404 BCE; Encyclopedia.com 404 BCE with one source giving 403.
2026-10-22 · -0323-death-of-alexander-the-great · outline · established · The day is 10 June on both main pages but WHE metadata allows 11 June. WHE says "palace" without naming it. Pin is the OSM Babylon site. Cause of death is unresolved (the NIH paper proposes West Nile encephalitis; WHE says modern historians generally accept natural causes).
2026-10-22 · place-saqqara-step-pyramid · outline · established · Least sure of the absolute date: the Ministry's c. 2667-2648 BC is one of several chronologies. No model written: the University of Memphis gives about 60 m and six levels but no base dimensions, and I found no museum, heritage-body or scholarly page with a sourced plan; the Poisson paper on arXiv is speculative geometry and I did not rely on it.
2026-10-22 · place-horyu-ji · outline · established · No model: the five-storey pagoda's height (about 32 m in search snippets) was not stated with a plan dimension on any page I could open (the World History Encyclopedia gives only the central column, over 35 m). UNESCO's XML lists two points (Horyu-ji and Hokki-ji, about 1.8 km apart); I used the one that agrees with the OSM Horyu-ji way (about 0.2 km away). The 607 founding date is from World History Encyclopedia only; UNESCO gives no founding year.

UNESCO's XML lists two points (Horyu-ji and Hokki-ji, about 1.8 km apart); I used the one that agrees with the OSM Horyu-ji way (about 0.2 km away). 2026-10-22 · person-alexander-the-great · outline · established · Least sure of the waypoint pins: Livius gives no coordinates, so Granicus is the midpoint of the Biga Çayı, Issus is Payas (the Pinarus), Gaugamela is computed 75 km northwest of Irbil, Hydaspes is Jhelum city; each is approximate. Birth and death day differ by source.

2026-10-22 · polity-roman-republic · outline · established · Least sure of the start year: World History Encyclopedia gives 509 BCE in its data but 510 BCE in the text, and Rome.net calls the 509 events traditional. Second source is a general site, not scholarly.

Vietnamese death estimates range from under 1 million to over 3 million across sources; only the BMJ-cited figures were read on an opened page.
2026-10-22 · -2300my-huronian-glaciation · outline · established · The pin (Cobalt) is one of five study areas named on the Lakehead page, not a formal type locality; the Waterloo museum places the rock at Elliot Lake. Opened pages give no tighter age than 2.2-2.45 Ga; many sources say about 2.3 Ga but I did not open one that does. Relation to the Great Oxidation Event left as a plain link.
2026-10-22 · -717my-sturtian-snowball-earth · outline · established · Pin is the Ogilvie Mountains, where the onset is dated, with a planet-scale uncertainty because the glaciation was global. Paleo position from PALEOMAP because MERDITH2021 has no plate at the point. Onset age varies by study (about 717 Ma; Franklin about 719.9-718.6 Ma).
2026-10-22 · -1000my-assembly-of-rodinia · outline · probable · Least sure: the exact date (1090-980 Ma is the Grenvillian orogeny, not a dated assembly end) and the pin, which is a village named for the Grenville Province, not a formal locality. The Australian page is a weak secondary source (class other). Rodinia as a single continent is not firmly evidenced: the same page says there is little definite evidence for it.
2026-10-22 · -445my-late-ordovician-mass-extinction · outline · established · Least sure: the date. Opened pages date only the boundary (443 +/-1.5 Ma), not the extinction; the id's 445 Ma is not on an opened page. Brachiopod percentages are one group's losses, not the overall extinction magnitude. Pin is the GSSP section, chosen as a sourced key section.
2026-10-22 · -372my-late-devonian-extinction · outline · established · Pin is Bad Wildungen as a proxy for the Steinbruch Schmidt quarry, which Percival et al. place near there; the quarry has no coordinates on opened pages. No opened page gives a percentage of species lost, so the summary omits one. The id says 372 Ma; the sourced boundary age is 371.87 Ma.
2026-10-22 · species-tiktaalik-roseae · outline · established · Both sources are news outlets; no museum or journal page opened. Pin is the island centroid, not the find locality, hence 400 km uncertainty. Age figures differ between sources (375 to about 385 Ma); I used the two that I opened.
2026-10-22 · -310my-carboniferous-coal-forests · outline · established · The date is the whole Carboniferous because no opened page gives 310 Ma. The pin (Illinois) is a sourced locality for the last lycopsid swamps, not the centre of the coal forests; hence 5000 km uncertainty. Paleo age 329 Ma is my midpoint choice.
2026-10-22 · species-pakicetus · outline · established · Dates differ (49 vs 48 Ma); the PNAS page says Pakicetus is younger than earlier thought and that Himalayacetus (about 53.5 Ma) is older, so Pakicetus is not the earliest whale relative. Pin is Chorlakki per the PNAS page; the NYIT page gives only provinces.
2026-10-22 · species-sahelanthropus-tchadensis · outline · established · Coordinates come from the PNAS abstract (16 15 N, 17 29 E, whole degrees-minutes), hence 10 km uncertainty. Age sources differ in method: cosmogenic (about 7.04 Ma) vs faunal (6-7 Ma). Hominin status is debated and the summary says so.
2026-10-22 · place-laetoli-footprints · outline · established · Pin is derived: the eLife paper puts Laetoli about 50 km south of Olduvai Gorge, and I offset the OSM Olduvai point by that distance, hence 40 km uncertainty. No opened page gives Laetoli coordinates. Discovery year conflicts: Archaeology and the eLife Insight say 1976 (excavated by 1978); the eLife paper says discovered in 1978. The Site G attribution to A. afarensis is called controversial in the paper.
2026-10-22 · place-brihadisvara-temple-thanjavur · outline · established · Shape stepped_pyramid for the 13-tier vimana. HEIGHT IS CONFLICTED: the ICOMOS evaluation (250bis) says the vimana is '65 m tall' and 'square in plan (30 x 30 m)'; UNESCO's property page says 'a height of 59.82 meters over the ground'. The model uses the ICOMOS pair, which comes from one page; the 30 m may describe a larger square than the tower's base proper. worldhistory.org (not used) says 63 m. Dates 1003-1010 are ICOMOS's; UNESCO's regnal-year dates match.
2026-10-22 · place-qutb-minar · outline · established · Shape tower (a tapering fluted shaft). Height 72.5 m and diameters 14.32 m (base) and 2.75 m (top) are as UNESCO gives them; Delhi Tourism repeats them. Dates from the Delhi Tourism page; UNESCO gives only early 13th century. Coordinates are the OpenStreetMap way for the tower; UNESCO's point for the whole property lies about 150 m away. Least sure of completion: the sources opened give the storeys but not a finish year.
2026-10-22 · place-great-mosque-of-samarra · outline · established · Shape tower for the Malwiya spiral minaret (the sources opened do not describe its profile beyond 'five circular layers rise forming a huge spiral tower'). ICOMOS: square base sides 32 m, tower 54 m high; a travel caption seen in search results gave 52 m and 33 m and was not opened. The 849-852 dates cover the mosque; ICOMOS does not date the minaret separately. UNESCO's property point (34.08, 43.92) is an area centre kilometres away; OSM objects were used, so the OSM objects and Wikidata were the coordinate sources.
2026-10-22 · place-minaret-of-jam · outline · established · Shape tower. ICOMOS: 65 m high from a 9 m diameter octagonal base, four tapering cylindrical shafts, fired brick; UNESCO repeats 65 m. 1194 is the inscription date reported by ICOMOS; the 1192 victory is ICOMOS's 'likely' reading. The 9 m is the octagonal base's diameter, not the shaft's.
2026-10-22 · place-choga-zanbil · outline · established · Shape ziggurat. The 105.2 m side and about 53 m height are UNESCO's figures for the ziggurat as originally built; the 53 m is an estimate, since UNESCO says the ruin now stands only 24.75 m high. The ICOMOS evaluation (poorly OCRed) gives the c. 1250 BC foundation and reign dates of 1265-1245 BC for Untash-Napirisha, which differ from UNESCO's 1275-1240.
2026-10-22 · place-temple-of-horus-edfu · outline · established · Shape block (a pylon-fronted temple; no colonnade plan was sourced). Yale gives the pylon towers as 'approximately 36m in height' and the temple as 'little over 140m long', area about 7000 m2; no width was found. A Heidelberg study seen only in search snippets gave 32.5 m and ~70 m; it was not opened. Dates: Yale says Ptolemy III began in 237 BCE and Ptolemy VIII inaugurated it in 142 BCE; the main door was fitted in 57 BCE.
2026-10-22 · place-koutoubia-mosque · outline · established · Outline only; no model written. The 77 m minaret height is in the UNESCO and ICOMOS texts, but no plan dimension for the minaret was found on a page that could be opened: a search snippet from Archnet gave 'almost 13 m' per side, but Archnet blocked the fetch (HTTP 429 and a bot check), and a travel listing's 12.8 m was not on the page when opened. The ICOMOS text is French with OCR errors; the 1147-1158 span is read from it and the spotcheck regex matches '1147-1158'. The sources opened do not give a finish date for the minaret.
2026-10-22 · place-suleymaniye-mosque · outline · established · Outline only; no model written. Sources seen only in search snippets gave 26.2, 27.25 and 27.5 m for the diameter and 49-53 m for the height, so the dome diameter is disputed. The MIT Dome record was not reachable.
2026-10-22 · place-bayon-temple · outline · established · Outline only; no model written: no height with a plan dimension could be sourced from a UNESCO, heritage-body, museum, university or scholarly page. The UNESCO list page for Angkor and the ICOMOS evaluation do not date the Bayon itself; the start year is a lower bound set by the 1177 sack, and the date is the weakest part of this record. 2026-10-22 · place-fogong-temple-pagoda · outline · established · Shape tower.
