2026-10-22 · -2580ka-onset-of-northern-hemisphere-glaciation · outline · established · Least sure: which date to call the onset (2.7 vs 2.58 Ma) and the placeholder location for a planet-wide process. Paleo position pending (GPlates down).
2026-10-22 · -25my-formation-of-the-east-african-rift · outline · probable · Least sure: the start date (other accounts give 30-35 Ma for Afar). Point is only the Afar origin of a system thousands of km long. Paleo position pending (GPlates down).
2026-10-22 · -15ka-missoula-floods · outline · established · Least sure: dates (the two sources overlap only at 15,500-15,000) and flood count. Point is the lake basin; floods reached far beyond.
2026-10-22 · -3000-end-of-the-african-humid-period · outline · probable · Least sure: timing (6,000 vs 5,000 years ago) and abruptness (abrupt per phys.org, patchier per Oceanbites). Point is a Sahara centroid; the shift was regional.
2026-10-22 · -6200-8-2-kiloyear-event · outline · probable · Least sure: duration (the EBSCO page itself gives 2,000-4,000 years in one place and 150-200 years in another; I used the latter) and cause (NASA abstract says drainage of Agassiz may have caused it). Location is a planet-scale reference point.
2026-10-22 · 1815-eruption-of-mount-tambora · outline · established · Least sure: death toll (11,000 to over 100,000 depending on source and on direct versus later famine deaths) and the exact eruption dates (5-15 April across sources).
2026-10-22 · place-el-mirador · outline · probable · Shape stepped_pyramid. Least sure of the plan: Guinness gives 500 x 300 m for the footprint but does not say whether that is the pyramid with its platform; it is the only page with a plan size. Height differs between Guinness (72 m) and Yale (70 m); I used 72. Guinness classed reference. Coordinates are Wikidata P625 for the pyramid; no publisher page opened gives coordinates.
2026-10-22 · place-lighthouse-of-alexandria · outline · contested · Shape tower; the real form was three stages, so a plain tower is an approximation. Confidence contested: height estimates run 100-200 m across pages (World History Encyclopedia 100-140; Muslim Heritage 103-118 or 140-183); I used the 122 m of the 1195 measurement from the Chicago page. The 30 x 30 m base is a modern scholar (McKenzie) quoted on Muslim Heritage, a non-institutional site (class other). Destruction date differs by page (1303 damage, 1330s, 1365, 1375); I used 1375 from Chicago. Coordinates are the Qaitbay Citadel, which the Chicago page says stands on the Pharos foundation.
2026-10-22 · place-imam-mosque-isfahan · outline · established · Shape domed_building. Least sure of: (1) the Imam/Shah alias is not stated on a page I opened; I treated the OSM-named Masjed-e Jame Abbasi at Isfahan as the same mosque, so verify. (2) Dome height 52 m (SAHC) vs 53 m (Studio MatrX); the 22.65 m is a structural span, not necessarily the outer diameter. SAHC is a university-network blog post (class reference); no overall plan size found.
2026-10-22 · place-three-gorges-dam · outline · established · Shape wall with model.linear true as instructed. Least sure of: start year (USGS 1992 vs WorldAtlas 1994), height (181 m USGS vs 607 ft WorldAtlas) and whether 2,335 m is the crest length; neither page says crest. WorldAtlas is class other; USGS is the only government source. Not found: crest width.
2026-10-22 · place-sydney-opera-house · outline · established · Shape block (a stand-in for the three groups of shells). Least sure of: width (120 m Permasteelisa, 118 m Archiweb, 130 m on a travel page) and whether 67 m is above sea level or above the podium (Archiweb says above sea level). Permasteelisa is a facade contractor (class other). UNESCO, Heritage NSW and DCCEEW pages were blocked or timed out, so no heritage-body page confirms the dimensions.
2026-10-22 · place-aswan-high-dam · outline · established · Shape wall with model.linear true as instructed. Egypt's State Information Service page (sis.gov.eg) gave a certificate error, so it is not used; the figures here are from EBSCO (reference) and NASA (government, no height). EBSCO says 'long' without saying it is the crest; I labelled it length (crest) because the page gives crest width right after. NASA says 3,830 m.
2026-10-22 · place-jetavanaramaya · outline · probable · Shape stupa. Least sure of: (1) all three publishers are non-institutional (Guinness classed reference); no government, museum or university page opened, and the Sunday Observer, CNN and Lakpura pages were blocked. (2) No diameter on any opened page: the platform side of 177 m is used as the plan size; the circumference of 366 m (Amazing Lanka) is not used. (3) The 12th-century phase start 1101 is a placeholder for "12th century". (4) Reign dates differ: 273-301 vs 276-303.
2026-10-22 · place-burj-khalifa · outline · established · Outline only, no model: no plan dimension (base, wing width, footprint) found on an opened page; the ICC page gives only a total floor area of 280,000 square metres. Height 828 m is on Guinness and the ICC page. Guinness is class reference. Skyscrapercenter, Emaar and burjkhalifa.ae pages were blocked (403).
2026-10-22 · place-sigiriya · outline · established · Outline only, no model: height 180 m is on both pages (NASA "as high as 180 meters", Central Province "approximately 180 metres"), but no plan dimension was found (the 140 x 40 m figure on the Central Province page is the painted rock face). UNESCO page was blocked (403). Palace dates are the reign of Kashyapa; the pages do not give the build years.
2026-10-22 · place-ban-chiang · outline · established · Coordinates converted from the DMS given by UNESCO (N17 24 27.979 E103 14 15.32). UNESCO says first discovered 1966; other pages say 1957, so discovery date is omitted. Penn says nearly 4,000 years ago, UNESCO 1495 BC; kept UNESCO range.
2026-10-22 · place-banaue-rice-terraces · outline · contested · Coordinates are the Banaue municipality centre, not a terrace; the property is five clusters in four municipalities, so uncertainty is 30 km. Age is contested; the 2018 article says IAP excavations were concentrated in Kiangan.
2026-10-22 · 1942-bataan-death-march · outline · established · No source opened gives dated intermediate stops, so no path animation. Tolls: Army 2,500 Filipino + 500 American on the march, 26,000 Filipino + 1,500 American at Camp O'Donnell; History.com estimate 17,000 during and after, and VA figures of 650 American and 16,500 Filipino. Least sure: exact start day of the march.
2026-10-22 · 1896-philippine-revolution · outline · established · Date and place of the first cry are disputed in the sources (23 vs 26 August; Pugad Lawin vs Balintawak); the pin uses the Balintawak barangay as a central point. Least sure: end of revolution (1898 from LoC guide span only).
2026-10-22 · 1945-august-revolution-vietnam · outline · established · Two sources are Vietnamese outlets (state news agency and a media company); the Library of Congress study is the independent anchor. Dates conflict on Bao Dai abdication (25 vs 30 August) and on the start (16-19 August); the summary states both abdication dates.
2026-10-22 · polity-majapahit · outline · established · No opened page gives 1293 as the founding year, so start is 1292 with +3 years. UNESCO tentative list gives the capital span as 13th-15th century in one place and says the kingdom was 14th to 16th century in another; the LoC dates are used. The LoC study says Melaka competition hastened Majapahit disintegration.
2026-10-22 · polity-funan-and-oc-eo · outline · probable · Point is Oc Eo; the kingdom was larger and its capital sites are uncertain (60 km uncertainty, Angkor Borei is the later capital per EBSCO). Dates are century-level and differ slightly by source.
2026-10-22 · 1991-eruption-of-mount-pinatubo · outline · established · Sources conflict: ash cloud 35 km (USGS fact sheet, Earth magazine) vs 40 km (USGS news); deaths 300-400 (Earth magazine) vs more than 840 from roof collapses alone (USGS news). Summary reports both.
2026-10-22 · 1965-indonesian-mass-killings · outline · established · Toll is attributed, not asserted: ~500,000 (Princeton UP blurb, Chandra), 600,000-1 million (National Commission on Human Rights via NPR, 2012), about 2 million (a former general, per NPR). Pin is Java as a coarse locator; killings were archipelago-wide. Least sure: exact start date of the purge.
2026-10-22 · person-jose-rizal · outline · established · Birth coordinates are the Calamba city centre (Wikidata). Noli Me Tangere year omitted: sources open on the page differ or leave it unclear. Execution site is Luneta per the LoC study, "Manila" per Biography.com.
2026-10-22 · place-kakadu-nawarla-gabarnmang · outline · established · Coordinates rest on Wikidata alone (Q5515272), which sources them from outside this atlas; another database listing (AMSD) gave a point about 100 km away, so uncertainty is set at 15 km. The Landscape Australia article is co-authored by Margaret Katherine, a senior Elder of the Buyhmi clan and traditional owner, with the excavation team. The 45,180 cal BP figure is the earliest dated stone-artefact association; the article says people began camping about 50,000 years ago.
2026-10-22 · 1854-eureka-stockade · outline · established · Death toll is not stated on either opened page and is left out; other sources give differing totals. "A few hundred" defenders is the Reform League wording only. Coordinates are Wikidata's point for the stockade, which the City of Ballarat page places at the Eureka Stockade site; accuracy of the exact stockade line is not asserted.
2026-10-22 · 1992-mabo-decision · outline · established · Pin is on Mer, the land at issue; the judgment itself was delivered by the High Court of Australia (location not pinned). Eddie Mabo is described by the Museum of Australian Democracy as having led the legal action for the Meriam people. The 1993 Act is named by the MoAD and ANU pages.
2026-10-22 · 1976-hokulea-voyage-to-tahiti · outline · established · Honolua point is Wikidata's "area in West Maui", not the bay mouth; Mataiva is the atoll centre. The crowd figure of more than 17,000 is given on the Finney page as Nainoa Thompson's statement. Other sources give 3 June for arrival; both opened pages say 4 June. Piailug is described by the resolution as a traditional master navigator from Satawal, Micronesia, chosen because no present-day Polynesians knew the ancient methods.
2026-10-22 · 1893-overthrow-of-the-hawaiian-kingdom · outline · established · The 2004 House resolution describes the event as an illegal conspiracy; the summary states only the dated actions. The pin is ʻIolani Palace; the resolution places US forces near the government buildings and the Palace, and the pages do not name the exact building where the Committee of Safety acted. Native Hawaiian source used: Kamehameha Schools article quoting the Queen's own book.
2026-10-22 · polity-tui-tonga-empire · outline · probable · Start date is oral tradition (950 CE) from Tonga's nomination text; the capital sequence (Heketā, then Lapaha around the 13th century) is from the same nomination and sources elsewhere disagree on details. Samoa and the empire's decline are not covered on the opened pages and are left out. The nomination text was prepared for the Tonga National Commission for UNESCO. Wikidata's Lapaha point may be a village centre.
2026-10-22 · 1942-kokoda-track-campaign · outline · established · Casualty figures differ between the opened pages (ANZAC Day committee: Australian casualties over 6000 and about 13,000 of 20,000 Japanese lost; these span the wider Papuan campaign) and are omitted. Isurava date is when the 2/14th began arriving, not the battle date (attacks began 26 August per other sources; the battle date was not checked). Efogi point is OSM's Efogi 1 node. The Japanese withdrawal reason (supply and fear of an American attack on Buna) is from the ANZAC Day committee page only.
2026-10-22 · 1642-tasman-voyage · outline · established · Sources disagree on the Murderers Bay losses: Princeton says four of Tasman's men were killed; New Zealand Geographic says three were killed and a fourth mortally injured. The summary avoids a number. Tonga and Fiji are named by New Zealand Geographic (Tonga and Fiji) and Princeton (Fiji) without dates, so they have no waypoints. Hobart is only the page's "vicinity" pointer, and the Golden Bay waypoint is where land was first met on 13 December, not the clash date. Māori are named as in the source ("local Māori"); the incident is described from Dutch records.
2026-10-22 · 1768-cook-endeavour-voyage · outline · established · Dates disagree across sources: New Zealand was sighted on 6 October (British Empire site) or 7 October (DOC); the return date is 12 June (British Empire) or July 1771 (Ruderman), so no return waypoint is drawn. Botany Bay has no day-level date on opened pages (the National Library of Australia gives 19 April 1770 for the first sighting of the coast). Endeavour River repairs and Batavia are described in the sources without dates and have no waypoints. The Poverty Bay stop describes the deaths of nine Māori at the landing (DOC); the Aboriginal people at Botany Bay are not described on opened pages.
2026-10-22 · 1769-tupaia-chart · outline · probable · Pin marks Tupaia's home island, not the place of drawing (aboard ship, location unrecorded on opened pages). The reading of the chart is the Eckstein and Schwarz interpretation named by the University of Potsdam page; the identity of many islands is described there as a long-standing riddle. The island count of the chart differs across sources (not stated here). Tupaia's spelling and Raʻiātea follow the sources.
2026-10-22 · place-haamonga-a-maui-trilithon · outline · probable · Shape block. Height sources disagree: World History Commons 5.2 m (used with its length and width), UNESCO 6 m. World History Commons is an unsigned overview and its figures may derive from other references. Builder and date are traditional ('it is said'). Least sure of the 1200-1300 construction span.
2026-10-22 · place-sydney-harbour-bridge · outline · established · Linear model (shape wall as the nearest path form). Length 1,163 m is the Engineers Australia figure with approaches; ASCE says '2 3/4 miles' for the bridge and approaches, a different total. Heights are to the arch top above water. Pin is the OSM relation centre, which agrees with ASCE's DMS coordinates.
2026-10-22 · place-itchan-kala-khiva · outline · probable · Modelled structure is Kalta Minor (tower, 29 m, 14.5 m diameter); the record's date range is for Itchan Kala as a whole. Kalta Minor figures vary by site (a guide site gave 26-29 m height and 14.2-14.5 m diameter) and the start year (1851, 1852, 1853); I could open only uzbekistan.travel. Material 'brick' is for the walls per UNESCO. Least sure of the 901 start.
2026-10-22 · place-mausoleum-of-khoja-ahmed-yasawi · outline · established · Shape domed_building. Height 38.7 m (UNESCO and ICOMOS); plan dimension is the main dome diameter 18.2 m because neither page gave the building's overall length and width. 'Intact 1405-1945' bridges a period with no dated alterations on the pages opened (restoration 'since 1945' is ICOMOS wording). Pin is the OSM way centre and matches UNESCO's N43 17 51.7 E68 16 15.8.
2026-10-22 · place-sukhothai-historical-park · outline · probable · No model written: no page I opened (UNESCO, ICOMOS, a Thai library page, travel sites) gave a height for a named structure from an acceptable publisher; Wat Mahathat's chedi is described as having a 20 m square base on a Muang Boran page with no height. Pin is the OSM park centroid; UNESCO's page gave no coordinates for this property. Dates: UNESCO's two statements differ (14th vs 15th century).
2026-10-22 · place-hue-imperial-citadel · outline · established · Shape wall (square enceinte, side 2,235 m, height 6.6 m); the ICOMOS PDF is OCR and prints 'm' as 'rn', which I read as metres. The 'originally 6.6 m' height is the Capital City wall as built. Pin is OSM's Hoang Thanh (Imperial City) node inside the Inner City, within 1 km of the enclosure's centre but I did not open a coordinate for the citadel centre. 'Intact 1832-1885' bridges an undated period.
2026-10-22 · place-petronas-towers · outline · probable · No model written: pages opened (Skyscraper Museum, Thornton Tomasetti, the official site) give 452 m and 88 floors but no plan dimension (only floor area, a 58.4 m skybridge, and an eight-pointed-star plan); CTBUH, Wiley and ScienceDirect pages were blocked. Completion is 1998 on two pages but 1997 on Pelli Clarke Pelli's page; Pelli Clarke Pelli and the official site were opened but only the latter is cited.
2026-10-22 · place-roman-leptis-magna · outline · probable · No model written: no page I opened gave a height for the theatre, amphitheatre or any other single structure (Livius gives only the amphitheatre arena as 57 x 47 m; theatrum.de gave cavea 87.6 m but is a database I did not cite). The date span is a constructed pin from Livius and UNESCO, not a sourced founding date. Pin uses OSM's site point; UNESCO lists N32 38 17.988 E14 17 35.016 for the property.
2026-10-22 · place-zaghouan-carthage-aqueduct · outline · probable · Linear (shape wall as the nearest path form). 38 m is the figure npj Heritage Science gives for the Miliane valley; a Roman-aqueduct database snippet I did not open gives 33.65 m and 20 m, and the Carthage and Hydria pages open give no heights. Length 132 km includes branches; the main conduit is just over 90 km. Phase gaps (533-698, 200-439) are not sourced as intact. Pin is OSM's Temple of Waters node at the Zaghouan end.
2026-10-22 · place-ctesiphon-taq-kisra · outline · contested · Shape block (facade with arched iwan). Iranica gives the facade 35 m high and the iwan 25.5 m wide; Amusing Planet gives a 37 m high, 26 m wide arch. These differ (they may measure different points) and I used Iranica first. Date is contested (Shapur I vs 6th century), so the 241-600 construction span is wide. The 600-1888 intact phase is a bridge; the Iranica article is dated 2017 and does not say who restored what after 1975. Pin is an OSM node for the iwan.
2026-10-22 · place-itchan-kala-khiva · outline · probable · Modelled structure is Kalta Minor (tower, 29 m, 14.5 m diameter); the record's date range is for Itchan Kala as a whole. Kalta Minor figures vary by site (a guide site gave 26-29 m height and 14.2-14.5 m diameter) and the start year (1851, 1852, 1853); I could open only uzbekistan.travel. Material 'brick' is for the walls per UNESCO. Least sure of the 901 start. Walled town measurements (kept out of the minaret model): Itchan Kala wall height 10 m (src-unesco-543); Itchan Kala length 650 m (src-unesco-543); Itchan Kala width 400 m (src-unesco-543).
2026-10-22 · place-gonur-tepe · outline · established · Least sure: the Vassar page is a course page; it lists 38°12′50″N 62°02′15″E, consistent with Wikidata. The Antiquity page is the main scholarly source. Mound area (22 hectares) is from Vassar only. Alternative dating of the urban phase was not read on an opened page.
2026-10-22 · -3500-botai-horse-domestication · outline · probable · Least sure: the pin uses the Wikidata coordinates of the village Botai; the opened pages say only northern Kazakhstan, steppe east of the Urals, so uncertainty is 100 km. The claim rests on one research programme (Outram et al., Science 2009) and the pages report it as a finding, not a consensus.
2026-10-22 · -2000-indo-iranian-migrations · outline · contested · Contested. Views on opened pages: (1) Narasimhan et al. 2019 read steppe-derived ancestry in Central and South Asia as a likely conduit for Indo-European languages and found the main BMAC population carried none; (2) Scientific Reports 2022 says the relation between modern Indo-Iranian speakers and ancient southern Central Asian populations remains unclear and shows continuity from the Iron Age. Archaeological disputes (Andronovo versus BMAC) were seen only in search snippets and are not recorded. The pin is a reference site, not an origin.
2026-10-22 · place-pazyryk-kurgans-and-issyk-gold-man · outline · probable · Least sure: this combined record pins only Pazyryk (Wikidata coordinates, 15 km); the Issyk kurgan has no coordinate in the record. The Hermitage page certificate failed in the fetch tool and was read with curl. Dates of the Issyk burial differ between sources (4th–5th and 4th–3rd centuries BCE) and are not used as the record span. Voices on Central Asia is a secondary outlet.
2026-10-22 · 0629-xuanzang-journey-to-india · outline · probable · Least sure: intermediate dates. Turfan and Samarkand appear on opened pages without years, so they are not waypoints. 632 (Bamiyan) and 637 (Nalanda) come from one non-scholarly timeline and conflict with other accounts (Encyclopedia.com: arrival in India 633). Only the 629 start and 645 return are confirmed by two reference sources. Bamiyan pin is the town, not the Buddha niches.
2026-10-22 · 1949-semipalatinsk-first-soviet-nuclear-test · outline · established · Least sure: the pin is the centre of the test site, not the RDS-1 ground zero, hence 30 km; the first-test location within the 18,000 km2 site was not read. Test counts differ slightly between pages (456 total in both, but 116 versus 111 atmospheric), so only the total is used. Yield 22 kilotons is from the KAS timeline.
2026-10-22 · 1465-kazakh-khanate · outline · probable · Least sure: the date (1465 versus 1465/66; other accounts place the break with Abulkhair in the 1450s) and the pin, which is Taraz as the centre of the Chu–Talas region named by Euronews, with 150 km uncertainty. e-history.kz text is garbled in places and is government-run. No relation links were added because none was stated on the opened pages.
2026-10-22 · -0700-scythians-saka-steppe-culture · outline · established · Least sure: the Wikidata pin is the Arzhan site generally, not necessarily the Arzhan-2 kurgan, so 10 km. The span is a conservative window from two opened scholarly pages; earlier Arzhan-1 dates and later Scythian phases were seen only in search snippets. Saka terminology (Saka versus Scythian) is not explained on an opened page.
2026-10-22 · place-bukhara-historic-centre · outline · established · Least sure: the span mixes an 892 historical start with a 1993 inscription end; the ICOMOS text is an OCR-quality PDF. Minaret dates differ (UNESCO 11th century, ICOMOS 12th century) so no minaret date is given. UNESCO coordinates N39 46 28.992 E64 25 42.996 match Wikidata. The Mongol sack (1220) and Timur (1370) are on both pages; the link to the Mongol invasion record is by event, not by a stated page claim about that record.
2026-10-22 · 1325-ibn-battuta-travels · outline · probable · Least sure: waypoint years are taken from section headings of the ORIAS teaching site (not a critical edition) and are single years; Cairo and Mecca share 1326. Pages seen only in search results give 1332–1333 for Delhi and mention scholarly doubt about parts of his China account; those were not opened. Mecca and Cairo are city pins from Wikidata. No related records were linked because none was clearly stated.
2026-10-22 · 1947-kon-tiki-expedition · outline · established · Least sure: the Raroia point is the island, not the reef. HISTORY gives 4,300 miles; the museum page gives no distance, so no distance is used. Neither page names intermediate stops, so the path has two waypoints.
2026-10-22 · 1858-first-transatlantic-telegraph-cable · outline · established · Least sure: the 4 vs 5 August landing at Newfoundland (sources differ), and the failure date (sources say only within a few weeks). IEEE and Scientific American give different mid-Atlantic splice coordinates, so none is used.
2026-10-22 · 1939-battle-of-the-atlantic · outline · established · Least sure: loss figures differ by source (Canadian War Museum: more than 3,000 Allied ships; Battle of the Atlantic Story: 3,500+ ships), so only the Canadian War Museum figure is used. The point is the middle of the North Atlantic, not a single site. D-Day link is only thematic.
2026-10-22 · 1820-bellingshausen-sights-antarctica · outline · probable · Least sure: priority is disputed. The British Antarctic Territory dates Bransfield's sighting of land on the Antarctic Peninsula to 30 January 1820. The coordinates are the position the Lynceans page gives for 28 January (69°21'28" S, 2°14'50" W), converted to decimal degrees, from one source only. The sighting day (27 vs 28) differs by source.
2026-10-22 · 1911-mawson-australasian-antarctic-expedition · outline · established · Least sure: the exact end date (sources give only 1914) and the Cape Denison landing day (early January 1912). The Macquarie Island party is not a waypoint because no opened page dates its landing. The Ninnis and Mertz deaths on the Far Eastern sledging journey have no sourced location.
2026-10-22 · 1789-bounty-mutiny · outline · established · Least sure: exact mutiny location (sources say near Tofua or near Tonga), launch length (23 vs 24 feet), and Bligh's Tahiti departure (4 vs 5 April 1789), which is not used. The path joins Bligh's launch and Christian's ship as two separate journeys.
2026-10-22 · 1831-beagle-voyage · outline · established · Least sure: the departure day (27 December per Darwin Online and the museum; 10 December per CITiZAN). Where Darwin Online gives a date range for a port, the waypoint year is its first day. Darwin Online has no Cape Town entry, only the Cape of Good Hope. No Darwin or Galápagos record exists yet to link.
2026-10-22 · 1520-strait-of-magellan-passage · outline · established · Least sure: the point is the centre of the strait, not a surveyed track, hence the large uncertainty. Duration differs by source (36 days, 27 days or about five weeks). This record covers only the strait passage; the wider voyage is in 1519-magellan-elcano-circumnavigation.
2026-10-22 · 1521-magellan-arrival-in-the-philippines · outline · established · Least sure: the arrival date (16 vs 17 March) and Magellan's death date (27 vs 28 April); the Cebu timeline does not name Homonhon, and the Library of Congress blog does not date Limasawa or Cebu. Other pages date Cebu events differently and were not used. The wider voyage is in 1519-magellan-elcano-circumnavigation.
2026-10-22 · 1817-san-martin-crossing-of-the-andes · outline · established · Least sure: the start date and army size (sources disagree: January 6, 12 or 18; 3,550 vs 5,000+). The Universidad de Mendoza page has a typo ("2017") that was read as 1817. The pass has no sourced coordinate, so the path has two waypoints. Links to Ayacucho and Bolívar are thematic, not causal claims.
2026-10-22 · 1010-shahnameh · outline · established · Pinned at Tus per World History Encyclopedia (Ferdowsi came from and returned to Tus); it does not say the poem was composed there. Pin is the GeoNames ancient-wall point for the city. Cambridge gives only the completion year; the 8 March day rests on one source.
2026-10-22 · -0400-mahabharata-and-ramayana · outline · probable · No opened page says where either epic was composed. The pin is the Mahabharata's battlefield at Kurukshetra with 300 km uncertainty (Encyclopedia.com places the story in the Ganges-Yamuna doab); the Ramayana is set at Ayodhya. Combined record; start year uses the earliest Ramayana estimate, though the id year is -0400.
2026-10-22 · 1300-little-ice-age · outline · contested · Boundaries are disputed: EBSCO says the evidence applies mainly to Europe and it could have been European only; EH.net calls the term questionable. The Iceland point is a marker with 5000 km uncertainty. Both sources are reference class; no scholarly paper opened. Tambora link is only thematic.
2026-10-22 · person-rumi · outline · established · Birthplace is disputed between sources (Vakhsh vs Balkh), so the pin is Konya, where the family settled. Neither opened page names his place of death. Both sources are reference class; no scholarly source opened.
2026-10-22 · person-confucius · outline · established · Dates are approximate (551/552 birth). The 498 BCE departure and 484 BCE return come from Encyclopedia.com only (read to 100,000 characters); World History Encyclopedia gives no years for the travels. No scholarly source opened.
2026-10-22 · -1754-code-of-hammurabi · outline · probable · Pinned at Susa (findspot), not the Louvre and not Babylon: the Louvre record gives only Mesopotamia as place of creation. The 1754 BCE date rests on one travel-guide caption; the Louvre gives only the reign, and World History Encyclopedia contradicts itself (1722 vs 1772).
2026-10-22 · 1215-magna-carta · outline · established · The animation consequence beat rests on the National Archives page. Runnymede pin is the GeoNames meadow point; the exact sealing spot on the meadow is not given.
2026-10-22 · person-william-shakespeare · outline · established · No opened page names his place of death or burial, so none is stated. The 1592 and 1613 waypoint dates are from World History Encyclopedia only; the move to London is not dated by it. London pin is a city centroid.
2026-10-22 · 1869-first-transcontinental-railroad · outline · established · Path waypoints are in date order, so the drawn line runs Sacramento, Omaha, Promontory Summit, not the two builders' separate routes. The 1,912-mile figure is combined track as the Census page gives it. Promontory pin is the GeoNames gap point. Waypoints ordered west to east (the two companies built toward each other), so the route is drawn static rather than growing by date.
2026-10-22 · place-chaco-canyon · outline · established · No model block written: the opened pages give Pueblo Bonito only in stories (four to five) and rooms (over 600), not a height in metres or plan dimensions. Pin is the GeoNames valley point, not Pueblo Bonito itself (36.0606, -107.9617).
2026-10-22 · -26ka-oruanui-supereruption · outline · established · Least sure of the date: sources differ (25.4 ka vs about 26,500 years ago) and neither states its reference year; the id says 26 ka. Pin is the volcano point, not the vent area. The 450 km³ super-eruption threshold from the Vanderbilt page was left out of the summary.
2026-10-22 · -800ka-vostok-and-epica-ice-cores · outline · established · Least sure of the single pin date: the record spans 420,000 to about 800,000 years and no drilling dates were read on an opened page. Pin is Dome C by Wikidata only; EU page was read as a PDF via pdftotext.
2026-10-22 · -34my-opening-of-drake-passage · outline · contested · Least sure of the date, which is genuinely contested; the id says 34 Ma but no opened source gives 34 Ma as the opening date. Pin is a point mid-passage. Only two studies were read; a 2021 deep-opening study was a search lead, not opened. The Eagles PDF was read via pdftotext.
2026-10-22 · -80my-hawaiian-emperor-seamount-chain · outline · probable · Least sure of the date: the 80 million year figure is an estimate for the northern end in a USGS newsletter, and the same page says the oldest dated seamount is 65 million years, so the sources disagree on measured ages. Pin is the Hawaii end by Wikidata only. Wikidata gives no polygon for the chain.
2026-10-22 · -6100-storegga-slide-tsunami · outline · established · Least sure of the id: it says 6100 BCE but the sources give about 8,150 cal BP (about 6200 BCE). Pin is the Wikidata point for the slide, not a precise scar location. A 2023 revision splitting off an earlier "Nyegga Slide" appeared only in a search result and was not opened.
2026-10-22 · -6my-messinian-salinity-crisis · outline · established · Least sure of the pin: the crisis affected the whole Mediterranean, so the Strait of Gibraltar point carries a 1500 km uncertainty. The end age is stated as 5.33 Ma on both pages; Garcia-Castellanos puts the drying at about 5.6 Ma, which is within the range.
2026-10-22 · 1783-laki-eruption · outline · established · Least sure of mortality and gas figures: the sources differ (fissure 25 vs 27 km, 130 vs 140 craters, livestock percentages), so only the figures where they agree are in the summary. Pin is the Wikidata point for Laki, not the fissure midpoint. The GeoExPro URL was opened but its exact title was not read, so the title field is descriptive.
2026-10-22 · 1859-carrington-event · outline · established · Least sure of the pin: this is a worldwide event, so the Redhill point has 6000 km uncertainty; Redhill is named only in a reference title (Carrington 1863) in the Hayakawa paper. The NESDIS page says auroras appeared "as far south as Panama"; the 17-hour gap is its wording.
2026-10-22 · -1600-minoan-eruption-of-thera · outline · contested · Least sure of how to set the single date range: sources disagree and I took the radiocarbon start (1627 BCE) and an approximate late-16th-century end. Both source PDFs were read through pdftotext. The pin is the Wikidata caldera point.
2026-10-22 · -94my-oceanic-anoxic-event-2 · outline · established · Least sure of the pin: the event was worldwide and the Wikidata point is the town of Wunstorf, not the exact section. The two durations (about 500 kyr vs 430-445 kyr) differ, so the end date is approximate. Start of 93.9 Ma is from the PNAS article text.
2026-10-22 · 1859-carrington-event · outline · established · Least sure of the pin: this is a worldwide event, so the Redhill point has 6000 km uncertainty; Redhill is named only in a reference title (Carrington 1863) in the Hayakawa paper. The NESDIS page says auroras appeared "as far south as Panama"; the 17-hour gap is its wording.
2026-10-22 · 1901-federation-of-australia · outline · established · The 60,000 crowd figure is Centennial Parklands only; the Australian Parliament pages reportedly give other figures (not opened). Parliament House APH, peo.gov.au and Museums of History NSW returned 403 and were not used. Queen Victoria proclamation date (17 September 1900) is on the Senate page but left out of the summary. Coordinates are the OSM park centre, not the Federation Pavilion.
2026-10-22 · place-budj-bim-eel-traps · outline · established · UNESCO (whc.unesco.org/en/list/1577), Parks Victoria and DJPR pages returned 403, so the listing month comes only from the budjbim.com.au page, which that page itself draws from the UNESCO listing; please confirm against UNESCO. The Gunditjmara wording ("deep time", native title recognised 2007) is from budjbim.com.au; the publisher relationship to Gunditj Mirring Traditional Owners Aboriginal Corporation was not verified from an opened page (the sites page says GMTOAC owns and manages several sites). The sea.museum page says the Muldoons charcoal is 6,600 years old and gives "around 6,000 years" for the start of the eel farms. Coordinate is the OSM peak/volcano point; the landscape spans about 35 km (sea.museum).
2026-10-22 · 1946-bikini-atoll-nuclear-tests · outline · established · Livermore says Operation Crossroads began in "July 1946"; other accounts (not opened) place the Able test on 30 June 1946, so the start year is firm but the month is not stated here. Livermore gives 167 people moved to Rongerik; the Charles Sturt page agrees. The 184 who sailed to Kili in November 1948 is a different count (Livermore and CSU both give 184). Livermore text lists coordinates "about 11°36′N 165°23′E"; OSM atoll centre used instead. The US Navy Crossroads page failed a certificate check and UNESCO returned 403. Tolls and health effects are left to the full record.
2026-10-22 · 1972-ho-chi-minh-trail · outline · established · The record id says 1972; the Air & Space Forces article says interdiction essentially ended in early 1972 and the Easter offensive began on 30 March 1972, but the trail itself ran 1959-1975 per VNA. The end year 1975 is inferred from VNA ("1959 to 1975"). The 20,000 km figure is Vietnamese state media; the same AFA article gives John Prados at about 12,000 miles, so lengths conflict. Waypoint years are the dates the source attaches to the trail stage (May 1959 order; early 1961 shift into Laos), not dates of passage at each point. Bombing totals conflict across sources and are omitted.
2026-10-22 · 1943-burma-thailand-death-railway · outline · established · The AWM gives the length as 420 km; the BTRMA gives 415 km (used). Death tolls: AWM, at least 2,815 Australians, over 11,000 other Allied prisoners and perhaps 75,000 romusha; BTRMA, over 12,000 POWs (over 2,700 Australians) and romusha "probably up to 90 000" or "between 75 000 and 100 000", record keeping being poor. Search leads (not opened) gave other completion dates and higher romusha tolls up to 100,000 or more. The DVA Anzac Portal pages timed out and were not used. Hellfire Pass is left out of waypoints for lack of an opened dated source.
2026-10-22 · 1955-bandung-conference · outline · established · The State Department page gives only "April 1955"; the exact days 18-24 are from the Google Arts & Culture story (the Jabar provincial page, Republika and others were 403 or not opened). The Merdeka Building is named on the Google page as a venue, not stated as the sole venue. Participant breakdown (Asia/Africa/observers) not confirmed on opened pages and left out.
2026-10-22 · 1835-great-trek · outline · established · Casualty figures conflict and are mostly left out: Blood River Zulu dead "about 3,000" (TheCollector, which says historians dispute it) versus "thousands" (EBSCO); Retief party about 100 and Weenen 534 killed (TheCollector only); total migrants 12,000 (Encyclopedia.com), 12,000-14,000 (EBSCO). The Retief killing is dated February 1838 by TheCollector; Encyclopedia.com gives no date. sahistory.org.za returned 403. The Vaal crossing is a river reference point with a very rough location, and the Vegkop battle (October 1836 per Encyclopedia.com) is not mapped because no reliable coordinate was found. Coordinates of the Blood River Heritage Site are OSM, on the west bank of the Blood River; the Ncome museum is across the river.
2026-10-22 · 1908-hejaz-railway · outline · established · Sources conflict: APM says "ordered in March 1900", Gulf International Forum says construction began September 1900; length is "approximately 1,300 km" (APM) while others cited in searches give 1,302 or 1,320 km, and CSMonitor gives about 800 miles; opening 1 September 1908 is from nabataea.net only (APM says only 1908). The 40 days to 72 hours and purpose quote are from The National. Gulf International Forum says 460 km was built by 1904 connecting to Jordan; no stations or dates between Damascus and Medina were confirmed, so waypoints are the two termini. APM, not Wikipedia, supports Arab Revolt sabotage between Ma'an and Medina in 1916. The Medina point is the city centre.
2026-10-22 · 1934-long-march · outline · established · Sources conflict on: distance (EBSCO about 6,000 miles; Model Diplomat Chinese official accounts roughly 12,500 km, which Sun Shuyun argues is overstated); survivors (EBSCO about 4,000; Model Diplomat 7,000-8,000); end date (EBSCO says completed on arrival at Wuqi and gives October 1934 to December 1935; Model Diplomat says October 1935). Luding Bridge date 27 May 1935 is EBSCO only; Model Diplomat gives no date; the "Liuding" spelling in EBSCO is as given there. Start town Ruijin is not named on opened pages so the start point is the Jiangxi province reference point, very approximate. The Xiang River and Tucheng crossings (EBSCO) are left unmapped. Related link "caused" rests on Model Diplomat only.
2026-10-22 · 1960-valdivia-earthquake · outline · established · The epicentre coordinates were not found on any opened page (USGS event pages did not render; a USGS value seen only in a search snippet was not used), so the point is the OSM reference point for Valdivia city and uncertainty is set to 150 km; the rupture was much longer. Death tolls conflict: NOAA NCEI says 490 to 5,700 in Chile and at least 122 in the tsunami at Maullin, Quenuir and La Pasada; NOAA Science On a Sphere says an estimated 2,000 in Chile and 2,223 in total; History.com says 5,000 and a main quake at 3:11 p.m. on May 22 after a May 21 foreshock. Hawaii 61 deaths is NCEI. The NCEI page also says the shaking destroyed over 58,000 houses.
2026-10-22 · place-library-of-alexandria · outline · contested · No surviving structure, so no model. The pin is the centre of modern Alexandria, not the library: EBSCO says it was "somewhere in the Royal Quarter" and Open University notes ignorance of its position and layout, so uncertainty_km is 5. Founder is unclear (Ptolemy I or II). The when range is wide on purpose: the end is debated and may be gradual. No scroll counts used because the sources give only unreliable ancient figures.
2026-10-22 · place-itaipu-dam · outline · established · Shape wall with model.linear true as instructed. Length 7,919 m is the plant's own "Total length: 7.919 meters" (a dot thousands separator); IEEE gives only the river width, 7.9 km. Wikipedia-derived dates of 1971 were not used. Point is a way on the dam from OpenStreetMap; the dam is nearly 8 km long so the point lies on it, not at its centre. No geometry path: neither source names end points.
2026-10-22 · place-brasilia · outline · established · The record is the city (UNESCO Pilot Plan), but the model describes ONE structure, the Metropolitan Cathedral, so the pin sits on the cathedral. Shape: dome (closest available to the hyperboloid of 16 members; mention for the reviewer). Height 40 m is from the Ministry of Tourism; a UnB thesis seen in search results gave 30 m but was not opened, so treat height as less sure than the 70 m diameter. Inauguration date conflicts: 1967 (Ministry of Tourism) versus 31 May 1970 (Arquivo Público DF); 1970 is used. The National Congress towers were not modelled because no opened page gave their height.
2026-10-22 · place-qanats-of-persia · outline · probable · The record covers the eleven-qanat property but the pin and model are ONE structure, the Qasabeh qanat, using the ICOMOS figures for its Qasabeh branch (over 13 km, 222 wells, 200 m mother well). Other pages give 33 km, 427 or 472 wells and 320 m for the combined Qasabeh and Doolab-e system, so they were kept out of the model. Shape wall with linear true is a stand-in: a qanat is underground, so "height" is recorded as depth of the mother well and should not be extruded upward. Dates conflict (700-500 BCE vs 3-4 centuries BCE). Pin is an OSM attraction node and could be the town-end rather than the mother well. Qasabeh qanat figures (not modelled: an underground channel): length 13000 m (src-icomos-qanat); height 200 m (src-icomos-qanat).
2026-10-22 · place-dholavira · outline · established · No model: the opened pages give the walled area (771.1 x 616.9 m in the arXiv table of the excavator's figures, 771.10 x 616.85 m on Abhijna) but no sourced height, and wall heights in search results came only from slide decks and travel pages. Wall thickness figures (13-18 m) are from a Current Science note and were not used. UNESCO's English text calls the settlement a "city" and says occupation began about 3000 BCE.
2026-10-22 · place-chand-baori · outline · probable · Shape block (stepwell, per instructions): the height is depth below ground and the plan is the side. The 35 m side is from Lonely Planet only; I did not find a heritage-body or scholarly page giving a plan dimension (ASI page unreachable), so the plan is less sure than the 19.5 m depth. Depth conflicts: 19.5 m (Rajasthan Tourism, a published paper repeating it) vs about 98 feet (Incredible India Jaipur page). Step count also conflicts: 1,000 (Rajasthan Tourism) vs 3,500 (Incredible India, Lonely Planet), so no step count is used. Date range wide on purpose.
2026-10-22 · place-grand-trunk-road · outline · probable · No model: a road has no sourced height. The pin is one listed site on the road (the Moti Gate of Sher Shah Suri and Kos Minar at Babarpur Bazipur, Delhi, N28 41 16 E77 16 50 in the UNESCO list), not the road itself, so uncertainty_km is large. UNESCO says Sur-period routes ran Kabul to Sonargaon, British-period Kabul to Chittagong; the GlobalSecurity text, quoting an older gazetteer, says Kabul to Calcutta, about 1,500 miles. Claims that Chandragupta Maurya or Ashoka built it were not stated on the opened pages and are left out.
2026-10-22 · place-zhaozhou-bridge · outline · probable · Shape wall with linear true. Sources conflict: length 50.82 m (China Daily; ASCE 167 ft = about 50.9 m) versus 64.4 m (Science and Technology Daily); span 37 / 37.02 / 37.37 m; dates 605 versus 581-600. Height is the arch rise (23.9 ft, converted to 7.28 m), not the bridge height, which no opened page gives. ASCE says the Jiaohe River and China Daily the Xiao River, so no river is named in the summary. Coordinates are ASCE's DMS (37 43 12.9 N, 114 45 47.7 E) converted to decimal.
2026-10-22 · place-dujiangyan · outline · established · The model is the Feishayan floodgate only (240 m long, 2 m high, ICOMOS dossier, which spells it Feiyashan), because the dossier gives dimensions for each component separately and mixing them would not describe one structure; shape wall with linear true. Point is the UNESCO property coordinate (N31 0 6.012 E103 36 19.008); the headworks are within about 1 km of it but this was not measured. The dossier gives the floodgate no date of its own, so phases follow the system (256 BCE per UNESCO, 251 BCE completion per Sixth Tone).
2026-10-22 · place-akashi-kaikyo-bridge · outline · established · Shape wall with linear true. Tower height: the operator says "about 300 m"; Roads & Bridges and EBSCO say 282.8 m (Roads & Bridges also gives 282.2 m in one place), so 282.8 m is used. EBSCO gives overall length as 3.8 km, the operator and Roads & Bridges 3,911 m. Point is an OSM way on the bridge; no end-point coordinates in sources so no geometry path.
2026-10-22 · place-abu-simbel · outline · established · Model: Great Temple facade, 30 m high by 35 m wide (Notre Dame Raclin Murphy collection page). Facade figures in sources I found conflict (30-33 m high, 35-38 m wide) and none came from a page I could open; the Ministry page has no dimensions. Coordinate is the first UNESCO point for property 88, which is the Abu Simbel location; the list does not label it.
2026-10-22 · place-babylon · outline · established · Least sure of the start: the city predates Hammurabi but no earlier date is given, so the span starts with his reign. No model: no height or plan dimension for a single monument was sought.
2026-10-22 · place-shwedagon-pagoda · outline · established · No model written: UNESCO's tentative list gives the height (99 m after 1774, 112 m after the 1871 umbrella) and a 5.66 ha platform, but no university, museum or heritage page opened gives the stupa's base diameter, so the plan-dimension rule is not met. Least sure of the start date: 'between the 6th and 10th centuries AD' is a wide window and the 750 midpoint is a convenience. Tentative-list coordinates (N16.798356 E96.149705) and OSM agree to about 100 m.
2026-10-22 · place-copan · outline · established · Least sure of the end year: UNESCO says abandonment in the early 10th century, so 900 is a round figure. No model written: UNESCO says the Acropolis mass rises some 30 m and the Great Plaza covers three hectares, but I found no sourced single-structure height with a plan dimension from an acceptable page.
2026-10-22 · place-monte-alban · outline · established · No model: no height-plus-plan dimension for a named structure was sourced. UNESCO's XML lists two unlabelled points; I used the one matching Wikidata's Monte Albán (about 0.3 km away); the other is the Oaxaca city centre. UNESCO's text says the site was inhabited 1,500 years by Olmecs, Zapotecs and Mixtecs, while the 500 BCE-850 CE span is the Zapotec capital's flourishing.
2026-10-22 · place-abu-simbel · outline · established · Model: Great Temple facade, 30 m high by 35 m wide, from the University of Notre Dame Raclin Murphy Museum collection page; other sources give 30-33 m high and 35-38 m wide. Coordinate is the first UNESCO point for property 88, which is the Abu Simbel location; the list does not label it.
2026-10-22 · -635my-marinoan-snowball-earth · outline · established · Least sure of the pin: the event was worldwide and King Island is only the site of one dated sample, so uncertainty is 5000 km. The id says 635 Ma and "Snowball Earth", but the opened pages date the end of the glaciation at 636.4-635.2 Ma and I did not open a page using the Snowball Earth hypothesis, so the title says only what the pages state. The start of the glaciation is not given as a single number on the opened pages.
2026-10-22 · -2023my-vredefort-impact · outline · established · Least sure of the age: IUCN gives 2,023 million years, IUGS 2.02 Ga and NASA only "about two billion years"; the opened pages state no error bar. IUGS calls it the second-oldest known impact structure, while the IUCN page calls it the oldest. Pin is the IUGS dome coordinates; uncertainty is half the stated original diameter.
2026-10-22 · -1850my-sudbury-impact · outline · established · Least sure of the original crater size: Crater Explorer lists 250 km as an estimate and 200 to 250 km elsewhere, NASA says at least 200 km. Crater Explorer coordinates (N 46°36′ W 81°11′) are given to the minute; pin uncertainty is half the stated diameter. Crater Explorer is a personal site, so the precise age rests on it alone; NASA gives only a rounded figure.
2026-10-22 · -470my-origin-of-land-plants · outline · contested · Least sure of the whole record: the id says 470 Ma, but the opened pages give 480 Ma (Australia) and 473-471 Ma (Argentina), each described as earliest by its own authors; the Argentine locality is not named on the page I opened. The pin is the Canning Basin from Wikidata with a 500 km radius (basin extent not sourced). The ScienceAlert text republishes a Conversation piece; the Science paper itself was not opened.
2026-10-22 · -5my-zanclean-flood · outline · probable · The 2020 abstract presents the flood as a hypothesis being tested, so confidence is probable. Both opened pages share a lead author: different publishers, not independent research groups. Pin is the Strait of Gibraltar; the 2020 abstract describes a channel nearly 390 km long from the Gulf of Cádiz to the Algerian Basin.
2026-10-22 · -14my-middle-miocene-climatic-transition · outline · probable · Least sure of the pin: the event was global and the opened pages name no type section, so the pin sits on the East Antarctic Ice Sheet with 5000 km uncertainty; the Wikidata point (-80, 80) is generic. Dates range from about 14 Ma to 15-13 Ma; the id uses 14. Debate over how much of the isotope signal is ice rather than cooling came from search summaries only and was not opened.
2026-10-22 · -129ka-eemian-interglacial · outline · established · Least sure of the pin: the Eemian was global, and NEEM is only the Eemian ice-core site named by the University of Copenhagen, so uncertainty is 5000 km. The id says 129 ka but the opened pages give 130,000-115,000 and 128,000-116,000 years. The temperature and sea-level figures come from the 2013 University of Washington news report. The phrase "interglacial before the present one" is my reading of "last interglacial period" in the Muhs title.
2026-10-22 · -183my-toarcian-oceanic-anoxic-event · outline · probable · Least sure of the age: PNAS says ~183 Mya, Deposits Magazine says 180 million years. The pin is the town of Whitby (Wikidata), standing in for the Yorkshire outcrops the magazine names; the event was global, so uncertainty is 5000 km. The Karoo-Ferrar link is from the magazine only; the PNAS abstract mentions volcanic CO2 but not the province.
2026-10-22 · -2200-4-2-kiloyear-event · outline · probable · Least sure of the date: the id says 2200 BCE, but the boundary is given as 4250 years before 2000 CE (astronomical year -2250) and The Conversation says 4,200 years ago. The pin is the Mawmluh Cave stalagmite, the sourced type section, with 5 km uncertainty for the cave; the event itself is wider. The civilization-collapse link is only noted as coincidence on the opened page, not established.
2026-10-22 · -120my-ontong-java-plateau-eruption · outline · probable · Least sure of the age: the id says 120 Ma, but the opened pages give 122 ± 3 and 90 ± 4 Ma (ODP prospectus) and newer 96-116 Ma argon ages; Tejada et al. call some of the older drill-site ages poor quality. The 700 km pin uncertainty is my own estimate from the stated 1.6 million km2 area, not a sourced figure; the Wikidata point is the plateau centre.
2026-10-22 · 1271-marco-polo-journey-to-china · outline · probable · Arrival 1275 and departure 1292 are on EBSCO and World History Encyclopedia; the Mariners' Museum gives no arrival year. Waypoint year 1275.3 is a placement for spring. Shangdu coordinates are the Wikidata point for Xanadu (EBSCO names Shangdu as the summer capital; World History Encyclopedia places Xanadu in Inner Mongolia); GeoNames Shangdu Xian is a different modern county. World History Encyclopedia says doubts remain over what he saw, but most scholars believe he went to China. Venice appears at both ends of the path. Acre and Venice degrees-minutes-seconds converted to decimal.
2026-10-22 · 0399-faxian-pilgrimage-to-india · outline · probable · Pataliputra stay 405-407 and Tamralipti 408-409 are on Encyclopedia.com only. Sri Lanka and Khotan have no sourced year, so they are not waypoints. The landing name Laoshan is on Encyclopedia.com; EBSCO says Shandong Peninsula. Laoshan Qu (Qingdao) is a GeoNames point for the name, not a precise landing site. Tamluk and Laoshan coordinates converted from GeoNames degrees-minutes-seconds. The identification of Pataliputra with Patna and Tamralipti with Tamluk is not stated on the opened pages.
2026-10-22 · 1804-lewis-and-clark-expedition · outline · established · Native roles come from NPS and a non-tribal heritage foundation; no tribal-nation source was opened (Shoshone-Bannock, Mandan Hidatsa Arikara Nation, Nez Perce and Clatsop pages still to add). Sacagawea is described as Shoshone; the foundation page says she was an interpreter, not a guide. Camp River Dubois is placed at the St. Louis point with 40 km uncertainty because no opened page gives its coordinates. Waypoint years are day-of-year fractions: 1804.37 = 14 May; 1804.82 = 27 Oct; 1805.95 = mid December; 1806.73 = 23 Sept. Pacific arrival: NPS says by November 1805; the Washington University timeline says Clark's Ocian in view entry of 7 November was still about 20 miles from the sea.
2026-10-22 · 1838-trail-of-tears · outline · established · Numbers: NPS trte says over 16,000 evicted, more than 1,000 died on the trip, perhaps several thousand more from consequences; NPS Little River Canyon gives Butler's estimate of over 4,000; the NPS Fort Smith page gives over 10,000 across all removed nations (not used). Detachment counts differ across pages (13, 14, 17); omitted. Cherokee agency: Ross petitioned Scott and supervised the detachments (Native History Association; Cherokee Trilogy says each was led by a Cherokee conductor); the Cherokee Nation and Eastern Band (about 1,000 avoided the roundup, per NPS) have not been consulted directly. Mantle Rock year 1839.0 is inferred (NPS gives January and says all survivors reached the West by March 1839). Tahlequah Aug 1839 is from NPS Little River Canyon. Tahlequah degrees-minutes-seconds converted to decimal. Only one non-NPS publisher with a government class was not found; all non-NPS sources are class other.
2026-10-22 · 1860-burke-and-wills-expedition · outline · established · Dates rest on EBSCO alone (National Museum of Australia and Museums Victoria pages returned 403). Australian Museum: organised by the Royal Society of Victoria, Burke dashed north with Wills, King and Gray; Gray's death attributed to short provisions, scurvy, weather and hostile Aboriginals; King helped by Aboriginals. Burke & Wills Web: seven men died; of the four who reached the north coast, King alone survived. No opened page names the Yandruwandha or other Aboriginal nations; add that from a museum or community source on expansion. The Cooper's Creek waypoint is an Innamincka point with 100 km record uncertainty. Waypoint years are day-of-year fractions.
2026-10-22 · 1853-livingstone-trans-african-journey · outline · established · Sources conflict: start May vs November 1853 (S2A3 distinguishes arrival in May from departure in November); Victoria Falls day 17 November is on EBSCO (its header says 1853 but the body supports 1855; S2A3 says November 1855); Quelimane 20 or 25 May 1856. Neither page says first to cross Africa, so that claim is omitted. Sekeletu supplied porters (EBSCO); the S2A3 page names Chief Sebituane instead. The Falls are Mosi-oa-Tunya (EBSCO). The return to Linyanti in September 1855 is on both pages. African guides and carriers are not individually named on the pages opened. Waypoint years are month fractions.
2026-10-22 · 1488-dias-rounding-the-cape · outline · established · History.com puts the turnaround at Kwaaihoek (12 March 1488) and does not mention Algoa Bay or the Great Fish River; EBSCO names the Great Fish River as the limit, 520 miles (837 km) east of the Cape, with no dates. EBSCO says sixteen months and seventeen days; History.com says 15 months at sea. The Cape sighting is undated in both. Mossel Bay as the site of Sao Bras is given by History.com. Waypoint years are month and day-of-year fractions. Lisbon appears at both ends of the path.
2026-10-22 · 1541-orellana-amazon-descent · outline · probable · December 1541 and June 1542 dates rest on Brewminate alone (class other). Brewminate gives 24 or 26 August 1542 for the open sea; the Atlantic mouth is not a waypoint (no coordinate source). Encyclopedia.com says about 60 men or 50 soldiers; Brewminate says about 50 then 49. Napo and Negro positions are loose (300 km uncertainty). Wikidata gives two coordinates for the Napo with unlabelled qualifiers; the first is used. Open Rivers (UMN) says the journey ran from the Andes to Marajo Island at the Atlantic, begins on the Napo, and that Carvajal described large populations and roads; Brewminate lists Omagua, Machiparo people, Pira-tapuya and Tapuya groups. Name Amazon: Encyclopedia.com says the Spaniards first named the land after Greek Amazons, the river later; Brewminate notes the link is uncertain.
2026-10-22 · 1513-balboa-crossing-of-the-isthmus · outline · probable · World History Encyclopedia says 200 conquistadors and over 1,000 native bearers; Encyclopedia.com says nearly 200 Spaniards and hundreds of Indian carriers; the summary keeps nearly 200 and many. Linda Hall says no one knows where the sighting happened, so only two waypoints are used (fewer than the 3 asked). Encyclopedia.com has an inconsistency (13 September in one passage, 27 September in another). Indigenous allies and guides are not named on any page opened. Acla position: Wikidata. The return to Darien in January 1514 (Encyclopedia.com) has no named place, so no third stop.
2026-10-22 · 1000-norse-vinland-voyages · outline · probable · Kept as a point record, not a route: no opened page locates Norse stops with dates, and the Greenland base is not named. Helluland/Markland positions are only suggested (northern Labrador or Baffin Island; central Labrador) so no route is drawn. Parks Canada says the site could be Leif Ericsson Vinland camp; World History Encyclopedia says likely Leifsbudir/Straumfjord. The extent of Vinland is debated. The sagas were written down in the 13th century. Indigenous peoples are described through the saga term skraeling (called derogatory by World History Encyclopedia); no Indigenous-authored source was opened.
2026-10-22 · place-pantheon-rome · outline · established · Shape domed_building. Least sure of the start year: Texas Tech gives Hadrian ca. 118-125 CE, italia.it 112-125, and the first Agrippan building (27 BC) is not modelled. Dome diameter and height vary by source (43.2 MIT course page, 43.3 Texas Tech, 43.44 italia.it); 43.3 from Texas Tech used.
2026-10-22 · place-baalbek-temple-of-jupiter · outline · established · Shape temple_colonnade. Least sure of: the construction start year (-26 is an approximation of "Augustan period", no year on any page), and column height (27 m original per Livius vs 22 m standing per ICOMOS Lebanon). Livius says the granite columns were removed from Justinian on; granite material is from the shrine page.
2026-10-22 · place-selimiye-mosque-edirne · outline · established · Shape domed_building. Least sure of dome height: Ministry gives 43.28 m from the floor, ICOMOS 42.3 m internal; dome diameter 31.28 m (Ministry) vs 31.3/31.5 m (ICOMOS). The point is the OSM landuse way of the complex, not the mosque building itself.
2026-10-22 · place-nemrut-dagi-tumulus · outline · established · Shape mound. Least sure of the date: the pages give only the reign of Antiochos I (69-34 BCE), so the construction phase spans the reign and the altered phase start (-33) is not a dated event. Diameter 145 m (UNESCO) vs 150 m (ICOMOS); height 50 m now vs estimated 60 m originally. Point is the Nemrut Dağı peak node in OSM.
2026-10-22 · place-kalyan-minaret-bukhara · outline · established · Shape tower. ICOMOS puts the Poi-Kalyan minaret in the 12th century (and the group in the 11th/12th); 1127-29 comes from the scholarly page and the Orexca page. Least sure of: the phases after 1129 (no damage events sourced) and the top diameter (Orexca, class other).
2026-10-22 · place-giant-wild-goose-pagoda · outline · established · Outline only, no model: the only height and base figures opened were on commercial travel pages (China Highlights: 64.517 m tall, 25.5 m base length), which do not meet the model brief; the Shaanxi government page was unreachable. 2026-10-22 · place-kuelap-fortress · outline · established · Shape wall with linear true, as instructed. 2026-10-22 · place-el-tajin-pyramid-of-the-niches · outline · established · Shape stepped_pyramid.
