Sources differ on whether the company dates to 1971 (AGNSW) or 1972 (Papunya Tula Artists); both shown. No restricted content described. A knowledgeable reviewer should confirm wording.
2026-10-22 · -2100-code-of-ur-nammu · outline · probable · Authorship (Ur-Nammu or Shulgi) is reported as uncertain by both sources; dates follow the conventional chronology. Pin is Ur, the king's city (EBSCO gives Ur as locale). Surviving tablets come from other sites; not described here.
2026-10-22 · 0533-corpus-juris-civilis · outline · established · The World History Encyclopedia corpus page is loose on dates (says two more parts followed "the following year" after April 529); the summary relies on the Max-EuP date for the Digest and the WHE April 529 and December 533 statements. Coordinates are Istanbul city, not a specific building.
2026-10-22 · 1920-08-18-nineteenth-amendment · outline · established · Place is Nashville, where the TSLA exhibit locates the final fight at the Hermitage Hotel near the Capitol; no opened page states the building of the vote in so many words. Another NPS page says "signed into law" 26 August 1920; both dates are shown.
2026-10-22 · place-leluh-ruins-kosrae · outline · established · No model: no opened source gives a wall height and plan for a single enclosure (the NRHP nomination is a scanned PDF that could not be read as text). HeritageDaily adds about 270,000 m2 and around 1500 inhabitants at its peak, and 20 compounds.
2026-10-22 · 1991-dissolution-of-the-soviet-union · outline · established · Least sure of the pin: it marks the Kremlin where the flag came down, while the accords were signed elsewhere (Belovezhie). The Alma-Ata meeting and 26 December are omitted for lack of an opened dated source.
2026-10-22 · 1981-hiv-aids-pandemic · outline · established · Pin is Los Angeles, the sourced first-reported location, not an origin of the virus (origin is not claimed). WHO cumulative death range is given as printed with no stated year. CDC page itself notes June 4 vs 5; 5 June used.
2026-10-22 · 1961-gagarin-first-human-spaceflight · outline · established · Pin is the launch site (OSM centroid of a large cosmodrome, hence 30 km); the landing site is about 51N 45E per ESA. ESA does not itself state the 108-minute duration (NASA and Planetary Society do).
2026-10-22 · 1975-cambodian-genocide · outline · established · Death toll is attributed and contested: "nearly two million" (USHMM) vs "approximately 1.7 million" (Yale, 1997). The 17 April 1975 takeover has its own record. Pin is the capital with wide uncertainty because the process was nationwide; the USHMM page does not name Phnom Penh.
2026-10-22 · 1962-algerian-independence · outline · established · The Library of Congress study is read on a mirror site (countrystudies.us); the US Historian page gives no 5 July date. Pin is Algiers as capital; the referendum was country-wide. War death figures are attributed estimates.
2026-10-22 · 1975-fall-of-saigon · outline · established · Scope is the 30 April event only; the war itself is in 1955-vietnam-war. The two sources name the palace differently (Presidential vs Imperial); the OSM way is Independence Palace.
2026-10-22 · 1956-suez-crisis · outline · established · Pin is Port Said, where Anglo-French troops landed; the crisis spans the Canal and Sinai. Ceasefire: State Dept says 6 November acceptance, UN says it took effect midnight GMT 7/8 November. "First large UN emergency force" in the importance text is not claimed in the summary. UN page gives nationalization only as about 26 July.
2026-10-22 · 2011-egyptian-revolution · outline · established · The wider Arab Spring is in 2010-arab-spring. CFR gives only the resignation date; start date from Amnesty. The Amnesty PDF was read via text extraction. "Nearly three decades" in importance rests on CFR dates 14 Oct 1981 to 11 Feb 2011.
2026-10-22 · 1978-reform-and-opening-of-china · outline · established · Both sources are Chinese state or Party outlets, so framing is official; no independent source was opened. Day 18 December rests on one source. Pin is the Beijing centroid, since the venue is not on any page opened.
2026-10-22 · 1987-montreal-protocol · outline · established · Pin is Montreal city, not the conference venue. The UN treaty PDF was read via text extraction. The 1986 freeze comes from the treaty text. Effects on the ozone layer are not claimed.
2026-10-22 · place-great-mosque-of-cordoba · outline · established · Model omitted at outline stage as cathedral chapter site provides no official height or plan dimensions; founding date 786-788 CE from Cabildo corresponds to UNESCO 8th century designation.
2026-10-22 · place-deir-el-bahari-temple-of-hatshepsut · outline · established · Model omitted as outline record because official ministry site gives no full plan dimensions and Met site returned HTTP 429; dates follow the Egyptian Ministry of Tourism and Antiquities.
2026-10-22 · place-gol-gumbaz-bijapur · outline · established · Model omitted at outline tier because Archnet returned HTTP 429; construction date 1659 CE and dome description are confirmed directly from the Archaeological Survey of India (ASI).
2026-10-22 · place-ananda-temple-bagan · outline · established · Model omitted at outline tier because no authoritative dimension sources were opened; dated c. 1105 CE under King Kyanzittha following NIU SEAsite and early 12th century following PIB.
2026-10-22 · place-great-mosque-of-kairouan · outline · established · Shape choice: tower (square-plan minaret in three storeys); the model describes the minaret only, not the 126 x 75 m mosque. Least sure: the Qantara page names no publisher, and UNESCO and Archnet pages could not be opened. Minaret attribution is debated (Al-Bakri credits Hisham, most archaeologists Ziyadat Allah I); the third storey was probably remodelled in the Hafsid period.
2026-10-22 · place-el-djem-amphitheatre · outline · established · Shape choice: arena (elliptical amphitheatre), axes and height from the Tunisian ministry page. Figures differ slightly by publisher: Princeton gives about 148 x 122 m, arena about 65 x 39 m and seats rising about 36 m; the ministry gives 147.90 x 122.20 m and arena 64 x 39 m. Least sure: the construction date, which no inscription fixes.
2026-10-22 · place-cairo-citadel · outline · established · Shape choice: domed_building, for the Muhammad Ali Mosque only (not the whole citadel, which the MWNF page gives as a northeastern section of about 560 x 317 m). Point is the mosque, within the citadel. Least sure: heights; MWNF lists dome height 52 m from the floor and minarets 82 m, while the ministry page gives minarets 84 m and no dome figures. Only MWNF gives dome and hall dimensions.
2026-10-22 · place-mausoleum-at-halicarnassus · outline · probable · Destroyed monument; model is a reconstruction from ancient-source and excavation figures. Shape choice: stepped_pyramid (podium plus 24-step pyramid; no closer shape exists, so the silhouette is approximate). Height about 42 m is Pliny's 140 ft as reported by the Cambridge chapter (Getty gives 43 m; WHE 45 m). Podium 38 x 32 m is from WHE; Livius (not cited) reads the substructure as 32 x 26 m. Least sure: height, and the date of collapse (unknown; possibly earthquake).
2026-10-22 · place-temple-of-artemis-ephesus · outline · probable · Destroyed monument. Model is the Hellenistic rebuild only (WHE gives about 105 x 55 m and columns 17.65 m from excavation evidence); the earlier temple has Pliny-based figures of 129.5 x 68.6 m and 18.3 m columns (WHE) or about 377 x 180 ft (Encyclopaedia Romana). No overall building height is stated on any opened page, so height is the column height. Shape: temple_colonnade. Dates of the Goths vary (c. 267 WHE; AD 262 Encyclopaedia Romana). Least sure: the height and these dates.
2026-10-22 · place-kailasa-temple-ellora · outline · established · Covers the Kailasa temple only; Ellora as a whole is place-ellora-caves. No model: the 32 m height (WHE) has no matching plan dimension on an opened page (the 90 m and 53 m figures describe the trenches, and the ASI gives no measurements). Shape would be stepped_pyramid or block. Least sure: start year (ASI and WHE give different reigns for Krishna I).
2026-10-22 · place-khajuraho-temples · outline · established · Group pin at the Kandariya Mahadeva (Western Group). No model: WHE gives a 31 m height for the main sikhara but no opened museum, university or government page gives a plan dimension (ASI gives none; the tourism and blog figures conflict). Shape would be stepped_pyramid. Least sure: the builder (ASI says Ganda, WHE Vidyadhara) and start year.
2026-10-22 · place-benin-city-walls · outline · probable · Linear work (model.linear would be true) with no model: no opened page gives a wall height (Bournemouth says ditch and bank 4 m deep; the Met and WMF pages giving 18 m and 1,200 km could not be opened), and no point on the wall line was sourced to 1 km. Pin is the city centre, uncertainty 10 km. Least sure: dating, since the project reports C13th–C15th and early C8th dates for different sites.
2026-10-22 · place-grand-canal-of-china · outline · established · Linear work (model.linear would be true); no model because no opened page gives a canal height or a point to 1 km on the line. Pin is on the Hangzhou stretch, uncertainty 10 km. Lengths differ: Beijing gives about 1,797 km, China Daily gives 3,200 km overall and 2,400 km for the Sui-era canal. Least sure: start year, as the canal was built in sections from 486 BC.
2026-10-22 · -8000-nabta-playa · outline · probable · Least sure: dating. UNESCO gives megaliths as 4,600-3,400 BC but Archaeology (1998) reports deposits 7,000-6,700 years ago; I span both and did not choose. Settlement start is UNESCO's "about 11,000 BP" converted with BP = 1950. Wikidata point is a basin centre, not a specific structure. The astronomical meaning is described by the source as speculation, so the summary says only that one sight line roughly matches the solstice sunrise.
2026-10-22 · -0100-austronesian-settlement-of-madagascar · outline · contested · Least sure: the start date. Sources disagree (late first millennium BC accepted by some, rejected by Mitchell 2019); I use Mitchell and the crop dates and mark confidence contested. Point is the island centre, not a landing site. Publication year of both papers is from their citations.
2026-10-22 · 1818-mfecane · outline · contested · Least sure: casualties. Neither page names who produced its figures; SAHO calls the 1-2 million "traditional estimates" now considered exaggerated, EBSCO says "hundreds of thousands" killed and some estimates reach 1-2 million. Both are reported as the publishers' own statements, not consensus. Point is a province centre; the area is much larger. Start 1815 follows SAHO only.
2026-10-22 · 1741-bering-great-northern-expedition · outline · established · Least sure: exact days. NPS says 17 July (Julian) sighting, Gale says 16 July; Bering's death is 8 December in one Gale entry and 19 December in another on the same page. Survivor counts differ (NPS 46 led by Waxell; Gale 45 of 77), so none is given. Okhotsk year 1740 comes from "by 1740 preparations at Okhotsk were completed". Bering Island and Okhotsk Wikidata points are rounded.
2026-10-22 · 1771-flight-of-the-torghut-kalmyks · outline · probable · Least sure: head count (169,000 is one Gale figure; I saw other figures only in search snippets, not on opened pages) and named intermediate stops, which no opened page gives, so the route has only start and end. Cambridge page is oral history, used for the leader's name only. Related link to the Kazakh Khanate follows the task note; no opened page states the connection. Start point is the Kalmykia republic centre, a stand-in for the Volga steppe.
2026-10-22 · 1799-humboldt-latin-america-expedition · outline · established · Least sure: route detail. Only three stops are named with a place and year on opened pages; Mexico and Cuba are mentioned but their visits are not dated or placed precisely, and one Gale entry says Cuba came before the Andes while another says after Mexico, so they are not waypoints. Smithsonian Magazine page has no publication date I could read. Cumana point is Wikidata city centre.
2026-10-22 · 1795-mungo-park-niger-expedition · outline · established · Least sure: place names. I could place only Segou; Pisania and Silla have no modern location on the pages I opened, so there is no path (the task note asked for one). Gale text gives conflicting distances (over 200 miles vs over 1,000 miles up the Niger), so none is used. Dates come from a secondary biography (Thomson 1890), not Park's own journal.
2026-10-22 · 1871-stanley-finds-livingstone · outline · probable · Least sure: the meeting date (27 October vs 10 November 1871), which History.com itself states both ways; kept as both. Intermediate stops such as Unyanyembe are not on pages I opened, so the path has two points. The claim that the meeting refocused attention on the Nile is NOT made in the summary; importance justification is editorial.
2026-10-22 · 1287-rabban-sauma-journey · outline · established · Least sure: stop order and dates. Only Baghdad (1287) and the 1288 papal meeting carry years; the rest follow the order of the Gale narrative and are undated. Rome appears twice (arrival before the pope was elected, then 1288). The second Gale entry mentions Naples and Genoa stops, which are not used. Gale entries conflict on when he wrote his account, so only its existence is stated. Both Gale pages are one publisher; Iranica is the independent source for the 1287 embassy and 1288 return.
2026-10-22 · 1977-vietnamese-boat-people · outline · contested · Least sure: death toll and span. The two sources give very different rates (10-15% vs 10-50%) and neither names its researchers; both are stated as the publishers' own claims, not consensus. The id year 1977 is a placeholder; neither page dates the start of the boat exodus precisely. Point is the sea centre, not a departure port.
2026-10-22 · person-ahmed-ibn-fadlan · outline · established · Ibn Fadlan's birth and death years are unknown; dates reflect his documented 921-925 embassy from Baghdad to the Volga River.
2026-10-22 · person-bhimrao-ambedkar · outline · established · Ambedkar was born in Mhow and converted to Buddhism in Nagpur, but is anchored at New Delhi where he chaired the Drafting Committee of the Constitution.
2026-10-22 · person-malala-yousafzai · outline · established · Living person; start year is birth in Mingora (1997), end year marks the 2014 Nobel Peace Prize.
2026-10-22 · person-liliuokalani · outline · established · Pinned to ʻIolani Palace in Honolulu where she reigned, yielded power under protest in 1893, and was later imprisoned in 1895.
2026-10-22 · person-nur-jahan · outline · established · Dated to her 1611-1627 period of imperial power alongside Jahangir; she retired from politics after Jahangir's death in 1627 and died in 1645 in Lahore.
2026-10-22 · person-sarah-baartman · outline · established · Birth year is estimated around 1789; her life ended in Paris in 1815, but she is anchored in Hankey in the Gamtoos Valley where her remains were interred in 2002.
2026-10-22 · person-shajar-al-durr · outline · established · Birth date is unknown; record spans her 1250 sultanate and rule alongside Aybak until her death outside the Cairo Citadel in 1257.
2026-10-22 · person-mirabai · outline · established · Exact biographical dates are uncertain in the historical record; traditional scholarship dates her from c. 1498 to c. 1546/1547.
2026-10-22 · person-sappho · outline · established · Least sure of her exact birth town on Lesbos (Mytilene vs. Eresos) and exact birth/death years, as her biographical details derive from later classical testimonia.
2026-10-22 · person-rigoberta-menchu · outline · established · Least sure of the exact geographic coordinates for her birthplace hamlet of Chimel, anchored here to its municipal seat of Uspantán with a 15 km uncertainty radius.
2026-10-22 · person-queen-tamar-of-georgia · outline · established · Least sure of her exact year of death, which primary Georgian chronicles leave ambiguous between 1210, 1212, and 1213.
2026-10-22 · person-sei-shonagon · outline · established · Least sure of her life and location after Empress Teishi's death in 1001 CE, as her post-court years and exact death date left almost no trace in contemporary records.
2026-10-22 · person-rosalind-franklin · outline · established · Least sure of how much she realized that Watson and Crick had accessed her unpublished King's College MRC report data and Photo 51 before publishing their 1953 model.
2026-10-22 · person-hurrem-sultan · outline · established · Least sure of her birth name and pre-enslavement childhood in Ruthenia (often traditionally identified as Aleksandra Lisowska), which rests on later Polish folklore.
2026-10-22 · person-himiko · outline · probable · Least sure of the geographical location of Yamatai, debated between northern Kyushu and the Kinai/Nara region (anchored here to Hashihaka Kofun in Nara).
2026-10-22 · person-manuela-saenz · outline · established · Least sure of the completeness of surviving archival documentation from her final decades in exile in Paita, Peru, where many personal papers were destroyed after her death.
2026-10-22 · -7my-formation-of-the-sahara · outline · probable · Least sure of the exact onset date: climate models identify Tortonian Tethys shrinkage (~7–11 Ma), while eolian dune deposits in Chad confirm desert conditions by ~7 Ma.
2026-10-22 · -10ka-submergence-of-sundaland · outline · established · Least sure of the exact terminal date of open land bridges between major islands, as local sill depths and tectonic subsidence created regional differences between 11 ka and 7 ka.
2026-10-22 · -17ka-heinrich-events · outline · established · Least sure of the trigger mechanism behind ice-sheet surges (subsurface oceanic warming versus internal glaciological binge-purge oscillations) and why Greenland temperatures remained decoupled during the discharges.
2026-10-22 · -2my-dmanisi-hominins · outline · established · Least sure of taxonomic status: whether the Dmanisi specimens represent a distinct geographic subspecies (Homo erectus georgicus), early Homo erectus sensu lato, or a single variable lineage spanning Homo habilis and erectus.
2026-10-22 · -183my-karoo-ferrar-volcanism · outline · established · Least sure of the relative greenhouse contribution of mantle-derived volcanic degassing versus thermogenic methane and carbon dioxide generated by magma sills baking organic-rich sediments.
2026-10-22 · -25my-formation-of-lake-baikal · outline · established · Least sure of the exact onset date of open lacustrine conditions versus initial shallow wetland rifting, with estimates ranging between 30 and 20 Ma.
2026-10-22 · -125my-jehol-biota · outline · established · Least sure of the exact chronological boundary between the lower Yixian Formation lacustrine beds and the overlying Jiufotang Formation across different sub-basins in western Liaoning.
2026-10-22 · -35my-popigai-impact · outline · established · Least sure of the impact's contribution to the late Eocene biotic turnover and subsequent Eocene–Oligocene transition global cooling, as multiple impacts occurred within a 2-million-year window.
2026-10-22 · org-british-east-india-company · outline · established · Pinned at Surat because neither opened page names a London base. The dissolution is dated 1858 by RMG but WHE gives both 1858 (government takeover of possessions) and 1874 (Parliament dissolves the company); the 1857 rebellion link is by chronology only. Surat first-post date differs: 1607 (RMG) versus 1612–1619 (WHE).
2026-10-22 · polity-mughal-empire · outline · established · Capital choice: the sources name Delhi (Shah Jahan moved the capital there); Akbar-era and earlier seats are not stated on the opened pages, so none is pinned. Date of Shah Jahan's move not given. Peak extent not covered.
2026-10-22 · -0258-edicts-of-ashoka · outline · probable · The 258–257 date rests on a single reference source (EBSCO); others give c. 260 BCE for first edicts. The UNESCO tentative-list entry prints the coordinates as "34°12’ E 72’9’ N", with the letters apparently swapped; read here as 34°12′ N, 72°9′ E. The edicts stand at many sites, so the Shahbazgarhi pin is a representative site only.
2026-10-22 · polity-khmer-empire · outline · established · Pinned at Angkor as the capital WHE names for the empire after 802; UNESCO says the park holds the remains of different capitals, so earlier capital sites are not pinned. 2026-10-22 · 1257-samalas-eruption · outline · established · Pinned at the Segara Anak lake in the caldera, which Discover names as the caldera formed by the 1257 eruption. Neither source ties the eruption to the Little Ice Age, so no link to 1300-little-ice-age is made. Volume stated as 35-40 km3 (Discover) and about 40 km3 (CNRS); the 2013 PNAS paper itself was not opened.
2026-10-22 · 1822-decipherment-of-hieroglyphs · outline · established · Dates differ across pages (14, 22, 27 September); 27 September is used because two pages tie it to the letter or its presentation. The pin is Paris as a city, not a named building. Scope is the 1822 announcement only, not the Rosetta Stone itself.
2026-10-22 · polity-kingdom-of-aksum · outline · probable · End date is uncertain: WHE gives decline from the late 6th century and from the 7th century on the same page and an end in the late 8th; UNESCO places political decline in the 10th. The WHE page also reports the Aksumite sack of Meroë (Ezana, r. c. 303-350 CE), linked only by related. Specific dates of the 1st-century founding are not stated.
2026-10-22 · polity-kingdom-of-kush · outline · probable · Start date differs (c. 1069 BCE WHE vs 8th century BCE UNESCO). The Kerma culture is described by WHE as an earlier culture and is excluded. Pin is the Meroë pyramid point in UNESCO's data, not a surveyed city centre.
2026-10-22 · polity-chola-dynasty · outline · probable · Start date is approximate (mid-9th century per WHE; other pages seen only in search results give c. 848 or c. 850). The 985-1024 expansion claim is from the short Encyclopedia.com entry. Capital: Tanjavur for most of the period; WHE also says Rajendra I built a new capital further north for a brief period.
2026-10-22 · 1400-rise-of-the-swahili-coast · outline · probable · A regional process with no single location; Kilwa Kisiwani is a reference pin only. The id says 1400 but the sources give c. 1100-c. 1500, which this record follows. The Gedi link is by WHE listing Gedi among the city-states; Gedi dates were not stated there.
2026-10-22 · 1851-victorian-gold-rush · outline · established · Sources disagree on the Clunes find (29 June in the Courier; Hambleton says Esmond made it public on 5 July), so the record dates the rush by the Buninyong find of 8 August, on which both agree. The pin is Golden Point; the goldfields also spanned Clunes and Buninyong. The 1852 end year rests on Hambleton only.
2026-10-22 · 1960-year-of-africa · outline · established · Continent-wide process: the pin is Cameroon, first to become independent in 1960 (LOC), with 2,500 km uncertainty. The EBSCO country list was not reconciled against other pages. Whether Sharpeville is linked only as a same-year event is a judgment.
2026-10-22 · 1942-second-battle-of-el-alamein · outline · established · End date differs by source: historyofwar gives 4 November; an EBSCO page I opened gives 3 November (Rommel ordered retreat). The AWM page supports only the 23 October start; the end date and force numbers rest on historyofwar alone.
2026-10-22 · 1991-indian-economic-liberalisation · outline · established · No opened page names where the budget was delivered, so the pin is a country-level point with 1,500 km uncertainty. Reserve figures differ between pages (about three weeks of imports in the Quint; two weeks on 24 May in the 1991 Project) and are omitted.
2026-10-22 · -1800-epic-of-gilgamesh · outline · probable · This record is about the text, not King Gilgamesh (see person-gilgamesh). The provenance of the c. 1800 BCE tablet is unknown; the Uruk pin is the story setting with 300 km uncertainty, not a sourced composition site. The 11-then-12-tablet statement comes from the Biblical Archaeology Society page about a later copy.
2026-10-22 · 1919-eddington-eclipse-expedition · outline · established · The record pins Eddington's Príncipe site (Roça Sundy per the Frankfurt page; FAPESP does not name it). Sobral could be added as a second region (OSM: -3.8565, -40.0439). Frankfurt gives the Príncipe result as 1.7 arcseconds while FAPESP gives 1.61 ± 0.30; the summary uses FAPESP. Sources mention later disputes over discarded Sobral plates.
2026-10-22 · -0200-natyashastra · outline · contested · Weakest record of the batch: dates are disputed and the two opened sources disagree, only one is a reference source, and no place of composition is stated. Treat pin and dates as provisional.
2026-10-22 · polity-timurid-empire · outline · established · End year is 1506 in New World Encyclopedia only; other references use 1507 but no opened page gives it. Scope: the state, separate from the person-timur and monument records.
2026-10-22 · 1795-kamehameha-unifies-hawaii · outline · established · No opened page dates the Nuʻuanu fight to a day. The pin is the Pali Lookout (OSM); landings were at Waiʻalae and Waikīkī. Casualty figures (800 over the cliffs, per GoHawaii and Huapala) are omitted. Kauaʻi stayed independent until 1810 per EBSCO, so the full unification came later.
2026-10-22 · -0202-battle-of-zama · outline · established · The battlefield is not firmly located: EBSCO says about 60 miles (97 km) southwest of Carthage near the village of Zama; the Catholic Encyclopedia places Zama Major at Djama. The pin is Siliana city (OSM), a reference point in that region and not named on the opened pages, hence 120 km uncertainty. The 19 October day is from the older Catholic Encyclopedia only.
2026-10-22 · polity-timurid-empire · outline · established · End year 1507 is the Uzbek seizure of Herat; no opened page states the empire ended that year in so many words. Scope: the state, separate from the person-timur and monument records.
2026-10-22 · person-khadija-bint-khuwaylid · outline · probable · Birth year is only estimated (565 vs c. 555). Britannica could not be opened. Whether she was previously married is reported differently and is left out.
2026-10-22 · person-zhang-qian · outline · established · Least sure of life dates: sources disagree on birth (164 vs 195 BCE) and return (125 vs 126 BCE). The pin is Chang'an, the departure point, not a single place of activity.
2026-10-22 · 1908-tunguska-event · outline · established · Least sure of the location: neither opened prose page gives coordinates, so the point comes from Wikidata with 50 km uncertainty. Area flattened differs: WorldAtlas 770 square miles (later 830), NASA 830.
2026-10-22 · 1970-bhola-cyclone · outline · established · Landfall point is not given by the opened pages; Bhola Island is a central reference with 150 km uncertainty. Toll: 300,000-500,000 (WMO, NOAA); Guinness cites 300,000 from the WMO committee.
2026-10-22 · 1953-first-ascent-of-everest · outline · established · Coordinates are the summit from Wikidata only. No person records for Hillary or Tenzing exist yet, so no related links.
2026-10-22 · person-al-biruni · outline · established · MacTutor gives the birthplace as Kath, Khwarazm and death at Ghazna; EBSCO places the birth in an outlying neighborhood of Khiva. Pin is Ghazna, a city centroid.
2026-10-22 · person-ernest-rutherford · outline · established · Nuclear Museum says born in Nelson, New Zealand; Science History Institute says only a farm in New Zealand. Nobel Foundation page returned 403 and was not used. Pin is a city centroid; Cambridge (Cavendish) is the alternative.
2026-10-22 · person-al-razi · outline · probable · EBSCO says there is little information on his life. NLM page title inferred from URL. Confidence probable because dates are approximate. Pin is the Rayy centroid.
2026-10-22 · person-mahavira · outline · contested · Life details rest on Jain literature and are tradition, not independently documented; the sects differ on marriage and clothing. World History Encyclopedia says place of birth and death are disputed. Pin is a district centroid, not a site. I took the queue entry (year -599, religion) to mean the Jain teacher, not the ninth-century mathematician Mahavira.
2026-10-22 · person-brahmagupta · outline · established · MacTutor says born possibly at Ujjain; the Schools Observatory says Bhinmal. Pin chosen from where he wrote his major work. Link to Aryabhata is thematic only; no opened source states a connection.
2026-10-22 · person-ulugh-beg · outline · established · Sources disagree: MacTutor says observatory construction began in 1428 and the catalogue lists 992 stars (1437); the IAU-UNESCO page says the observatory was founded four years after the 1420 school and dates the Zij to 1438-39. Summary omits the observatory start date for that reason. His madrasa fronts the Registan; MacTutor says his tomb was found in 1941 in a mausoleum Timur built in Samarkand (Gur-e-Amir link rests on that description, not verified).
2026-10-22 · person-imhotep · outline · probable · His existence is supported by the inscription on the Djoser statue; the medical and literary attributions (e.g. Edwin Smith Papyrus c. 1600 BCE) and deification are later tradition. Museum publisher name taken from the domain; operating institution not confirmed. Pin is the Saqqara village centroid.
2026-10-22 · person-omar-khayyam · outline · established · Al Jazeera gives his age inconsistently (26 on entering service). Pin is a city centroid; the observatory site is not located by the sources. Alternative pin: Nishapur.
2026-10-22 · person-sushruta · outline · contested · Historicity and dates are uncertain; descent from Dhanvantari is tradition and the PMC article treats it as belief. World History Encyclopedia says the link to a Mahabharata Sushruta is not accepted by most scholars. PMC article title not recorded; described by ID. Pin is a city centroid.
2026-10-22 · person-aung-san-suu-kyi · outline · established · Living person; summary limited to events through 1995 and the 1991 prize as stated by the opened pages, with no judgment of her later record. Later detentions, offices and controversies are not covered here and need balanced, attributed sources at the full-record stage. Nobel page is an editor-compiled chronology. Pin is the Yangon city centroid (family home was on University Avenue by Inya Lake).
2026-10-22 · person-rani-durgavati · outline · probable · Place is the least certain element: the tourism page says only that Singrampur marks the site of her last stand against Mughal forces and names no battle; Encyclopedia.com calls the battle Narhi and does not give coordinates. Treat the 40 km radius as covering this gap. Encyclopedia.com says Asaf Khan acted on his own initiative but with Akbar's permission. Army figures are as that page reports them.
2026-10-22 · person-eva-peron · outline · established · Assessments of her are divided; the Argentine government page is commemorative while Encyclopedia.com notes charges of corruption. Argentine page dates the suffrage law to 9 September 1947 (Ley 13.010). Coordinate is the OSM Buenos Aires city point.
2026-10-22 · person-bhagat-singh · outline · established · The Dawn article dates the Saunders killing to December 1929, which conflicts with Encyclopedia.com (after November 1928); the summary follows Encyclopedia.com and gives no exact day. Views of his methods differ and are not assessed here. Pin is the Lahore city centroid.
2026-10-22 · person-rosa-parks · outline · established · History.com says local leaders had been planning a challenge to the bus laws for months and that Parks was privy to the discussion; this is not in the summary. Death day is not given by the opened pages. Link to the civil rights movement record is thematic (part_of).
2026-10-22 · person-gulbadan-begum · outline · established · Sources differ: Encyclopedia.com dates the Humayun-nama c. 1580 and gives Kabul as birthplace, while Iranica says it was probably begun in 1586-87 and that her early childhood was spent in Kabul. Other pages (not used) give other pilgrimage dates. Pin is a judgment call; a Hijaz or Kabul pin would also be defensible.
2026-10-22 · person-chico-mendes · outline · established · History.com places the shooting in the backyard of his home in Acre; Encyclopedia.com names Xapuri. Encyclopedia.com says he became president of the rural workers union in Xapuri in 1981. Trial details and sentences are not stated on the pages used. Pin is the Xapuri municipality centroid.
2026-10-22 · person-juana-azurduy · outline · probable · Sources conflict on birth: the Argentine page says 12 July 1780 in Toroca (north Potosi); Encyclopedia.com says Chuquisaca in 1781. They also differ on husband first name (Miguel vs Manuel). Argentine page links the colonel promotion to Bolivar after 1 April 1825. Pin is Sucre, not a battlefield, because no battle coordinates were sourced.
2026-10-22 · person-rani-durgavati · outline · probable · Place is the least certain element: the tourism page says only that Singrampur marks the site of her last stand against Mughal forces and names no battle; Encyclopedia.com calls the battle Narhi and does not give coordinates. Treat the 40 km radius as covering this gap. Encyclopedia.com says Asaf Khan acted on his own initiative but with Akbar's permission. Army figures are as that page reports them.
2026-10-22 · place-gol-gumbaz-bijapur · outline · established · Model omitted at outline tier because Archnet returned HTTP 429; construction date 1659 CE and dome description are confirmed directly from the Archaeological Survey of India (ASI).
2026-10-22 · place-giant-wild-goose-pagoda · outline · established · Outline only, no model: the only height and base figures opened were on commercial travel pages (China Highlights: 64.517 m tall, 25.5 m base length), which do not meet the model brief; the Shaanxi government page was unreachable. Least sure of the history: sources differ on whether the present tower is the 704 rebuild or Ming-era.
2026-10-22 · place-empire-state-building · outline · established · No model: the landmark report, the Skyscraper Museum and the building's own site give height (1,250 ft; 1,454 ft with antenna) but no plan dimension on a page I could read (the NPS nomination PDF is a scanned image). Shape would be tower. The LPC PDF is OCR text; it gives completion in April 1931, the building site gives opening 1 May 1931.
2026-10-22 · place-petronas-towers · outline · probable · No model written: pages opened (Skyscraper Museum, Thornton Tomasetti, the official site) give 452 m and 88 floors but no plan dimension (only floor area, a 58.4 m skybridge, and an eight-pointed-star plan); CTBUH, Wiley and ScienceDirect pages were blocked. Completion is 1998 on two pages but 1997 on Pelli Clarke Pelli's page; Pelli Clarke Pelli and the official site were opened but only the latter is cited.
2026-10-22 · place-ai-khanoum · outline · probable · Least sure of the founding date: the two sources disagree (4th century BC vs around 300 BC), so the range is wide. The Past does not state excavation years in its text; the 1964-1978 span is from World History Encyclopedia. Identification with Alexandria on the Oxus was not stated on an opened page and is not claimed. Pin is the Wikidata point for the site.
2026-10-22 · place-lothal · outline · probable · Weakest point: the two sources with dates are specialist/educational web pages (class other); WHE only places Lothal in Gujarat among Indus urban centres and coastal settlements. Their dates disagree (2400 vs 2200 BCE); the excavation span 1955-60 is given by both (a memoir span of 1955-62 is also quoted by StudioMatrx). Nothing here asserts the basin was a dock beyond attributing the reading to Rao.
2026-10-22 · person-phoolan-devi · outline · probable · Both sources are popular web articles, not scholarly or archival. The surrender date differs (12 Feb in TheCollector; month missing in MediaIndia), so only the year 1983 is used. The pin is the Wikidata point for Jalaun district because the village Gorha Ka Purwa could not be located on an opened page; uncertainty set to 60 km. The 1981 Behmai episode is described as disputed by TheCollector and is left out.
2026-10-22 · 1235-epic-of-sundiata · outline · legendary · Tradition, not a dated event: the date is the source's own "c. 13th century" and the id's 1235 is only a label. The pin is Nyagassola, where UNESCO says the epic is kept alive; the epic's own setting (Mande, Nyani) was not located on an opened page. Scope kept separate from the Kirina and Mali Empire records.
2026-10-22 · 1982-unclos · outline · established · Dates agree across sources. The page text on adoption is "after nine years of negotiations adopted the Convention" without an adoption date, so the record uses the signature date. Pin is the town of Montego Bay, not a specific venue.
2026-10-22 · polity-sasanian-empire · outline · established · Start is 224 (CAIS, WHE) but 226 in Livius; the 2-year positive uncertainty covers that. The pin is the capital Ctesiphon (CAIS), not the empire's extent. Overlaps with the Ctesiphon/Taq Kisra place record; scope here is the dynasty.
2026-10-22 · 0325-council-of-nicaea · outline · probable · Least sure of day-level dates: the sources conflict, so only 325 is recorded. Attendance is also disputed (about 300 bishops vs 318 vs 250 vs about 220) and is left out of the summary. The sources call the city Nicaea/Nicea/Nice; its modern name was not on an opened page, so modern_name says only modern-day Turkey. Pin is the Wikidata point for ancient Nicaea.
2026-10-22 · 0850-one-thousand-and-one-nights · outline · contested · Tradition, not an event: 9th-century Baghdad rests on the Library of Congress wording about the manuscript tradition; no surviving 9th-century manuscript was cited by an opened page. The pin is Baghdad as named by LOC, with the full range of origins (Persia, India, Syria, Egypt) recorded in the summary. Dated by tradition; the id's 0850 is a label.
2026-10-22 · 1913-tagore-nobel-prize · outline · established · Least sure of the pin: it is Santiniketan, the town the LOC page names in Tagore's life, not the place where the prize was decided or presented; neither opened page names a venue or an exact date. Nobel Foundation pages returned 403 and were not read, so a stronger source should confirm the year and citation.
2026-10-22 · place-sulawesi-cave-art · outline · probable · Dates are minimum ages. The pin is the centre of Maros Regency, not a cave; the article gives no coordinates and the karst spans Maros and Pangkep. Leang Bulu' Sipong 4 is given as about 48,000 years in the main text and 50.2 ka in the abstract, so the summary uses the Griffith figure. Leang Tedongnge (45.5 ka) is left out because it was seen only in search snippets.
2026-10-22 · 0550-origins-of-chess · outline · contested · Tradition and scholarly estimate, not a dated event. The pin is the Wikidata point for India with 1500 km uncertainty because sources name only "northern India" or "India"; it marks no site. Dates conflict (5th vs 6th century) and the span covers both. The spread to Persia is only stated as chatrang within the Sassanian period, with no date used.
2026-10-22 · polity-ethiopian-solomonic-dynasty · outline · probable · Least sure of the pin and end: the dynasty had no single capital named on an opened page, so the pin is the centre of the Amhara Region (the source calls Amhara the geographic and political centre) with 200 km uncertainty; WHE does not say who ended the dynasty in 1974, so the end year is its "takeover of 1974". The Met essay was not readable (HTTP 429). The Solomonic descent is treated as a claim.
2026-10-22 · place-baikonur-cosmodrome · outline · established · Coordinates differ by source: ESA 45.6 N 63.4 E, NASA 46 N 63.6 E, Wikidata 45.9648 N 63.3051 E, so 30 km uncertainty. Pin is the Wikidata point. The ESA page gave no dates for the Sputnik and Gagarin launches; those are kept in their own records.
2026-10-22 · -2000my-oklo-natural-reactors · outline · established · Dated with the sources' own wording ("two billion years ago"), so precision is coarse. SciAm gives both "up to 1 million years" and "several hundred thousand years" for operation; the summary uses the latter. The reactor count (17) is SciAm's; other sources may differ. The OSTI abstract names the Francevillian basin; SciAm says Franceville Basin. Pin is the Wikidata point for Oklo.
2026-10-22 · person-hildegard-of-bingen · outline · established · Pin is the former Rupertsberg convent site per OpenStreetMap; the opened pages name Rupertsberg but do not state a place of death. Birth place Bermersheim from the Society page only.
2026-10-22 · person-mary-wollstonecraft · outline · established · Link to the French Revolution is thematic (she travelled to France in December 1792 per the Stanford Encyclopedia). Pin is a city centroid. Exact death day is not on the opened pages.
2026-10-22 · person-florence-nightingale · outline · established · Victorian Web gives arrival at Scutari as 4 November 1854; the National Army Museum page opened gives only the 21 October 1854 departure and "November 1854". Identification of the Selimiye Barracks coordinate with the barrack hospital is not stated on the opened pages, so the pin is kept at 3 km (Scutari district).
2026-10-22 · person-joan-of-arc · outline · established · Sources differ on her arrival (28/29 April 1429, per EBSCO). Pinned to Orléans; Rouen (trial and execution) is the alternative. World History Encyclopedia gives birth 6 January 1412; EBSCO says only c. 1412.
2026-10-22 · person-nefertiti · outline · probable · Google Arts & Culture page gives discovery as 6 December 1912 but quotes an excavation record dated 6 December 1913; Archaeology Travel gives 1912. Nefertiti's own dates are not given; the span is a proxy. Pin is the Tell el-Amarna village point from OSM, site approximate.
2026-10-22 · person-dihya-al-kahina · outline · contested · Traditional figure known through medieval chronicles; Medievalists.net says the sources are "steeped in legend" and dates, name and ancestry are uncertain. Pin is a region point with 100 km uncertainty. Link to the caliphate is thematic only.

Encyclopaedia Africana says campaigns reached Kano, Katsina and Nupe; Encyclopedia.com says Nupe and southern and western expansion. Death place Atagara is only in Encyclopedia.com.
2026-10-22 · person-nehanda-nyakasikana · outline · probable · Pindula gives capture December 1897 and trial March 1898 but also contradicts itself on whether a trial occurred; Encyclopaedia Africana gives no capture date. 2026-10-22 · person-mekatilili-wa-menza · outline · probable · Sources differ: Sheffield says she was recaptured two days after returning home (January 1914); Kenyanhistory.com says rearrested in August 1914 and held at Kismayu. 2026-10-22 · person-funmilayo-ransome-kuti · outline · established · Dates differ: New Lines says the club was renamed AWU in 1946; Encyclopaedia Africana says club founded 1944 and gives the Alake's abdication as 1948. 2026-10-22 · person-sammuramat · outline · probable · Traditional Semiramis legend not covered here. 2026-10-22 · person-aisha-bint-abi-bakr · outline · probable · Religious figure treated neutrally; traditions differ on events and ages. 2026-10-22 · person-lady-trieu · outline · legendary · Traditional figure: both sources rely on tradition for her life; birth is c. 225 (Encyclopedia.com) or 226 (legend, per the newspaper). 2026-10-22 · person-queen-suriyothai · outline · contested · Legendary or contested figure. No museum, university or government page could be opened; ayutthaya-history.com is classed reference by the agent (a long-running research site on Ayutthaya) and should be confirmed. 2026-10-22 · person-ahilyabai-holkar · outline · established · Both sources are Government of India or state tourism pages, not scholarly work. Not cross-checked against a historian's account. The pin is the Maheshwar town centre.
2026-10-22 · person-begum-rokeya · outline · established · The school date differs (1909 vs 1911) and Encyclopedia.com says the marriage date is unknown; the Daily Star article does not state the Calcutta move in the passage checked. 2026-10-22 · person-qiu-jin · outline · established · Birth year is disputed in the sources (1875 vs 1877-1879). The OSM point is the centroid of Shaoxing city as returned by Nominatim, so uncertainty is set at 15 km.

2026-10-22 · person-mary-reibey · outline · established · The Dictionary of Sydney page opened is a summary entry with milestones and property list, so business details rest mainly on Encyclopedia.com. 2026-10-22 · place-songyue-pagoda · outline · established · Shape pagoda, 15 tiers (China Daily "15 stories"; china.org.cn "fifteen pent roofs"). Date conflict 520 vs 523 kept as a span. 2026-10-22 · place-toji-pagoda · outline · established · Shape pagoda, 5 tiers. Height 55 m from the temple and MLIT (a travel site and others give 54.8 m, not used). Record covers the present pagoda; World History Encyclopedia says the first pagoda was built in 826, Tō-ji says 9th century.
2026-10-22 · place-tokyo-skytree · outline · established · Shape tower; the base is triangular, noted in the footprint. Official page gives no construction dates, so phases rest on Nippon.com (project start 2006, so the construction phase starts at the project start, not ground-breaking).
2026-10-22 · place-azadi-tower · outline · established · Shape block: closest available; the real form is an inverted-Y arch on a podium, so the block is a bounding stand-in. Height 45 m and width 64 m both from VisitIran only; the 64 m width may be the monument's overall width rather than a base side. Dates conflict between sources (1967/1972 vs 1966/1971).
2026-10-22 · place-tomb-of-cyrus-pasargadae · outline · probable · Shape stepped_pyramid: six-tier plinth plus gabled chamber, height covers both. Construction date c. 530 BCE rests on one lesser source (Studio Matrx) and is marked probable; no opened page gives a firmer date. Records the tomb only, not Cyrus or the wider Pasargadae site.
2026-10-22 · place-cologne-cathedral · outline · established · Shape block: footprint and nave interior height describe the whole church body; tower height (157.22 m) is deliberately not used because the plan is not the tower. Nave height is an interior value (cathedral site 43.35 m, UNESCO 43.58 m). Cathedral-site pages are German; as_recorded strings are verbatim German headings.
2026-10-22 · place-st-peters-basilica · outline · established · Shape domed_building. The model describes the dome only (all three measurements are the dome on the Holy See page), so the globe shows nothing for the basilica before 1546. Other pages give 136 m or 136.57 m to the cross and 42.56 m internal diameter; the official page was used. Only UNESCO and the Holy See were usable publishers; the Holy See is also the owner.
2026-10-22 · place-leaning-tower-of-pisa · outline · established · Shape tower, round (sourced diameter). The model is drawn upright; the lean is not modelled. Completion year is unsourced on any page I could open (Italia.it says 1300; common accounts say 1372 but the ICE page was blocked), so the single phase is open-ended. Heights differ by source (58.36 m OPA/Italia.it; 56 m Visit Tuscany); OPA used.
2026-10-22 · place-ivan-the-great-bell-tower · outline · established · Outline only, no model: no plan dimension on a page I could open (kreml.ru returned 504 on every try). Height differs by source: UNESCO 82 m, the NVCC notes over 260 feet. The highest-building claim is UNESCO wording.
2026-10-22 · place-ulm-minster · outline · established · Outline only, no model: the only height on an opened page is the 161.53 m tower, and no plan dimension of that tower or building was found (Structurae was blocked). The two City of Ulm sources share one publisher; ReidsGuides is a travel page used only for the dates.
2026-10-22 · place-washington-monument · outline · established · Shape obelisk. Metre values are converted from the feet and inches printed on the pages (555 ft 5 1/8 in = 169.29 m; 55 ft 1½ in = 16.80 m); the 1884-85 height is a historical figure. The 152 ft pause figure is on both NPS and National Archives pages.
2026-10-22 · place-cn-tower · outline · established · Outline only, no model: no plan dimension on a page I could read. A Canadian Journal of Civil Engineering paper said to give a 54 m base diameter was blocked (ResearchGate 403), so it could not be opened or spot-checked. Height 553.33 m is on the Canadian Encyclopedia and EIC pages.
2026-10-22 · place-gateway-arch · outline · established · Shape block (no arch shape in the enum), span used as the plan as instructed. 630 ft = 192.02 m, converted from the feet on the pages. The 28 October 1965 completion day is not used.
2026-10-22 · place-edzna-five-story-building · outline · established · Shape stepped_pyramid. The second source is an INAH press release reprinted by a news outlet, so it is not fully independent of INAH. Coordinate is the Wikidata point for the Edzná site, not the building itself (about 0.5 km uncertainty). The phases leave a gap between 1450 and 1959 because no page opened describes its condition then. The name refers to five visible levels but the release counts nine.
2026-10-22 · place-hassan-tower-rabat · outline · established · Shape tower, square (no diameter sourced). Start date conflicts: 1184 (UNESCO) versus about 1195 (History Hit). Phase 1199-1755 state altered is my label for the unfinished tower.
2026-10-22 · place-great-mosque-of-agadez · outline · established · Outline only, no model: height 27 m is on three pages but none gives a plan dimension for the minaret (the ICOMOS text says only that it is a truncated pyramid and that the mosque group covers 5600 sq m). 2026-10-22 · -47my-azolla-event · outline · probable · Least sure of the pin: the opened pages say only "Arctic Ocean" and "central Arctic Ocean" and give no drill-site coordinates, so the Wikidata ridge point is used with 2500 km uncertainty. The CO2 role is a hypothesis in the source.
2026-10-22 · -16my-columbia-river-flood-basalts · outline · established · Least sure of the pin: USGS puts the vents along the Washington/Oregon/Idaho border, so Steens Mountain (named in the USGS-hosted Jarboe abstract) is a representative point, not the vent centre, hence 400 km. 2026-10-22 · -2100my-francevillian-biota · outline · probable · Least sure of the pin: the opened pages give no coordinates, so the Wikidata point for Franceville is used with 50 km uncertainty. 2026-10-22 · -40my-middle-eocene-climatic-optimum · outline · probable · Least sure of the pin: global event with no sourced type site on the opened pages, so a generic Pacific point is used with 10000 km uncertainty. 2026-10-22 · -120my-oceanic-anoxic-event-1a · outline · established · Least sure of the pin: the Cismon Core is named without coordinates, so the Wikidata point for Cismon del Grappa is used; 5000 km because the event is global. 2026-10-22 · -234my-carnian-pluvial-event · outline · probable · Least sure of the pin: the episode is global, so the Dolomites point (Roghi et al.'s best age-constrained section) is a reference locality, hence the 5000 km uncertainty. 2026-10-22 · 2011-fukushima-daiichi-disaster · outline · established · The sources differ on the number of units with core damage (WNA says units 1-3; GreenFacts quoting IAEA says two units), so the summary says several units. Evacuee figures also differ (over 100,000 vs some 160,000); I give the lower figure. WNA is an industry body.
2026-10-22 · -0100-hopewell-ceremonial-earthworks · outline · established · Multi-site property: the pin is the Chillicothe park point, with 90 km uncertainty to cover Newark and Fort Ancient. No model: neither source gives a mound height and plan dimension.
2026-10-22 · -3400-watson-brake-mounds · outline · established · No model: the only source for the heights and the 853-foot oval is a 1998 magazine brief, not a heritage body, museum or paper. The GeoNames feature is a swamp polygon centre; the two sources differ on the date convention (5400 B.P. versus around 3500 BCE), so the range spans both.
2026-10-22 · -9000-folsom-site · outline · established · The NPS record restricts the address, so the pin is the GeoNames historical marker at Folsom, a proxy with 20 km uncertainty; the magazine places the site about 30 miles east of Raton. The magazine says 16 spearpoints and 32 bison. Date ranges differ (about 10,000 years vs 7000-8999 BC).
2026-10-22 · 1070-serpent-mound · outline · contested · Date and builder are contested: ca. AD 1070 (Fort Ancient) versus 381-44 BC (Adena era); the range uses 1070 with an uncertainty back to 320 BCE. No model: the height is from History.com only and no width is sourced to a heritage body or paper. WHE gives 1,346 feet; History.com gives 1,348 feet; I use 411 m.
2026-10-22 · 0625-sutton-hoo-ship-burial · outline · probable · The id says 625 but my two pages give only the 7th century and 1,400 years ago; the c. 625 date from the British Museum page was not opened. Raedwald identification is a likely attribution, not proven. No model: mound height and diameter are not sourced.
2026-10-22 · 0793-lindisfarne-viking-raid · outline · established · The day is uncertain between sources (7 June versus 8 June suggested); I record the year. The ships and warrior numbers are from a single secondary source (EBSCO).
2026-10-22 · -1600-nebra-sky-disc · outline · probable · Neither page gives a BC date, only 3,600 years; the -1600 range is derived from that. Pin is Nebra town; the Mittelberg site is nearby, so uncertainty is 6 km. A 2020 Iron Age dating challenge is not covered by my sources.
2026-10-22 · -0728-kushite-conquest-of-egypt · outline · probable · Least sure of the date: the opened sources give Piye's reign as 742-716 and 743-712 BCE and the stela as c. 725 BCE, and none dates the conquest itself, so the range runs from accession to the stela. Pin is Memphis (the stela has the king sailing north to Memphis); Napata was the Kushite seat. Tefnakht details come from the stela translation only.
2026-10-22 · 1250-mamluk-seizure-of-power-in-egypt · outline · established · Least sure of the pin: the sources place Turan Shah's claim to the sultanate in Cairo but I did not open one naming where he was killed. 2026-10-22 · 1062-founding-of-marrakesh-and-almoravid-empire · outline · probable · Least sure of the date and the founder: 1062 vs 1070-72 is unresolved among opened sources; the Encyclopedia.com Yusuf ibn Tashfin entry credits the foundation to him without a year. Pin is the modern city.
2026-10-22 · 1899-second-boer-war · outline · established · Least sure of the pin: a city centroid stands for a war across several territories. Camp death figures differ between sources and are left out of the summary.
2026-10-22 · 1967-biafran-war · outline · established · Least sure of death counts: Encyclopedia.com says about three million Biafrans lost their lives (about one million from malnutrition) while History.com says about one million died of malnutrition; neither figure is in the summary. 2026-10-22 · 1952-mau-mau-uprising · outline · established · Least sure of the end date: the emergency continued past 1956 but I did not open a page stating when it ended. Pin is a regional centroid at Mount Kenya, where Encyclopedia.com places the guerrillas with the Aberdares.
2026-10-22 · 1986-lake-nyos-gas-disaster · outline · established · Least sure of the death toll: EBSCO says about 1,700 and History.com nearly 2,000; the summary gives both. Cause is only described as a carbon dioxide release; I did not state the trigger. Pin is the lake.
2026-10-22 · person-ramesses-ii · outline · probable · Least sure of the reign dates: the two opened sources differ by 25 years. Birth and death years were not given on an opened page. 2026-10-22 · person-akhenaten · outline · probable · Least sure of the dates: opened sources disagree by about 25 years and Encyclopedia.com's two entries differ from each other, so the range is wide. I did not state the monotheism question. Pin is the Amarna site he founded, not a birthplace.
2026-10-22 · person-taharqa · outline · probable · Least sure of the end of his reign: BlackPast's 656 for the Assyrian expulsion conflicts with its own 664 death date, so I used 664 and did not state 656. Pin is Napata, where both sources place his home base; birth place and year are unknown.
2026-10-22 · person-augustine-of-hippo · outline · established · Least sure of the pin: Hippo is where he was bishop and died; his birthplace Thagaste (Souk-Ahras) is named in the sources but not pinned. Dates are as the Catholic Encyclopedia gives them.
2026-10-22 · person-kwame-nkrumah · outline · established · Least sure of the pin and the birth date: the pin is Accra, the capital he governed from, not Nkroful where he was born; the sources' exact birth day is unofficial. 2026-10-22 · person-sol-plaatje · outline · established · Least sure of the pin: Kimberley is where SAHO says he settled and edited a newspaper; he was born in the Boshof district and also lived in Mafeking, so the pin is a city centroid for his working life, not his birthplace.
2026-10-22 · -0600-nilometers-of-egypt · outline · probable · Least sure of the date: the id suggests c. 600 BCE, but the opened sources give a Middle Kingdom attribution (EBSCO, unverified) and a third-century BCE example; I dated from their wording. 2026-10-22 · 1932-japanese-military-comfort-women-system · outline · established · Least sure of the end year (not on opened pages) and of the estimate range, which is the Asian Women's Fund's own list of researchers (Hata 1999: 20,000; Su Zhiliang 1999: 410,000; Yoshimi 1995: 45,000-200,000). 2026-10-22 · 1968-tet-offensive · outline · established · Pin is Saigon (US Embassy); the attacks covered many cities, so uncertainty is set to 400 km. Sources differ slightly on the start (30 vs 31 January); both are recorded.
2026-10-22 · 1845-new-zealand-wars · outline · established · Least sure of the end date: the opened pages do not date the peace, so the record ends 1845 although the war is usually dated into early 1846 in sources not opened here. 2026-10-22 · 1866-russian-capture-of-tashkent · outline · probable · The id says 1866 but the opened sources date the capture to 1865; the 1866 annexation is not on an opened page. 2026-10-22 · 1975-indonesian-invasion-of-east-timor · outline · established · The CAVR figure is a minimum (102,800, about 18,600 killed or disappeared and about 84,200 from hunger and illness), per HRDAG, which worked with the commission; HRDAG also describes an upper-bound 183,000 for hunger and illness deaths. 2026-10-22 · 1147-almohad-capture-of-marrakesh · outline · established · Only the year is stated by both sources. The siege length and the execution of the last Almoravid ruler are from EBSCO, which does not name him; web accounts differ on how he died, so no name or manner is added. The 1269 end of Almohad rule in Marrakesh is from the Gale entry. Replaced a non-reference source after finding it cited Wikipedia.
2026-10-22 · 1054-east-west-schism · outline · contested · Whether 1054 was "the" schism is contested: EBSCO says it is unclear whether a state of schism existed, and Christian History Institute says scholars reject 1054 as the start. The day-level date rests on one source; EBSCO gives only 1054. The Wikidata pin is the present building.
2026-10-22 · 0532-nika-riots · outline · probable · Death figures are ancient claims attributed to Procopius (more than thirty thousand) and to Theophanes and the Chronicon Paschale (thirty-five thousand); Encyclopaedia Romana also notes Theodora's speech is likely a rhetorical set-piece. Byzantine Culture attributes the 30,000 figure to Procopius but gives it as "some 30,000 rioters". No person record for Justinian exists in the atlas, so none is linked.
2026-10-22 · 0301-armenia-adopts-christianity · outline · contested · 301 is the traditional date and is marked as tradition. The place is a proxy pin: EBSCO places the church headquarters at Vagharshapat, then-capital; no opened page says where the conversion itself occurred. The 314 date and the 302 baptism date conflict with 301.
2026-10-22 · 1953-iranian-coup-d-etat · outline · established · No opened page gives a death toll for the unrest, so none is stated (a figure of about 300 appears in search snippets only). 2026-10-22 · 1973-wollo-famine · outline · probable · The death toll is contested: 40,000-80,000 (Seaman and Holt, via HRW) against 200,000 (Ethiopian Nutrition Institute, said by HRW to rest on over-pessimistic assumptions); Miller and Holt note the lack of definitive data. The pin is Dessie as a proxy for the province, and HRW names it as provincial capital in the text. Miller and Holt supply the 1973 year, the failed rains and the Wollo setting.
2026-10-22 · 1948-02-04-ceylon-independence · outline · established · Least sure of the pin: no opened page names where the independence ceremony was held, so it is a proxy pin at Colombo. The two government sources are US publications; the Country Studies page is hosted at countrystudies.us. The Senanayake and United States points come from those pages.
2026-10-22 · 1960-10-01-nigerian-independence · outline · established · The venue is the Race Course (now Tafawa Balewa Square) per Horniman and Historical Nigeria; the OSM pin is the present square. 2026-10-22 · 1963-12-12-kenyan-independence · outline · established · Another web account places the flag-raising at Uhuru Park; the summary follows the Citizen Guide (Uhuru Gardens). 2026-10-22 · 0672-greek-fire · outline · contested · The 672 date comes from Royal Museums Greenwich, stated without qualification; Medievalists.net questions the Theophanes chronology and notes the inconsistency of Constantine IV equipping the fleet with Greek fire a year before Kallinikos invents it. The pin is a proxy for Constantinople; the RMG page gives no year for the siege.
2026-10-22 · 1000-olonkho-epic · outline · established · Origin date of the tradition is not given in opened sources, so 'when' covers the 2005 proclamation and 2008 inscription. 2026-10-22 · -2000ka-okavango-delta-formation · outline · contested · No opened source dates the delta's formation to 2 Ma; IRD gives 2 Ma only as the upper bound of a 12 ka-2 Ma range, and Vainer et al. 2024 put fan deposition by ~250 ka, so the pin year is -250000 with wide uncertainty. Coordinates are UNESCO's property centre.

Wall shape chosen as closest; no_mesh set.

2026-10-22 · place-al-qarawiyyin-fez · outline · established · No model: no height was found on a page I could open; AKDN gives a size of 7,200 m2 only. 2026-10-22 · place-kerma-deffufa · outline · probable · Least sure: the measurements are from EBSCO Research Starters (a reference product, the same class used for the Aswan record) and the height matches Archaeology magazine within a metre; no heritage-body or excavation-report page was opened (UNESCO returned 403). Other pages give 45 x 27 m or 50 x 25 m. Block shape chosen as closest; the start year -1999 is only 4,000 years before the 2020 article, and no page gives a precise date for the chapel or the enlargement.
2026-10-22 · place-casarabe-llanos-de-mojos · outline · probable · Pin is the OSM village of Casarabe, Beni, as a rough centre of a culture area of about 4,500 km2; the paper gives no coordinates for Cotoca or Landívar (I did not find them), so uncertainty is set at 60 km. No model: it is a region of sites, not one structure. The paper says one site controls about 500 km2, which does not match the hectare figures, and gives Cotoca's core earth volume inconsistently.
2026-10-22 · place-djamaa-el-djazair · outline · established · Least sure: no owner/operator (ANARGEMA) or government page was opened, so the model is no_mesh. Height is 264.3 m (Guinness) vs 265 m (KSP Engel, Egis); plan 28 x 28 m is from KSP Engel only. The pin is the OSM mosque outline centre; the minaret is at the western end of a complex about 600 m long, so uncertainty is 0.5 km. The osm-footprint tool found no mapped minaret outline. Tower shape chosen as closest.
2026-10-22 · -0600-meroe-iron-smelting · outline · probable · Dates differ by source: UCL gives 7th–6th century BCE (2017 radiocarbon), Penn (1993) a single 6th-century date; I set start at -600 (601 BCE) as the 6th/7th century boundary. The end year is a floor derived from "over one thousand years". Placed under northern_africa (UN M49 treats Sudan so) though Penn says sub-Saharan. UCL says no furnace earlier than the first century AD has yet been found at Meroe, which I did not put in the summary.
2026-10-22 · -0500-waru-waru-raised-fields · outline · probable · The record id says -0500 but both sources date the fields to about 1000 BCE onward, so start is -999 and I kept the id. Penn gives fields 4–10 m wide and 10–100 m long; ArchDaily gives 5–10 m and up to 50 m or more, so I cite only Penn's totals. Pin is Huata district centre (the Penn study area is Huatta); the field area covers much of the basin, hence 50 km. Another study dates some fields to about 600 CE, which I did not open.
